# TASK-055 — Full HTML Living Standard Conformance

**Details name-group exclusivity restored at the DOM layer 2026-10-30:** The checkpoint audit found that the in-flight work deleted `checkExclusiveDetails` from the `internal/dom` attribute-mutation path, moved the rule into `internal/js`, and deleted `test/dom/exclusive_details_test.go` instead of repairing it; restoring that file reproduced the loss (both its tests failed) and showed the script-free activation fallback at `internal/engine/engine.go` opened a `<details>` without closing its named peers. The tree rule now lives in `internal/dom/details.go` as `DetailsTreeScope` (moved verbatim, shadow trees remain separate scopes) and `CloseOtherNamedDetails`, which closes same-name open peers and returns them in tree order so each caller owns its event semantics: `internal/js` keeps its coalesced trusted `ToggleEvent` queue, and `toggleDetailsWithoutRuntime` in `internal/engine` dispatches the trusted toggle for the element and for every peer it closed. Coverage is restored in `test/dom/details_group_test.go` (name-group peers, shadow-tree separation, unnamed groups exempt, detached subtree scoping). A mutation dropping the name comparison makes the oracle fail by closing all three open members and was restored. Evidence: pinned `html/semantics/interactive-elements/the-details-element` re-run at 19 pass, 0 fail, 1 support skip (100%) in `/tmp/goosie-t55-details-after-helper/results.json`, unchanged from the pre-refactor baseline; `go test ./test/dom ./internal/dom ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./internal/archtest -count=1` all pass; `go build ./...`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `gofmt -l` are clean. This is a layer-correctness fix for the script-free path, not new HTML conformance ground; TASK-055 remains open.

Repository checks at this checkpoint: `go build ./...` passes, the 68-package `go test ./...` run (every package except `test/qc` and `test/wpt`) passes with exit code 0, and `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1` passes. `test/qc` remains red by its own rule (`traceability.golden.json` is stale and 59 cases await QC sign-off; §9 forbids touching the golden without a proposal in §8) and `test/wpt` `TestWPTSuite` still exceeds its budget, so the full `testdata/wpt-task55-full-config.json` profile remains unrun and is still the blocking acceptance gate. Note for the next session: several §8 rows are stale and overstate gaps (`GOOSIE-DOCWRITE-001`, `GOOSIE-DOMPARSER-001`, `GOOSIE-RANGE-001`, `GOOSIE-CSSOMVIEW-001`, `GOOSIE-TREEWALKER-001`, `GOOSIE-LAYER-001` all name code that now exists in `internal/js/document_write.go`, `internal/js/domparser.go`, `internal/js/range.go`, `internal/js/cssom_view.go`, `internal/js/treewalker.go`, and `internal/css/parser.go`); they are left unrevised here because rewriting them would claim conformance without fresh measurement. `container queries` and `aspect-ratio` are the two §8 rows verified still absent from the source tree. **Document-clone event paths refreshed 2026-10-29:** `cloneDocumentWrapper` now repoints cloned Document children at the embedded Document node, preserving the document ancestor in event paths. Window dispatch steps are limited to the Runtime's own Document tree, so events from cloned documents do not reach an unrelated Window; root detection uses the tree itself, including documents whose nodes have no `Doc` pointer. The pinned `Event-dispatch-bubbles-false.html`, `Event-dispatch-bubbles-true.html`, and `event-listener-not-fully-active-navigated.html` pass 3/3: `/tmp/goosie-task55-events-window-and-inactive-v3/results.json`. The refreshed full `dom/events` profile is running against these changes. TASK-055 remains open.

**Navigated iframe event targets and nested `top` refreshed 2026-10-29:** Runtime tracking now retains the browsing-context association for former iframe Documents, so an old document does not become active merely because it is no longer the current document. Nested iframe `top` now follows the ancestor Window chain; this also makes top-level state written by nested frames visible to the top-level page. The pinned `event-listener-not-fully-active-navigated.html` passes all 3 tests: `/tmp/goosie-task55-inactive-navigated-v2/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest -count=1`, `go vet -all ./internal/js ./test/js`, and scoped `git diff --check` pass. The full pinned events profile completed at 74 passed, 76 failed, 6 skipped, and 27 errored, but its binary predates these iframe changes and still reports the navigated-frame fixture failing. The focused rerun above passes; TASK-055 remains open.

**MutationObserver parser, iframe, and HTML replacement refresh 2026-10-29:** `innerHTML` and `outerHTML` enqueue child-list records with combined added/removed node lists and the correct target; the pinned `MutationObserver-inner-outer.html` passes. `Element.append()` now resolves iframe wrappers through the owning runtime and queues one combined child-list record; a Go regression verifies two appended text nodes appear in that single record. Same-origin iframe access now initializes `MutationObserver`/`MutationRecord`, and sibling-frame callback bridging preserves the callback realm; `MutationObserver-cross-realm-callback-report-exception.html` passes. The in-document WPT runner disables `testharnessreport.js` output so progress updates do not mutate the document being observed. With this correction, the fresh pinned 14-file `MutationObserver-*.html` sample passes 14/14 with no skips/errors: `/tmp/goosie-task55-mutation-observer-full-sample-v5/results.json`; `MutationObserver-document.html` passes all four subtests, including parser insertion and removal. The focused runner regression, `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./internal/archtest -count=1`, `go vet -all ./internal/js ./test/wpt`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes the sampled MutationObserver files, not TASK-055 as a whole; the full HTML Living Standard scope remains open.

**MutationObserver attribute and character-data records checked 2026-10-29:** `observe()` now defaults all mutation types to false, applies the implicit `attributes`/`characterData` defaults for old-value/filter options, validates incompatible options, and filters namespaced attributes and `attributeFilter` local names. Attribute mutations from core Element methods, reflected attributes, DOMTokenList, NamedNodeMap/Attr, and character-data methods queue records; per-observer `oldValue` preserves empty strings and is null unless requested. Focused Go tests cover empty old values, filtering, and option validation. The pinned `dom/nodes/MutationObserver-attributes.html` and `MutationObserver-characterData.html` both pass (2/2 files, no skips/errors). This closes these two WPT files only; other MutationObserver behaviors and TASK-055 remain open. Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-mutation-observer-final/results.json`.

**MutationObserver core behavior extended 2026-10-29:** Callback invocation supplies the observer as `this` and the second argument, MutationRecord objects carry the correct prototype, explicit false `attributeOldValue`/`characterDataOldValue` options trigger implicit observation, and disconnect/reobserve works. Child-list records cover insertion/removal, textContent, Range deletion/extraction/splitting/surrounding, and normalization. The pinned nine-file core sample now passes 9/9 with no skips/errors: attributes, callback arguments, characterData, childList (40/40), disconnect, overlapping registrations, sanity, `takeRecords()`, and `textContent`. The callback failure was traced to Goja's compiler failing to capture the outer function's implicit `arguments` binding in a nested arrow closure; `lookupName()` now moves that binding into the closure stash. Go regressions cover callback receiver/arguments, MutationRecord branding, and lexical argument capture. Current report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-mutation-observer-final-core/results.json`. Other MutationObserver WPT files and TASK-055 conformance remain open.

**IndexedDB key primitives checked 2026-10-29:** The runtime exposes a stable `indexedDB`/`self.indexedDB` IDBFactory object and implements `cmp()` for number, Date, UTF-16 string, ArrayBuffer/view byte, and recursively ordered array keys, with `DataError` for invalid keys and `TypeError` for missing arguments. Comparison input is bounded to 64 nested key levels and 65,536 array entries to limit work on untrusted values. `IDBKeyRange` now implements `only()`, `lowerBound()`, `upperBound()`, `bound()`, its bound/open properties, and `includes()`. Three Go regressions cover key ordering, invalid keys, singleton identity, and empty ranges from equal open bounds. Four pinned IndexedDB WPTs (`idbfactory_cmp`, `globalscope-indexedDB-SameObject`, `idbkeyrange`, and `idbkeyrange-includes`) pass 4/4 with no skips/errors; focused JS/architecture tests, `go vet -all ./internal/js`, browser build, and `git diff --check` pass. **Resizable and growable buffers in structured cloning checked 2026-10-29:** Added a tracked local Goja fork with native resizable ArrayBuffer construction/resize/maxByteLength, growable SharedArrayBuffer construction/grow, dynamic length-tracking TypedArray/DataView metadata, out-of-bounds handling, and a read-only host API for preserving view brands/modes during cloning. Goosie structured clone now preserves buffer resize mode and view constructor/type, rejects OOB views, preserves transferred resizable-buffer maximums, and rejects SharedArrayBuffer cloning while `crossOriginIsolated` is false. Added a bounded `WebAssembly.Memory` buffer/grow surface used by the pinned WPT's SAB constructor probe, plus Go regressions for clone identity/view tracking, transfer atomicity/OOB rejection, SAB isolation behavior, and Memory buffer construction. The focused pinned `messagechannel.any.js` WPT passes all 152 subtests except one optional ReadableStream-transfer skip; before this tranche it had 11 failing subtests. Report: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. `go test github.com/dop251/goja -count=1`, `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/dom ./test/dom ./internal/archtest -count=1`, and `git diff --check` pass. Remaining limitations include cross-agent shared-memory semantics for SAB when isolated, full WebAssembly APIs/compilation, and unrelated HTML Living Standard/WPT gaps; TASK-055 remains open.

**Input Events deletion ranges and block-boundary editing checked 2026-10-29:** Backward deletion stops its whitespace scan after one preserved `pre-line` newline, element-boundary Backspace targets an atomic child, and list-item joining includes consecutive trailing `<br>` elements. Around `<hr>`, target ranges include invisible whitespace on either side and an immediately preceding `<br>`; forward deletion also removes collapsed whitespace adjacent to a deleted `<hr>`. Paragraph joins target only the final trailing `<br>`. The engine joins inline content with a neighboring block and preserves the remaining line structure. Empty paragraph placeholders target the prior editable boundary through their placeholder nodes; non-editable inline and block boundaries are handled separately. Collapsed `deleteContentForward` derives its deletion range from the same host-aware target-range algorithm used by `beforeinput`, so block joins stop at the next block's start instead of deleting its first character. Delete after an image before a terminal placeholder `<br>` leaves the DOM unchanged without firing `input`. Forward editing selects through trailing collapsed whitespace before a following block and retains one trailing `<br>` when deleting between consecutive breaks. Forward target ranges cover collapsible whitespace across adjacent and nested blocks, skip leading spaces in normal-flow destination blocks while retaining them in preformatted blocks, avoid crossing table-cell boundaries, preserve inline text boundary containers, and correctly start before a trailing `<br>` when the following text crosses a block boundary. When an empty paragraph precedes a paragraph whose leading child is noneditable inline content, its forward-delete target now ends at the next paragraph start. Inline noneditable content otherwise stops the range at the preceding caret. `pre-line` beforeinput ranges include the expected boundary after a single collapsed space-newline sequence without changing the actual text deletion range. Forward word deletion preserves one collapsed separator before a following word while removing a full trailing space run at end of text. On macOS, Meta+Delete uses `deleteSoftLineForward` and selects through the end of the current soft line. The forward-delete action now searches for the next block from the actual caret node through ancestor sibling scopes, including blocks nested inside an editing host. Joining across a `<br>` also preserves later lines, recognizes an atomic inline predecessor, joins through invisible leading spaces included in the target range, and moves following inline text into the preceding block without deleting its first character. Forward Delete flattens the first item from a following single-item list and joins following inline text into the last item of a list. Testdriver regressions cover direct and nested block joins, a preceding line break, following inline text, and both list-boundary joins; the pinned fixture confirms these paths end to end. Joining a paragraph to a preformatted block alternates non-breaking spaces into the preserved leading space run so its visible width survives the join. The WPT action-sequence shim routes ArrowLeft/ArrowRight through the engine's editable-caret behavior; this removes the Unicode arrow-then-delete case from the forward-delete failures. Go regressions cover `pre-line`, Unicode caret movement, forward word-delete whitespace, inline-to-block editing, text and element carets before cross-block breaks, inline noneditable boundaries, empty placeholders before noneditable content, cross-block whitespace, trailing list breaks, atomic-image deletion, preformatted-space preservation, whitespace cleanup around `<hr>`, and multi-range table-cell target ranges and selected-cell deletion (complete, partial, and mixed ranges). The WPT action-sequence regressions exercise `.focus()` followed by forward-delete at direct and nested inline/block boundaries, including a `<br>` before the next paragraph, text following a paragraph, and list item boundaries. Latest configured 27-file Input Events profile: 20 passed, 7 failed, 0 skipped, and 0 errored; Backspace matrix failures are 4/173, and forward-delete passes 180/182 assertions (up from 179/182). The mixed table-cell target-range failure remains cleared. The 27-file profile now runs all 4 configured query-variant fixtures instead of reporting “Unhandled variant”. Remaining forward-delete failures are now isolated to 2 assertions: Shift+Delete trailing-space cleanup and Meta+Delete soft-line target-range length. The pinned tentative fixture derives an expected end offset of 10 from the post-edit text, while the `deleteSoftLineForward` range spans through offset 11 to include the final `s` that the edit removes; the fixture comment marks modifier cases as platform dependent. Table-cell deletion preserves complete and partial selected cells across multiple ranges, and Delete at the start of a paragraph after a noneditable block now removes only the preceding empty placeholder. The Backspace matrix now has 4/173 failures: only Shift/Control/Alt/Meta+Backspace modifier assertions remain, and the pinned fixture marks modifier behavior as platform-dependent. Trusted keyboard deletion now selects the same pre-line whitespace range reported by `beforeinput`; block/list joins, empty-paragraph placeholders, image deletion, and `<br>`/`<hr>` joins pass. Focused WPT report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-backspace/results-v10/results.json`. The tentative after-dispatch target-range fixture expects ranges to persist after dispatch, while the stable `input-events-get-target-ranges.html` fixture expects them to clear. A trial change broke the stable fixture, so Goosie retains the stable behavior and leaves the contradictory tentative check unresolved. Fresh 27-file Input Events profile, with configured query variants now loaded by the WPT test runner: 20 passed, 7 failed, 0 skipped, and 0 errored. Variant cases are executing; unresolved failures now expose engine mismatches in list and definition-list selection paths. Profile: `/tmp/goosie-task55-backspace/profile-27-v3/results.json`. The isolated forward-delete fixture improved from 34/182 to 2/182 failures at `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-meta-softline-clean/report.html`; changes in this pass preserve selected table cells, including mixed whole-cell and partial-text ranges, and remove the empty placeholder before a noneditable block without deleting the next paragraph’s first character. Exact testdriver regressions cover a selected cell, multiple selected cells, mixed table-cell ranges, the noneditable-block boundary, Backspace joining paragraphs across leading spaces, removing `pre-line` whitespace, flattening list boundaries, deleting empty paragraph placeholders, and joining across `<br>`/`<hr>`. `go test ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest -count=1`, the focused WPT testdriver Backspace regressions, the WPT harness compile-only run, the thirteen focused testdriver deletion regressions, and `git diff --check` pass. An unconfigured `go test ./test/wpt` did not finish within 82 seconds and was interrupted; the focused configured Input Events profile did finish. Earlier repository checks recorded a resizable-buffer structured-clone test failure and a `go vet` copied-mutex warning; both remain to be rechecked before final TASK-055 closure. TASK-055 remains open.

**Default passive listener options on global targets checked 2026-10-29:** `addEventListener()` now defaults `passive` to true for `touchstart`, `touchmove`, `wheel`, and `mousewheel` on Window, Document, the document element, and body. Explicit `{passive: false}` remains non-passive; an omitted or `undefined` passive option uses the default. Ordinary element targets retain a false default. The pinned `dom/events/passive-by-default.html` baseline had 32/100 failing subtests, all for omitted/undefined passive on those global targets: `/tmp/goosie-task55-passive-default-correct-results/results.json`. After the fix the exact fixture passes all 100/100: `/tmp/goosie-task55-passive-default-final/results.json`. A Go regression covers all four event types, all four global targets, explicit false, and a regular element. Focused node/window/generic passive listener tests and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.isEqualNode() namespace and document-tree semantics checked 2026-10-29:** Element equality now compares namespace URI, prefix, and local name; attribute equality compares namespace URI, local name, and value while ignoring prefix. Document and DocumentFragment nodes compare by their child trees without applying element metadata checks. `createHTMLDocument()` now adds a `<title>` only when the nullable title argument is supplied, matching the standard and the equivalent constructed document tree. Added a Go regression covering differing attribute prefixes and equality of default HTML documents constructed through both APIs. The exact pinned `dom/nodes/Node-isEqualNode.html` improved from 7/9 to 9/9 harness tests. Baseline: `/tmp/goosie-task55-equalnode-baseline/results.json`; final: `/tmp/goosie-task55-equalnode-after/wpt/results.json`. Focused JS checks, architecture checks, browser build, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Top-level worker browsing-context activity checked 2026-10-29:** `isFullyActiveBrowsingContext()` now treats any non-iframe Runtime as active before requiring a DOM Document. Dedicated/shared worker globals have no Document but still dispatch events such as SharedWorker `connect`; iframe child contexts continue to require a valid current Document, connected frame, and active parent context. This fixes both previously reproduced SharedWorker regressions: `TestSharedWorkerConnectsMessagePortsAndReusesNamedWorker` and `TestSharedWorkerIsSharedAcrossSameOriginRuntimes` pass. The pinned `event-listener-not-fully-active-callback.html` and `event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html` both pass completely in one fresh run (2/2 files): `/tmp/goosie-task55-worker-context-final/results.json`. All four focused inactive-iframe Go regressions also pass, as do both SharedWorker tests and `git diff --check`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Iframe content-attribute event handler compilation checked 2026-10-29:** `nodeHasBrowsingContext` now recognizes a current, connected same-origin iframe Document even when the node is wrapped by its parent Runtime. This allows an inline handler content attribute to register and compile when `element.onclick` is read before iframe removal; subsequent dispatch after removal remains suppressed by the inactive-document path check. Added a Go regression for compilation-before-removal and callback suppression afterward. The exact pinned `dom/events/event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html` now passes all 16/16 tests: `/tmp/goosie-task55-content-handler-final/results.json`. All four focused inactive-iframe dispatch regressions and `git diff --check` pass. Reproduced the two outstanding SharedWorker package failures (`TestSharedWorkerConnectsMessagePortsAndReusesNamedWorker`, `TestSharedWorkerIsSharedAcrossSameOriginRuntimes`) twice in a focused run; these are in worker code and remain outside this event change, so the full JS package is not claimed green. TASK-055 remains open.

**AbortSignal state across removed iframe contexts checked 2026-10-29:** `AbortController` imported from an iframe retained a stale copied `signal.aborted` value when aborted after removing the frame. AbortSignal `aborted` and `reason` now read live readonly state, and controller abort updates that backing state before running abort algorithms. Added a Go regression for aborting a removed iframe's controller and observing both `aborted` and the custom reason through the parent realm. In the exact pinned `dom/events/event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html`, the baseline had 13/16 testharness failures; after the fix, 15/16 pass, including both AbortSignal subtests. The sole remaining failure is the unrelated compiled inline event-handler assertion (`onclick` expected function, got object). Final report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-abort-state/wpt/results.json`. Focused Go regression, architecture checks, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Iframe WindowProxy dispatchEvent and inactive Window listeners checked 2026-10-29:** Same-origin iframe WindowProxy now exposes `dispatchEvent` and forwards event dispatch into the frame runtime. Window dispatch skips listener callbacks once that frame’s browsing context is no longer fully active. A Go regression verifies that dispatch on a removed iframe Window returns true while invoking zero listeners. In the exact pinned `dom/events/event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html`, failures dropped from 3/16 to 1/16 subtests; the Window case now passes, leaving only the compiled content-handler typing case. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-iframe-window-final/results.json`. The three focused inactive-iframe dispatch regressions and `git diff --check` pass. A full `go test ./test/js -count=1` rerun had two unrelated SharedWorker failures (`TestSharedWorkerConnectsMessagePortsAndReusesNamedWorker`, `TestSharedWorkerIsSharedAcrossSameOriginRuntimes`); the same package passed in the prior integration check. TASK-055 remains open.

**Unowned Document dispatch context checked 2026-10-29:** The event-path continuation check now treats Documents absent from the runtime’s current document and iframe-document map as active, preserving event dispatch for synthetic/unowned Documents. Tracked iframe Documents still require the current connected frame and active child browsing context, preserving removed-iframe callback/path suppression. This resolves 12 existing JS event regressions; `go test ./test/js -count=1` passes. Focused checks for the reported regressions and both new inactive-iframe regressions pass. In a fresh WPT run, `event-listener-not-fully-active-callback.html` passes; `event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html` passes 13/16 subtests, with the same three Window/content-handler/AbortSignal failures. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-active-document-final/results.json`. `git diff --check` passes. TASK-055 remains open.

**Inactive iframe propagation path cancellation checked 2026-10-29:** DOM path dispatch now accepts a continuation predicate and checks it between listener invocations. JS node dispatch supplies the target Document’s fully-active check, so removing the iframe from a capture listener prevents the target and bubble steps while dispatch still completes and resets event state. Added a Go regression for capture removal. The focused pinned `dom/events/event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html` now has 13/16 subtests passing (down from 7/16 passing at the preceding checkpoint); the capture-removal path case passes. Three unrelated failures remain: removed-iframe Window dispatch, compiled event-handler content attribute typing, and removed-iframe AbortSignal state. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-inactive-path-final/results.json`. Focused JS regressions and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Attr ownerDocument through adoption and insertion checked 2026-10-29:** The pinned checkout has no `Node-ownerDocument.html`; the focused `dom/nodes/Document-adoptNode.html` fixture exposed three failures where Attr nodes had no `ownerDocument`. Attr wrappers now retain their owner Document, report it through a readonly getter, resolve it dynamically from `ownerElement` after adoption, and preserve identity when the element is adopted or inserted into another document. Document-specific `createAttribute()`/`createAttributeNS()` now seed the correct owner Document, including detached documents; cloned Attr nodes preserve their owner Document. A Go regression checks explicit element adoption, cross-document insertion, Attr identity, and Attr clone ownership. The pinned adoption fixture now passes all 7/7 tests (baseline 4/7 passed, 3 failed). Baseline: `/tmp/goosie-task55-document-adopt-node-baseline/results.json`; final: `/tmp/goosie-task55-document-adopt-node-after-fix/results.json`. Focused JS tests, `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, browser build, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.normalize() around XML non-Text children checked 2026-10-29:** `Node.normalize()` correctly operates only on Text nodes; its focused WPT initially failed while constructing the fixture because the insertion type guards rejected XML `CDATASection` children. `appendChild()` and `insertBefore()` now allow CDATASection nodes under Element/DocumentFragment parents while the Document child validator continues to reject them as Document children. Added a Go regression that interleaves Text, ProcessingInstruction, CDATASection, Comment, and Element nodes, calls normalize, and verifies all non-Text node identities and positions remain unchanged. The pinned `dom/nodes/Node-normalize.html` now passes 4/4 tests (baseline 1/4 passed, 3 failed); the exact `Node-appendChild.html` and `Node-insertBefore.html` fixtures remain green at 11/11 and 40/40. Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-normalize-baseline/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-normalize-after-fix/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-appendchild-cdata-check/results.json`, and `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-insertbefore-cdata-check/results.json`. Focused Go regression, architecture checks, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Inactive EventTarget dispatch in removed iframe checked 2026-10-29:** Generic EventTarget dispatch now checks that its target browsing context remains fully active before invoking each listener. This preserves dispatch state while suppressing callbacks when the target realm is inactive, including when an earlier listener removes the iframe; a Go regression checks callback suppression and the final Event fields/dispatch result. On pinned `dom/events/event-listener-not-fully-active-target.html`, failures dropped from 13/16 to 7/16 subtests. Baseline: `/tmp/goosie-task55-inactive-target-baseline/results.json`; after: `/tmp/goosie-task55-inactive-target-current/results.json`. The remaining failures involve capture-path propagation, Window/Document/Element event targets and handlers, and AbortSignal handling, so the fixture is not yet green. The focused Go regression and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**HTML element interfaces exercised by Node.cloneNode() checked 2026-10-29:** HTML element prototypes are now shared per WebIDL interface, so heading tags share `HTMLHeadingElement`, `col`/`colgroup` share `HTMLTableColElement`, `del`/`ins` share `HTMLModElement`, and `td`/`th` share `HTMLTableCellElement`. Those interfaces are exposed before first use. Added the standard obsolete `HTMLDirectoryElement` (`dir`) and `HTMLFontElement` (`font`) interfaces, including their reflected attributes. A Go regression checks the original and cloned brands for these tags, shared prototype identity, and obsolete-interface reflection. The exact pinned `dom/nodes/Node-cloneNode.html` now passes all 135/135 tests, up from 125/135 after the prior clone fixes (baseline 120/135). Final report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-clonenode-interface-fix/results.json`. Focused JS tests, `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, browser build, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.cloneNode() Attr and metadata copying checked 2026-10-29:** `Attr` now has its Node-derived prototype/interface brand and `cloneNode()` creates an independent detached Attr preserving namespace/name/value. Generic DOM node cloning now also preserves DocumentType name/public/system identifiers; cloned Documents inherit the source document URL metadata. Go regressions cover Attr identity/brand/value independence and DocumentType/Document clone metadata. In the pinned `dom/nodes/Node-cloneNode.html` profile, the `createAttribute`, `createAttributeNS`, DocumentType, and Document clone checks now pass; total failures dropped from 15/135 to 10/135. The remaining failures concern HTML element interface constructors/prototype brands for `col`, `del`, heading tags, `td`, and unsupported `HTMLDirectoryElement`/`HTMLFontElement`; the fixture is not yet green. Baseline: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-clonenode-baseline/results.json`; final: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-clonenode-final/results.json`. Focused JS tests, `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, browser build, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Fully active listener callback suppression and iframe load task checked 2026-10-29:** Generic EventTarget callbacks, node EventTarget callbacks, and `on*` handler callbacks retain their callback realm and are skipped when that realm's browsing context is no longer fully active. Fully active status follows the iframe tree, current active Document, and frame connectivity, so removing an iframe prevents callbacks from its old Window even when the target is still active. A Go regression verifies that a `handleEvent` object created in an iframe is not invoked after its frame is removed. Diagnosing the same pinned test's `handleEvent` promise timeout showed that `srcdoc` set on a detached iframe was not queued for load when the frame was later inserted through `ParentNode.append()`: this insertion path ran connection effects but only `appendChild()` enqueued iframe loads. Connected iframe insertion now queues the load from the shared DOM-insertion activation path. Pinned `dom/events/event-listener-not-fully-active-callback.html` now passes as a complete WPT file: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. Before the callback fix, its first subtest ran the inactive callback (counter 2, expected 1); before the insertion fix, later `addFrame()` calls did not complete. The focused Go regression passes. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.removeChild() NotFoundError behavior checked 2026-10-29:** When the supplied Node is not a child of the receiver, `removeChild()` now throws a branded `NotFoundError` `DOMException` with code 8. Missing arguments and non-Node values continue to throw `TypeError`; removal from leaf-node receivers likewise follows the not-a-child rule. A Go regression checks the exception brand/code, leaf receiver, TypeError, successful return value, and detachment. The pinned `dom/nodes/Node-removeChild.html` now passes all 28 cases; baseline was 27/28, with the failing test containing the repeated detached/non-child cases across main, iframe, and synthetic documents. Baseline report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-removechild-baseline/results.json`; final: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-removechild-after-fix/results.json`. Focused JS and architecture tests and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.appendChild() WebIDL and hierarchy validation checked 2026-10-29:** `appendChild()` now rejects a missing or non-Node argument with `TypeError` before checking whether the receiver can contain children, rejects Document nodes with `HierarchyRequestError`, and validates the complete resulting Document child list before mutation. The focused Go regression covers null on both a normal parent and text-node leaf, plus atomic rejection of appending another Document. The pinned `dom/nodes/Node-appendChild.html` now passes all 11 cases; baseline was 6/11, with failures in WebIDL conversion, leaf-node error precedence, Document insertion, and adoption of a non-orphan iframe node. Final report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-appendchild-after-fix/results.json`; baseline: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-appendchild-baseline/results.json`. Focused JS and architecture checks and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.insertBefore() pre-insertion validation checked 2026-10-29:** `insertBefore()` now exposes its WebIDL arity (`length === 2`), requires both arguments, accepts only Node/null/undefined as the reference, checks container and ancestor constraints before reference-child membership, rejects prohibited node kinds, and validates the complete prospective Document child sequence before mutation. A Go regression covers required second argument, function arity, atomic Document text rejection; focused JS tests also retain append-on-explicit-null and foreign-reference behavior. The pinned `dom/nodes/Node-insertBefore.html` now passes all 40 cases; baseline was 27/40, with 13 failures spanning check order, argument conversion, and Document hierarchy. Baseline: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-insertbefore-baseline/results.json`; final: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-insertbefore-after-fix/results.json`. `go test ./test/js -run 'TestInsertBefore(WithNullReferenceAppends|PositionsAndRejectsForeignReference|RequiresSecondArgumentAndValidatesDocumentHierarchy)' -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Node.replaceChild() hierarchy and exception ordering checked 2026-10-29:** `replaceChild()` now raises `HierarchyRequestError` for ancestor insertion before checking old-child membership, validates the complete prospective Document child sequence before mutation, and rejects Document/DocumentType children under non-Document parents. Shared child-membership failures now produce a `NotFoundError` `DOMException` with the standard code rather than a `TypeError` renamed to `NotFoundError`. Go regressions cover DOMException branding/code, required ancestor-check precedence, and atomic rejection of replacing the document element with text. The pinned `dom/nodes/Node-replaceChild.html` now passes all 29 cases (previously 17 failed); `ChildNode-before.html` was measured green at 45/45 immediately before this fix. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-node-replacechild-after-fix/results.json`; before report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-childnode-before-check/results.json`. Focused JS tests, architecture checks, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-global EventListener object exception reporting checked 2026-10-29:** Ordinary EventTarget listener records now retain the listener value's owning Window separately from the realm of a callable `handleEvent` method. During listener invocation, cross-realm callback exceptions propagate to the event-dispatch step, which reports them on the listener object's Window and transfers the original exception value through the iframe tree. The regression reproduces the WPT case where a listener object belongs to one iframe, its `handleEvent` function to a second, and the EventTarget to a third; it checks the error target, original method Error prototype, and that sibling windows do not receive the error. The pinned `dom/events/Event-dispatch-throwing-multiple-globals.html` now passes 2/2 testharness cases: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest -count=1` and `git diff --check` pass. Broader event-dispatch and exception-reporting conformance remains open; TASK-055 remains open.

**ChildNode.after() receiver-in-sequence behavior checked 2026-10-29:** `after()` now moves the receiver when it appears among the converted arguments and inserts before the first original following sibling not included in the move sequence. Previously the receiver was removed from the argument sequence, producing the wrong order for cases such as `child.after('text', child)`. Added a Go regression covering Comment, Element, and Text receivers. The pinned `dom/nodes/ChildNode-after.html` now passes 45/45 assertions (was 42/45): `/tmp/goosie-task55-childnode-after/results/results.json`. The broader 12-file `dom/nodes` refresh was interrupted after it remained CPU-active for more than four minutes without producing per-file output; no aggregate result is claimed. Focused JS and architecture checks and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Structured-clone transfer substitution checked 2026-10-29:** MessagePort and ArrayBuffer transfer now stage destination objects, substitute them into nested payloads during cloning, and commit MessagePort endpoint/queue/started state only after validation and payload cloning succeed. This fixes the focused WPT cases for ArrayBuffer, MessagePort, rejecting transfer of a detached platform object, and receiving a MessagePort after its constructor is deleted from the global. The pinned `messagechannel.any.js` profile now passes 141/152 subtests, with one optional skip; 11 failures remain, all involving Goja's missing resizable/growable buffers and length-tracking or out-of-bounds views. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-clone-fixed/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./internal/archtest -count=1` and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Priority:** P0. **Scope:** Full conformance to the current [WHATWG HTML Living Standard](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/), including the supporting platform behavior required by that standard across parsing, DOM, elements, scripting, loading, interaction, and rendering. “HTML5” here means the Living Standard, not a selected subset of page features.
**Base:** `main` after TASK-052 (element interfaces) merges.
**Method:** Multi-phase, measured against the normative algorithms, upstream parser tests, WPT, and Chromium at each gate. Unsupported behavior is a tracked gap, not an implicit skip.
**Language:** English; every claim backed by measurement or test.

**ChildNode.replaceWith() receiver and empty sequence checked 2026-10-29:** `replaceWith()` now removes its receiver with no arguments, is a no-op when the sole argument is the receiver, and preserves the receiver's position when it appears among sibling arguments. For multi-node conversion, the implementation inserts the temporary fragment before the receiver's first following sibling not present in the supplied node sequence, matching the DOM Standard algorithm. Go regressions cover Element, Comment, and Text removal plus sibling ordering and receiver identity. The pinned `dom/nodes/ChildNode-replaceWith.html` now passes 33/33 assertions, up from 27/33 before the change. Focused `go test ./test/js -run 'TestChildNodeReplaceWith(NoArgumentsRemovesReceiver|KeepsReceiverWhenPassedAsArgument)|TestChildNodeBeforeAndReplaceWithMutateInOrder' -count=1` passes. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-replacewith-after-fix/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**MessagePort transfer and structured clone follow-up checked 2026-10-29:** `MessagePort.postMessage()` rejects transferring its own source port and validates the full transfer list and serialized payload before detaching any transferables. A focused regression verifies that failed payload cloning and source-port self-transfer preserve the channels. The pinned MessagePort WPT profile passes 5/5 files: `/tmp/goosie-task55-messageport-atomicity-results/results.json`. The `.any.js` runner now supplies `GLOBAL`, allowing the structured-clone `messagechannel.any.js` test to execute. MessagePort cloning preserves undefined, BigInt and special numbers, boxed Boolean/Number/String/BigInt values, cycles and shared references, Dates, RegExps, built-in Error subtypes with message/cause while omitting custom properties/stack, Blob/File data with destination-realm prototypes and File metadata, and FileList values. Blob/File prototype references survive deletion of the global constructors. A Go regression verifies FileList identity/prototype/liveness and a second covers Blob cloning after `globalThis.Blob` is deleted. Constructor-backed `Response` supports bounded BodyInit consumption and is rejected by structured serialization. The focused WPT now passes 122/152 testharness subtests (one optional skip); 29 fail across ImageData/ImageBitmap, resizable/shared buffers, ArrayBuffer, and MessagePort cases. Report: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest -count=1` and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**ImageData construction and structured cloning checked 2026-10-29:** `ImageData` construction and Canvas `createImageData()`/`getImageData()` now return branded objects backed by `Uint8ClampedArray`; cloning checks dimensions and data length and allocates the pixel array in the destination runtime. A Go regression checks constructor behavior, clone identity, dimensions, typed-array branding, and copied pixel bytes. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest -count=1` passes. The focused `messagechannel.any.js` WPT profile now passes 128/152 testharness subtests (one optional skip), up from 122; 23 failures remain, including unsupported ImageBitmap/OffscreenCanvas, resizable/shared buffers, and additional MessagePort/ArrayBuffer cases: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**ImageBitmap canvas-source cloning checked 2026-10-29:** Added runtime-owned ImageBitmap metadata and a non-constructible interface in `internal/js/image_bitmap.go`. `createImageBitmap(canvas)` resolves a Promise with a fresh bitmap for DOM canvas sources, copying a committed canvas bitmap when dimensions match and otherwise using transparent pixels. MessagePort structured cloning creates a distinct ImageBitmap in the destination runtime with copied dimensions/pixels; closed or malformed bitmaps fail cloning. The Go regression verifies Promise result branding/dimensions and fresh identity after cloning. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest -count=1`, `git diff --check`, and the browser build pass. The focused WPT run reports 135/152 passing subtests, one optional skip, and 17 failures; the ImageBitmap failure still reports missing `OffscreenCanvas`, so this run does not establish the audited OffscreenCanvas WPT case as passing: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. Additional source types, cropping, and full close-state behavior remain unsupported; TASK-055 remains open.

**WPT `.any.js` Window global setup checked 2026-10-29:** Generated Window documents for `.any.js` cases now initialize the upstream `GLOBAL` helper (`isWindow() === true`, `isWorker()` and `isShadowRealm()` false) before loading `testharness.js`. The runner regression verifies helper values and script order. The focused structured-clone WPT now passes 122/152 testharness subtests; one optional case is skipped and 29 fail on other unsupported clone types. `go test ./test/wpt -run '^TestAnyJSTestDocumentLoadsHarnessDependenciesAndSource$' -count=1` and `git diff --check` pass. This is runner support evidence, not a product conformance pass; TASK-055 remains open.

**Synthetic Response body consumption checked 2026-10-29:** All runtimes now expose a constructor-backed `Response` with status/type/bodyUsed reflection and `text()`, `json()`, `blob()`, and `arrayBuffer()` body-consumption methods. BodyInit handles strings, Blob/File data, ArrayBuffers, and views for this bounded slice. A Go regression verifies `instanceof Response`, Blob and typed-array bodies, fresh Blob/ArrayBuffer results, bytes, and body-used state. Response values are marked non-serializable, and `Promise(executor)` now preserves the executor's exact thrown JavaScript value rather than stringifying it; Go regressions check rejection identity/type and Response clone errors in synchronous and promise paths. The pinned structured-clone WPT passes 122/152 testharness subtests, with remaining failures in ImageData/ImageBitmap, resizable/shared buffers, and additional clone types: `/tmp/wpt-results/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest -count=1`, `go test ./test/wpt -run '^TestAnyJSTestDocumentLoadsHarnessDependenciesAndSource$' -count=1`, and `git diff --check` pass. Static Response factories, full Headers/body streams, and broader Fetch conformance remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**Iframe MutationRecord attributes bridge and parser checkpoints checked 2026-10-29:** Source-backed iframe Element wrappers now expose `attributes` through the receiving runtime's cached live `NamedNodeMap`; copying the child runtime's dynamic collection had dropped its `length` property. The iframe identity regression now verifies that a child custom element and its `contentDocument` wrapper share the element and attributes collection, and that `attributes.length` and the attribute name are available. A freshly built browser binary passes the paired pinned `custom-elements/perform-microtask-checkpoint-before-construction.html` and `perform-microtask-checkpoint-before-construction-xml-parser.xhtml` WPTs (2/2): `/tmp/goosie-task55-ce-paired-current/results.json`. Focused JS, DOM, engine, and architecture tests pass; `go vet -all ./...` and `git diff --check` pass. This closes these two checkpoint cases only; TASK-055 remains open.

**ParentNode Document hierarchy validation checked 2026-10-29:** `append()` and `prepend()` now validate the complete prospective child sequence before mutation, rejecting invalid Document child combinations atomically. The pinned `ParentNode-append.html` and `ParentNode-prepend.html` both pass (including the prior 15 hierarchy assertions); a Go regression verifies a failed prepend preserves the existing document element. Focused `dom/nodes` profile: 6/12 files pass, with remaining failures in separate ChildNode/Node mutation algorithms; no fixtures were excluded. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-domnodes-mutation-results/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js -count=1` and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-global EventListener object error reporting gap checked 2026-10-29:** The pinned `dom/events/Event-dispatch-throwing-multiple-globals.html` passes the function-listener case but fails the interface-object case: an exception from `w.listener.handleEvent` (whose method comes from `w2`) is reported at the target's `w3` global rather than the listener object's `w` global. The full `dom/events` profile at this checkpoint was 67 passed, 77 failed, 6 skipped, and 28 errored. A narrowly targeted realm-routing experiment did not fix the interface-object case and was removed; no passing implementation is claimed. Focused repro report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-dom-events-realm-fix/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Form constraint validation and adjacent-break deletion checked 2026-10-29:** `willValidate` now excludes form controls beneath a `datalist`, input submit/image states remain validation candidates, and buttons expose the shared constraint-validation interface with type-based eligibility. The pinned `form-validation-willValidate-datalist.html` passes all 17 subtests; a Go regression covers dynamic datalist ancestry and a foreign-namespace boundary. Backspace at the start of a text node now targets the immediately preceding `<br>` in a cross-block sequence; its Go regression passes, and the pinned Backspace matrix now has 86 failing assertions out of 173, down from 89. The combined `go test ./internal/js ./test/js -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, and `git diff --check` pass. A broad DOM/events profile was not completed, though focused event WPTs pass; TASK-055 remains open.

**Detached form-control activation checked 2026-10-29:** Checkbox/radio click activation now emits `input` and `change` only when the control remains connected after click dispatch. The pinned `dom/events/Event-dispatch-detached-input-and-change.html` passes 1/1; Go regressions cover detached `.click()`, synthetic click dispatch, and connected control activation. The focused JS and archtest suites and `git diff --check` pass. Report: `/tmp/goosie-dom-events-detached-focused/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Maximum Unicode scalar escape in script strings checked 2026-10-29:** Goja's lexer panicked on the valid JavaScript string escape `\u{10FFFF}` because it left the closing brace unconsumed at the maximum scalar value. Goosie now rewrites only that exact escape inside quoted strings to its equivalent surrogate pair before compilation; escaped backslashes are preserved. The pinned `form-validation-validity-patternMismatch.html` passes, and a Go regression covers both `Run` and `Evaluate` plus escaped-backslash behavior. TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-editing-host InputEvent target ranges checked 2026-10-29:** A non-collapsed selection spanning different editing hosts (or a non-editable boundary) no longer reports a misleading `beforeinput` target range. The pinned `input-events-get-target-ranges-deleting-range-across-editing-host-boundaries.tentative.html` passes 1/1, and a Go regression covers the cross-host selection. A focused nine-file target-range profile remains 3 passed and 6 failed, with deletion and list-join gaps outstanding. TASK-055 remains open.

**Event path snapshot across Window capture checked 2026-10-29:** Dispatch now snapshots the DOM event path before Window capture listeners run and reuses it for node propagation and `composedPath()`, so moving or removing the target during a Window listener preserves the dispatch path. The pinned `Event-dispatch-target-moved.html` and `Event-dispatch-target-removed.html` each pass 1/1. Focused DOM/JS, architecture, and diff checks pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Object EventListener callback errors checked 2026-10-29:** Object listeners now resolve `handleEvent` for each dispatch, preserve the listener object as `this`, and report non-callable values and callback exceptions through the owning Window error path. The pinned `EventListener-handleEvent.html` passes 1/1, and a Go regression checks getter evaluation and TypeError reporting. Focused JS, architecture, and diff checks pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Implicit form submission through testdriver Enter checked 2026-10-29:** The WPT testdriver now performs implicit submission once for eligible single-line input controls, avoids a duplicate engine key-default submission, honors canceled `keydown`, and excludes textarea and button-like input types from this implicit-submit path. The pinned `form-submission-0` profile passes 31 runnable tests with one unclassified fixture skipped; `implicit-submission.optional.html` and the `the-form-element` suite pass. Focused harness/engine checks pass. Report: `/tmp/forms-agent-form-submission-current/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Exact small-step number validation checked 2026-10-29:** `input[type=number]` step mismatch now uses exact decimal rational arithmetic when comparing a value to its step base, preserving fractional quotient information when the quotient exceeds float64 integer precision. The pinned `form-validation-validity-stepMismatch.html` passes all 28 assertions, and a Go regression covers value 17 with step `3e-15`. Focused JS and architecture suites pass. Report: `/tmp/task55-form-step-exact/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Constraint validity state and textarea IDL reflection checked 2026-10-29:** `ValidityState.valueMissing` now applies mutability only to value-mode input and textarea states while retaining the distinct disabled-state rules for checkbox, radio, file, and select controls. Textarea reflects `disabled`, `required`, and `readOnly`; required radio groups with an empty name are not treated as missing. The pinned 11-file validity set passes 11/11, and the targeted disabled-state assertions pass 3/3. Go regressions cover dynamic cloned textareas and disabled/readonly required states. Reports: `/tmp/task55-form-validity-final/results.json`, `/tmp/task55-form-disabled-final/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**HTML name property reflection checked 2026-10-29:** The `name` IDL property now reflects the content attribute on `embed`, `frame`, `object`, and `textarea`. The pinned `name-content-attribute-and-property.html` passes 1/1, and a Go regression covers attribute/property round trips. Focused JS, architecture, and diff checks pass. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-name-reflection-results/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Rendered text getter/setter follow-up checked 2026-10-29:** `innerText` and `outerText` getters apply inherited computed `text-transform`, omit hidden/collapsed and non-rendered embedded/control content, collect select option rows, preserve atomic-inline whitespace boundaries, separate block children with line breaks, add separators around empty block children, and normalize/collapse whitespace by `white-space` mode. The setters convert normalized CR/LF sequences to `<br>` elements; `outerText` also handles empty replacement, adjacent text-node merging, and detached-node exceptions. Go regressions cover transform, visibility, block boundaries, inline runs, whitespace modes, select rows, and setters. The refreshed eight-file innerText/outerText WPT profile passes 7/8; `dynamic-getter.html` passes 7/7, `innertext-with-white-spaces.html` passes all 12 declared variants, and both setter files pass. `getter.html` failures fell from 150 to 40 of 276 assertions. Remaining getter gaps include `::first-line`/`::first-letter` text, SVG content, empty option boundaries in parent text, paragraph blank-line semantics, and table whitespace. Report: `/tmp/goosie-innertext-atomic-integrated/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Repository curated WPT profile attempted 2026-10-29:** A fresh run of `testdata/wpt-config.json` generated 2,456 test and 2,453 reference images, then produced no further visual artifacts while the runner remained alive. Since subsequent testharness cases may not emit image files, this does not establish a hang; the run was terminated before its final report and is not counted as verification. Focused WPT profiles and package tests remain the available evidence; TASK-055 remains open.

**Integrated package verification checked 2026-10-29:** After the event, range, and form changes above, `go test ./internal/dom ./internal/js ./test/dom ./test/js -count=1`, WPT harness compilation (`go test ./test/wpt -run '^$' -count=1`), `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This scoped integration run does not replace the full repository test and gate runs required for TASK-055 completion.

**Cross-inline deletion and target-range tree scoping checked 2026-10-29:** Backward word deletion extends its `beforeinput` StaticRange across adjacent inline text nodes, removes empty formatting left by deletion, preserves the separating space as `&nbsp;`, and clears ranges after dispatch. ShadowRoot listeners receive events, and trusted and synthetic InputEvents scope target ranges to the current tree root. The testdriver Command+B shortcut emits `beforeinput`/`input` with `formatBold`. Go regressions cover cross-node target ranges and bold event order. The pinned `input-events-get-target-ranges.html` and `input-events-get-target-ranges-shadow-dom.html` both pass. The `<pre>` cross-block deletion range and Backspace under `white-space: pre-line` were corrected. Backspace range handling now uses the expected parent boundary after a preceding `<br>`; the pinned Backspace matrix has 86 failing assertions out of 173. The current configured 27-file Input Events profile reports 20 passed, 7 failed, and 0 errored at `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile-backspace-boundary/results.json`; `input-events-typing.html` passes 13/13. TASK-055 remains open.

**Legacy editing commands and clipboard Input Events checked 2026-10-29:** `document.execCommand()` now handles ordered/unordered list wrapping and toggling, line/paragraph/rule insertion, strike/superscript/subscript formatting, color and font formatting, block alignment/indentation, link creation/removal, and clipboard copy/cut/paste. Color commands parse CSS colors for normalized `InputEvent.data`; cut and paste use read/write and read-only `DataTransfer` payloads and report `deleteByCut`/`insertFromPaste`. A Go regression covers list switching, color event data, link/unlink, and clipboard round-trip. The pinned `input-events-exec-command.html` passes all 307 assertions. The refreshed configured 27-file Input Events profile reports 18 passed, 8 failed, and 1 timeout at `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile-keyfix/results.json`; remaining failures are complex deletion target ranges/list joins, editing-host boundaries, shadow-root ranges, and the `getTargetRanges()` timeout. TASK-055 remains open.

**WPT keyboard editing event order checked 2026-10-29:** The testdriver adapter maps the macOS Command key to `Meta`, reports uppercase printable keys when Shift is held, dispatches cancellable `keypress` between `keydown` and the editing default, and focuses a contenteditable host when `test_driver.click()` targets an editable descendant. Forward Delete now reaches the next editable text node when the caret sits at the end of inline text. A no-op Delete/Backspace emits the paired `input` event for an empty editing host, while unchanged deletions in nonempty hosts do not. The pinned `input-events-typing.html` passes 13/13 tests; `input-events-delete-selection.html` passes 6/6. The refreshed configured 27-file Input Events profile reports 17 passed, 9 failed, and 1 timed out at `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile-keyfix/results.json`. Remaining failures include `execCommand` editing commands, complex `getTargetRanges()` and list joins, and shadow-root target ranges; this is partial Input Events progress and TASK-055 remains open.

**Contenteditable testdriver deletion path regression checked 2026-10-29:** A focused engine regression follows the WPT sequence: focus a contenteditable through `HTMLElement.focus()`, select text, and invoke the runner's key-default hook. It emits `beforeinput` and `input` with `deleteContentBackward` and removes the selection. The earlier 04:52 Input Events report showed this WPT failing 6/6, but this was superseded by the 05:05 and 05:12 pinned profiles, both of which report `input-events-delete-selection.html` passing 6/6. A standalone rerun attempt stalled without a report; the regression is additional engine coverage and the WPT result is established by the later profile reports. TASK-055 remains open.

**Repository verification checked 2026-10-28:** `go vet -all ./...` passes. A serialized full `go test ./...` run passes all packages except the `test/qc` traceability gate, which reports 59 automation cases awaiting QC sign-off and a stale `traceability.golden.json`; TASK-055 §8 restricts changes to that golden without an explicit proposal, so it was left untouched. An earlier concurrent verification hit one 10-second timeout in `test/surface/TestFrameMarkRecordsFiveTimestamps`; the isolated test and serialized full run both pass. The test, gate, and WPT packages otherwise pass.

**HTML fragment parsing and WPT byte-stream coverage checked 2026-10-29:** raw-text and RCDATA fragment contexts (`title`, `textarea`, `style`, `xmp`, `iframe`, `noembed`, `noframes`, `script`, `noscript`, and `plaintext`) now consume fragment input as text through EOF. Raw-text serialization preserves text without escaping `<`/`&`; `noscript` text is escaped in template contents and retains raw serialization outside templates. Fragment parsing seeds the form pointer from an ancestor form, and `DocumentFragment.getElementById()` supports template contents. The WPT runner fetches linked stylesheets through a bounded same-origin path, which lets encoding tests observe decoded document text through their external selector sheet. The full pinned `html/syntax/parsing-html-fragments` profile reports 14 passed, 0 failed, 2 skipped, and 0 errors (all runnable candidates pass); the nine HTTP/meta/BOM byte-stream fixtures pass 9/9. The refreshed pinned `html/syntax/parsing` profile reports 60 passed, 1 failed, 32 skipped, and 0 errors; the sole failure is tentative `DOMContentLoaded-defer-task-order.tentative.html`, whose expected ordering is explicitly described by the fixture as unspecified between the DOM manipulation and timer task sources. Focused parser, DOM, JS, and WPT runner regressions pass. Overall HTML conformance remains open.

**BroadcastChannel constructor, close, delivery ordering, and detached iframe eligibility checked 2026-10-29:** the constructor accepts the empty string and a JavaScript wrapper enforces construction with `new`; `postMessage()` reports DOMException codes for `InvalidStateError` and `DataCloneError`. Closing a channel suppresses queued inbound events, including later messages queued before a handler closes it. The hub queues deliveries for each top-level browsing context in recipient creation order, including across iframe runtimes. Reading `contentWindow.BroadcastChannel` creates the initial child runtime, and BroadcastChannel send/receive is suppressed whenever an iframe in the runtime's ancestor chain is disconnected. Go regressions cover constructor rules, error codes, close behavior, target identity, ordered delivery, and detached iframe messaging. The pinned `interface.any.js`, `basics.any.js`, `blobs.html`, `sandbox.html`, `detached-iframe.html`, `ordering.html`, and `cross-origin.html` pass; the refreshed full `webmessaging/broadcastchannel` profile reports 7 passed, 1 failed, and 3 errored (11 files): `/tmp/goosie-task55-broadcastchannel/results.json`. Remaining failure/error cases are the unsupported SharedWorker subtest, service-worker, cross-partition, and opaque-origin tests. Focused JS, engine, app, and architecture tests, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Full BroadcastChannel and HTML conformance remain open.

**Dedicated worker BroadcastChannel and importScripts support checked 2026-10-29:** the local worker runtime now fetches classic worker scripts and `importScripts()` dependencies through a host-provided bounded fetch hook, resolves relative URLs against the worker script URL, and shares its creator's BroadcastChannel hub. Blob worker URLs inherit their embedded origin, and workers inherit their creator's top-level site for BroadcastChannel storage partitioning. Worker runtimes are ticked as part of the parent's task drain so later channel messages are delivered, and `close()` prevents further BroadcastChannel sends while allowing already-running script completion. Go regressions cover bounded script loading, relative import resolution, HTTP and Blob worker BroadcastChannel delivery, and suppression from a closed worker. The pinned `workers.html` now passes 9/10 testharness tests; its remaining failure is the unsupported SharedWorker constructor. The refreshed full profile reports that file as a failure because of this one subtest. Service workers and SharedWorker remain open; full BroadcastChannel and HTML conformance remain open.

**URLSearchParams query-string input and multi-frame BroadcastChannel order checked 2026-10-29:** the constructor now strips one optional leading `?` from string input as required by URLSearchParams parsing. The WPT iframe ordering helper uses `window.location.search` to select per-frame behavior; the earlier timeout came from parsing `?id=...` as part of the parameter name. A focused Go regression covers the leading question mark, and the pinned `ordering.html` passes after iframe deliveries are drained in creation order across the top-level context. The refreshed full BroadcastChannel profile now reports 7 passed, 1 failed, and 3 errored; full HTML conformance remains open.

**Cross-origin WPT iframe transport checked 2026-10-29:** the pinned `cross-origin.html` passes. The WPT host-info fixture generates HTTPS aliases even though the runner owns one HTTP listener; the server now substitutes its local port for the HTTPS port-zero token, and the iframe fetch adapter maps only configured `*.wpt.localhost` aliases with that port to the local listener. The child's Runtime retains the original HTTPS host and scheme, so BroadcastChannel still observes distinct origins. Unit tests cover HTTPS host substitution and reject unknown hosts, foreign ports, and userinfo. The refreshed full BroadcastChannel profile reports 7 passed, 1 failed, and 3 errored; the cross-partition fixture still times out while exercising popup, anonymous iframe, dispatcher, and worker paths.

**Custom-element construction fallback and cross-realm error reporting checked 2026-10-28:** failed autonomous custom-element construction uses the native `HTMLUnknownElement` fallback prototype, and customized built-ins use their native tag interface prototype, independently of the constructor's defining realm. Imported iframe Node proxies resolve their source prototype dynamically; Go regressions cover source-prototype updates, fallback interface identity, and Promise continuation behavior. When a foreign constructor throws, the bridge retains the original error and throwing runtime on the active construction stack and dispatches the definition-window `ErrorEvent` after the destination element receives its fallback prototype. A fresh WPT runner confirmed the asynchronous iframe registry probe passes; the earlier registry-loss result came from a stale runner binary. That fresh run exposed a different error: `functionRealm()` found the imported constructor's source runtime but then replaced it with the wrapper function's runtime, so a customized built-in that returned a different element reported its validation exception to the destination document. The lookup now retains an explicitly mapped source realm while following proxy targets. A Go regression covers that invalid-return case and checks the destination built-in fallback prototype. The fresh pinned focused WPT report passes both `custom-elements/cross-realm-callback-report-exception.html` and `custom-elements/upgrading/upgrade-custom-element-error-event.html` (2/2): `/tmp/goosie-task55-crossrealm-construct-fresh-runner/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1` passes. The iframe implementation and full HTML conformance remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.

**Input directory refreshed after reftest and smoke-test classification, 2026-10-28:** the pinned input directory now reports 167 candidates: 161 passed, 0 failed, 5 skipped, and 1 timed out (99.4% of non-skipped candidates), at WPT commit `11ae8c1179be2dd38070846b01c2e9338ef81770`; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-final7/results.json`. The five skips are four manual interaction tests and one form-action helper referenced by a test. Eight range reftests with unquoted `rel=match` and nine `-crash` no-crash pages are now discovered and pass; smoke-test success means only that bounded headless rendering completed. Module scripts in external iframe realms now use the existing bounded default-import executor. The cross-origin picker iframe still times out: its child posts a message and the parent receives it, but the async test reports 3/4 subtests failed or timed out, so the origin assertions are unresolved. The iframe API implementation and the broader HTML conformance task remain incomplete.

**XML DOM Parsing refresh checked 2026-10-28:** `DOMParser.parseFromString()` now exposes `Document` rather than the legacy `XMLDocument` interface while preserving XML parsing state. Application/xhtml+xml `createElement()` nodes carry the XHTML namespace, and the DOM retains actual qualified-name prefixes so XML fragment and XMLSerializer paths perform namespace fixup and reject an unprefixed colon name with `InvalidStateError`. The pinned WPT files `DOMParser-parseFromString-xml.html`, `XMLSerializer-serializeToString.html`, `innerhtml-01.xhtml`, `innerhtml-03.xhtml`, and `xml-serialization.xhtml` pass 5/5. A refreshed complete pinned `domparsing` run reports 43/77 pass, 25 fail, 9 errors, and 0 skipped: all 42/42 non-tentative files pass; remaining failures/errors are in tentative streaming-markup coverage. `go test ./internal/dom ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/dom ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes the current non-tentative DOM Parsing XML failures only; streaming markup and full HTML conformance remain open.

**Window event target propagation and listener options checked 2026-10-28:** Window listeners now parse the legacy boolean capture argument and the options dictionary's `capture` and `once` members. One-shot listeners are removed before callback invocation. Connected node events run Window capture before the node path and Window bubble after it; detached nodes do not propagate to Window. Go regressions verify capture/bubble order, one-shot behavior, and detached-target isolation. An engine regression verifies an external iframe module import, child-to-parent `postMessage`, and parent `async`/`await` resolution. The focused input WPT aggregate remains 161 passed, 5 skipped, and 1 timeout; the cross-origin picker fixture is still unresolved.

**Input value and checkable activation checked 2026-10-28:** the WPT curator now includes `html/semantics/forms/the-input-element`. Input `value = null` follows legacy null-to-empty conversion; color values parse through CSS color parsing and serialize as opaque lowercase sRGB hex, including system colors. Clean attribute-backed values are sanitized on first `.value` read while `defaultValue` preserves the raw content attribute; this covers URL/tel line-break removal and URL whitespace trimming. Input type keyword matching uses ASCII case-insensitive comparison, so non-ASCII lookalikes remain invalid. Input clicks pre-activate checkbox/radio state before click listeners, restore checkedness when canceled, clear checkbox indeterminateness, and dispatch trusted input/change events after successful state changes; radio required validity uses the radio group’s tree scope and form owner. The focused value/color/radio WPT selection passes 5/5, checkbox/radio activation and event-order files pass 4/4, `default-type.html` passes 1/1 (72 subtests), and `url.html` plus `telephone.html` pass 2/2. The initial broad input run reported 62/148 passing. The refreshed pinned input-directory run contains 167 candidates: 161 passed, 0 failed, 5 skipped, and 1 timed out (99.4% of non-skipped candidates); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-final7/results.json`. It includes hidden `_charset_`, mutability, blur/change, Backspace/Delete, number-state and email editing, Unicode pattern cases, radio grouping and sequential focus, FileList identity, table-form pointer fixes, `Element.after()`, testdriver clicks/action sequences, focus scrolling, range datalist tick marks, image-button dimensions, form restoration through `window.open()`, bounded default-import module scripts, picker activation consumption, input cloning, dynamic focus-visible state, and pointer-driven text selection. Four skips are manual interaction tests; one is a form-action helper referenced by its parent test. The cross-origin picker iframe is the only timeout. Input and full HTML conformance remain open.

**Image button dimensions checked 2026-10-28:** dynamic `input[type=image]` elements now load their `src` when inserted or when connected `src`/`type` changes, and expose `width`/`height` from valid dimension attributes and loaded image sizes. Dimension parsing accepts a non-negative signed integer prefix (including `-0`), and ratio-only SVGs use the HTML default 300×150 sizing algorithm. The WPT harness now supplies its bounded same-origin image fetcher to script-created image loads. The pinned `image-width-height.html` passes all 32 assertions (1/1 file), covering PNGs, SVG defaults, rendered/hidden/detached inputs, malformed dimension values, and image load completion. A Go regression covers reflected dimension parsing. This WPT passes in the latest 167-candidate input report (`/tmp/goosie-task55-input-final7/results.json`); full input and HTML conformance remain open.

**Input and select `showPicker()` checks checked 2026-10-28:** `HTMLInputElement.showPicker()` and `HTMLSelectElement.showPicker()` check disabledness and state-applicable `readonly` before origin and transient-activation restrictions; input file/color pickers remain available in cross-origin child contexts. Select pickers also throw `NotSupportedError` when detached or hidden by `display:none` or `content-visibility:hidden`. Successful calls consume activation without focusing the control. `navigator.userActivation` reports active and sticky activation; host input dispatch and the WPT testdriver hook provide transient activation. The WPT curator now includes `html/semantics/forms/the-select-element`; the pinned select picker files `show-picker-being-cv-hidden.html`, `show-picker-disabled.html`, `show-picker-multiple.html`, and `show-picker-size.html` pass 4/4. Go coverage checks input exception ordering, activation consumption, the readonly state matrix, and cross-origin behavior. The bounded static default-import module path now allows the focused input show-picker module tests to run; `show-picker-disabled-readonly.html`, `show-picker-does-not-focus.html`, and `show-picker-user-gesture.html` pass. The cross-origin iframe fixture still times out before its message assertions. Native file/color/date/select picker UI, exact activation-expiry behavior, and end-to-end picker selection remain unimplemented; this closes API precondition behavior only.

**Select options, insertion, and required validity checked 2026-10-28:** script-facing append/insert/replace operations now reject inserting an ancestor into its descendant with `HierarchyRequestError`, preventing cyclic DOM trees and traversal stack overflows. The legacy `Option()` constructor creates detached options with text, value, default-selected attribute, and the HTML constructor's distinct initial-selectedness rules. `HTMLSelectElement` exposes live indexed option access, and its `item()` method returns the indexed option or null. Inserting an already-selected option into a single-select clears the previous selectedness, including a detached placeholder option reinserted at the start. The pinned WPT files `common-HTMLOptionsCollection-add.html`, `option-selectedness-script-mutation.html`, `select-add.html`, `select-remove.html`, `select-selectedOptions-and-selected.html`, `select-validity.html`, and `show-picker-being-rendered.html` pass 7/7; these include indexed access, ancestor-insertion rejection, and placeholder removal/restoration. Go regression coverage checks placeholder restoration and `Option()` defaults. The full 345-file select-element aggregate could not be measured: two attempts were stopped for runaway memory use; the first reached about 3.8 GB RSS and the second exceeded 1.6 GB while processing early cases. The targeted API set passes; full select and HTML conformance remain open.

**WPT manual and unclassified candidate reporting checked 2026-10-28:** `DiscoverTests` now retains manual and unclassified pages, and the runner reports explicit skip reasons instead of silently dropping them. It excludes standalone `resources/` helper files and reference pages from the candidate set; manual detection recognizes `-manual` filenames and WPT `flags=interact` metadata before the testharness marker. The pinned input cases `disabled-click-picker-manual.html`, `event-select-manual.html`, `file-manual.html`, and `maxlength-manual.html` report explicit manual skips. The latest 167-candidate input report has 161 passes, 0 failures, 5 skips, and 1 timeout; four skips are manual interaction tests and one is a form-action helper referenced by its parent test. Go coverage verifies discovery, classification, support-file exclusion, and explicit skip reasons. The report is `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-final7/results.json`; HTML and WPT conformance remain open.

**Bounded module-script subset, input cloning, and focus-visible behavior checked 2026-10-28:** the engine now executes inline and external module scripts using a strict module-scope wrapper when they contain static default imports whose dependencies expose JSON-serializable default values. The graph is capped at 32 module scripts, depth 8, 2 MiB total source, and the existing per-script byte/fetch budgets; unsupported syntax and fetch failures are surfaced as script errors. External iframe realms use the same bounded executor. This is not a native ECMAScript Module Record implementation: named/side-effect imports, live bindings, cycles, top-level await, import maps, CORS/module MIME validation, and complete module graph identity are not implemented. The ten-file module-driven input sample passes 9/10 files after pointer-selection support; the cross-origin picker iframe remains unresolved despite its child module executing and delivering a message. The latest full input aggregate passes 161/162 non-skipped files. `cloning-steps.html` passes all 68 assertions after cloning the input indeterminate flag; removing that copy made its 22 indeterminate assertions fail. Focused dynamic input type-change tests pass 2/2; removing the focused-input visibility rule made both files fail. `click-user-gesture.html` passes 1/1 after picker-capable input activation consumes a blessed transient activation; removing the consume step makes the Go regression fail for all seven picker states. `selection-pointer.html` passes 21/21 after the WPT shim models pointer-driven text selection; removing the selection behavior makes all 21 assertions fail. The latest full input report is `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-final7/results.json`; the cross-origin picker iframe is the only timeout in that run.

**Range datalist ticks checked 2026-10-28:** range inputs keep their intrinsic inline-block sizing and paint one-pixel datalist ticks for enabled options with numeric values inside the input's min/max range.
