The template owner-document file passes 112/115; its three failures also require creating a rendered iframe document. This is a narrow template slice, not a parser-conformance result.

**Table/template slice checked 2026-10-26:** `HTMLTableElement.rows`, `tBodies`, `caption`, `tHead`, and `tFoot`, table-section `rows`, and table-row `cells` now expose live DOM collections/accessors. Table insertion mode delegates `template`, `style`, and `script` start tags through the in-head rules without foster parenting, which keeps templates in their table parent. The focused table/template WPT files for clearing table, table-body, and table-row contexts pass 3/3; Go regression coverage confirms live collection lengths and that template contents do not count as table rows. Setters and table mutation methods remain part of the unfinished table-interface work.

**SVG foreign-content slice checked 2026-10-26:** parser-inserted SVG tags now use the SVG namespace, apply the standard adjusted names for legacy camel-case SVG elements, and recognize HTML integration at `foreignObject`, `desc`, and `title`. HTML documents now match `getElementsByTagName` case-insensitively for HTML elements and case-sensitively for foreign elements; `getElementsByTagNameNS` and element `namespaceURI` are exposed. The pinned WPT `Document.getElementsByTagName-foreign-{01,02}.html` and `Element.getElementsByTagName-foreign-{01,02}.html` pass 4/4. MathML integration points, SVG attribute-name adjustment, and broader foreign-content rules remain incomplete.

**Fragment parsing slice checked 2026-10-26:** `ParseFragmentBounded` now initializes parsing from an element context, resets insertion mode against that context, tokenizes raw-text/RCDATA contexts, applies the adjusted-current-node namespace to CDATA, and returns a `DocumentFragment`. Element and template `innerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, shadow-root `innerHTML`, and the current document-write insertion path use this parser instead of synthetic `<div>` wrappers. The focused CDATA fragment WPT passes 15/15 subtests; table/textarea/SVG/HTML context regressions pass; the complete pinned parser directory is 15/62 passing, 29 failing, and 18 timed out. Full parser construction, scripting states, and MathML tree construction remain incomplete.

**Parser text visibility at script pauses checked 2026-10-26:** text nodes are now committed before a parser-blocking script executes, so a script reading the partially built document sees every consumed character token; clearing the builder map also prevents the parser's later EOF flush from overwriting script edits to those nodes. The engine regression reproduces the WPT's misnested-form-inside-template fixture and checks the exact `innerHTML`; removing the flush makes it fail with the trailing `G` missing, then restoring it passes. The focused `misnested-form-in-template.html` WPT passes. That run of all 62 pinned `html/syntax/parsing` files reported 56 passed, 3 failed, 3 errored, 0 skipped (90.32% file pass rate), up from the earlier 45/62 snapshot. At that measurement, remaining failures included defer-task ordering, tentative pagehide reentrancy, no-script head element interface behavior, lifecycle events in `the-end.html`, and SVG script execution; the parser-directory score is not a whole-standard conformance score.

**SVG parser-script timing checked 2026-10-26:** parser-eligible SVG script elements now execute at their parser pause: after a matching `</script>` closes them or immediately after an SVG self-closing script start tag. Unclosed and HTML-breakout-closed SVG scripts remain ineligible. A Go engine regression verifies a closed SVG script's side effect is visible to the next HTML parser script; suppressing the close-time callback makes it fail. `unclosed-svg-script.html` passes all 5/5 assertions, including its external self-closing script case. The fresh 62-file parser-directory run now reports 57 passed, 2 failed, 3 errored, 0 skipped (91.94%). Remaining non-passing files are defer-task ordering, tentative pagehide reentrancy, scripting-disabled `in head noscript` element prototype behavior, the `no-doctype-name.html` harness error, and lifecycle-event assertions/timeouts in `the-end.html`. This is parser-suite evidence only, not whole-standard conformance.

**Sandboxed srcdoc scripting flag checked 2026-10-27:** `HTMLIFrameElement.sandbox` now reflects a stable token-list object and forwards string assignments to the content attribute. A sandboxed `srcdoc` without the ASCII `allow-scripts` token is parsed with scripting disabled; `<noscript>` uses the in-head-noscript insertion mode, ignores a nested `<head>` token, and retains the following `<style>` as an element. With scripting enabled, head `<noscript>` remains raw text. Interface prototypes expose Web IDL `@@toStringTag`, so wrappers stringify with their interface names. Go parser/interface regressions pass, and `inhead-noscript-head.html` passes 1/1. A mutation that forced the srcdoc scripting flag on made that WPT fail with a Text node instead of `HTMLStyleElement`. A fresh 62-file parser run reports 58 passed, 1 failed, 3 errored, 0 skipped (93.55% file pass rate); the remaining files are defer-task ordering, tentative pagehide reentrancy, `no-doctype-name.html` harness completion, and `the-end.html` lifecycle events. This parser suite is not whole-standard conformance, and broader sandboxed-origin/security behavior remains incomplete.

**Document lifecycle event follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** DOMContentLoaded, document load, and pageshow now carry trusted event state; `load` targets the document and propagates through the window path, and pageshow exposes the `PageTransitionEvent` interface and `persisted` value. Go lifecycle regressions verify targets, trust, propagation flags, interface strings, and persisted state. A mutation making DOMContentLoaded untrusted was caught by the focused WPT `the-end.html`; with the implementation restored, that WPT passes 1/1. A fresh full 62-file parsing run reports 59 passed, 1 failed, 2 errored, 0 skipped (95.16% file pass rate). Remaining results: `DOMContentLoaded-defer-task-order.tentative.html` times out on task ordering, `adoption-agency-reparenting-pagehide.tentative.html` fails because pagehide fires synchronously during adoption-agency reparenting, and `no-doctype-name.html` completes without reporting harness tests. `go test ./test/dom ./test/js ./test/engine -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, and scoped `go vet -all` pass. This improves only a narrow parser/lifecycle slice and does not establish full HTML Living Standard support.

**Window event handlers and iframe-delayed document load checked 2026-10-27:** Window `onload`, `onpagehide`, `onpageshow`, `onunload`, `onhashchange`, `onpopstate`, `ononline`, and `onoffline` are now handler IDL attributes backed by the Window listener list. Callbacks run with Window as `this` and `currentTarget`; replacing a handler preserves its listener slot. The engine now fires DOMContentLoaded, processes queued iframe document loads and frame load events, then fires the parent load/pageshow phase. This exposed and fixed `no-doctype-name.html`: parser-correct empty-name doctypes in three loaded iframe files were previously hidden behind each frame's initial `about:blank` document when the parent `load` handler ran. A regression verifies an externally loaded iframe's doctype name in its load handler; a WPT mutation moving parent load before iframe completion fails the no-doctype test, then the restored order passes. Focused `no-doctype-name.html` and `the-end.html` pass 2/2. The refreshed 62-file parser run reports 60 passed, 0 failed, 1 skipped, 1 errored (98.36% over non-skipped tests). The remaining results are the tentative defer-task ordering timeout and tentative pagehide-reentrancy case, now correctly classified as optional unsupported. DOM, JS, and engine suites, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. Child iframe navigations remain synchronous and this does not constitute complete browsing-context or HTML conformance.

**WPT optional-precondition reporting checked 2026-10-27:** `assert_implements_optional()` produces test status `PRECONDITION_FAILED` (4), which the runner previously counted as a product failure. The result collector now separates those statuses into explicit skips and retains each reason; global optional precondition failures are also reported as skips. An integration regression mixes pass, fail, and optional subtests and verifies the runner counts each accurately. In the parser corpus, `adoption-agency-reparenting-pagehide.tentative.html` now reports a skip because the upstream test marks pagehide-on-iframe-disconnection as optional. This is a reporting correction, not a new engine behavior. `go test ./test/wpt -run TestWPTTestHarnessRunnerExecutesAndReportsTests -count=1` and `go vet -all ./test/wpt` pass. The refreshed parser aggregate above is 60/62 passing, one optional skip, and one defer-task-order timeout.

**Cross-window postMessage for srcdoc frames checked 2026-10-27:** `postMessage()` now queues messages between a parent Runtime and a scripted `srcdoc` child Runtime, applies target-origin matching against the receiver's origin, and clones JSON-compatible payloads into the receiving realm. The parent pumps child iframe task queues; iframe `contentWindow` supports message listeners registered before or after child creation and an `onmessage` bridge. A Go integration regression verifies child-to-parent and parent-to-child delivery, asynchronous tasks, `MessageEvent.source` identity in both realms, Window `currentTarget`, and parent-side listeners on the child Window. Changing the cross-runtime event type from `message` made this regression fail; the mutation was restored. At that checkpoint, externally navigated frames did not yet have child JavaScript runtimes. Transfers/full structured clone and cross-origin browsing-context policy remain incomplete.

**External iframe child-runtime follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** fetched iframe documents now get a child Runtime, execute synchronous classic inline and external scripts in document order, and can exchange `postMessage` with their parent. The iframe loader now receives the active document URL for each request, so nested frame URLs resolve against the child document; already completed unchanged frame sources are not reloaded when the parent rebinds its DOM. An engine regression checks external classic script fetching against the child URL, async parent message delivery and source identity, nested iframe load completion, and the nested loader's base URL. Removing the child's inherited frame loader makes the nested-load assertion fail; the mutation was restored. Focused engine iframe tests pass. Async/defer script scheduling, redirects, sandboxed opaque origins, cross-origin Window access controls, structured-clone transfers, and broader navigation lifecycle behavior remain open.

**Iframe Window.onerror and cross-realm callback follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `Element.outerHTML` now serializes and replaces an element using fragment parsing; `innerHTML` and `outerHTML` inserted content attributes are activated as event handlers, and replacing `body` refreshes the document's body reference. Parent objects exposed in child realms use identity-preserving proxies, and callback arguments share an object map so the same object remains strictly equal when passed more than once across realms. Pinned WPT `html/webappapis/scripting/events/onerroreventhandler.html` now passes 1/1 files (all three subtests), after previously timing out or failing. A mutation that used a fresh cross-realm clone map per callback argument makes its ErrorEvent object identity assertion fail; disabling inserted-subtree activation also makes the innerHTML handler and iframe onerror regressions fail. The eight-case focused event-handler set including `onerroreventhandler.html` passes 8/8. A ninth related `event-handler-attributes-windowless-body.html` case completed without reporting tests and remains unresolved. The focused engine regression verifies outerHTML-created body onerror arguments, arity, and error-object identity. Other outerHTML edge cases and full cross-realm semantics remain open.

**Windowless body handler IDL follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `HTMLBodyElement` and `HTMLFrameSetElement` handler accessors now return `null` and ignore assignments when their owner document has no browsing context; browsing-context bodies still reflect the handler to Window. This fixes the specialized body/frame-set prototype accessors as well as the generic element handler accessors. A focused Go regression checks windowless `body.onload` remains null, assignment leaves `window.onload` untouched, `body.onclick` stays element-local, and ordinary body `onload` reflection still works. A mutation that removed the browsing-context gate on the specialized setter was caught by the regression. `go test ./test/dom ./test/js ./test/engine -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. The related pinned WPT file still completes without reporting tests after its asynchronous `/interfaces/html.idl` setup; this result is a harness/setup error, not a conformance pass. Broader event-handler and HTML conformance remain incomplete.

**WPT WebIDL resource and async-fetch setup checked 2026-10-27:** the runner now supplies its existing local-origin, redirect-checked, response-size-limited transport to `fetch()`, and its WPT server maps the pinned suite's legacy `/resources/WebIDLParser.js` URL to the checkout's current `/resources/webidl2/lib/webidl2.js` bundle. This allowed the windowless-body WPT to generate and run its actual subtests; the focused eight-file event-handler set now passes 8/8, including the previously empty case. Runner diagnostics for a zero-test completion now include page readiness, harness start/result count, fetch counts, and script errors. The resource alias regression fails with 404 when the alias is mutated away, then passes after restoration; async `fetch().then(response.text()).then(...)` and rejection of an outside-origin request are also covered. `go test ./test/wpt -count=1`, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. This validates the focused event-handler set only; HTML Living Standard coverage remains partial across all other chapters.

**Deferred data-URL classic scripts checked 2026-10-27:** external classic scripts can now load bounded `data:` sources with JavaScript MIME types, percent decoding or base64 decoding, and the normal one-megabyte decoded-source cap. Non-JavaScript data MIME types remain inert; the decoder also bounds the encoded URL before decoding. An engine regression checks deferred data-script execution, base64 content, MIME rejection, and the observed ordering against `DOMContentLoaded-defer.html`; disabling data-URL decoding makes it fail. The pinned `DOMContentLoaded-defer.html` passes. `DOMContentLoaded-defer-task-order.tentative.html` expects the opposite timer ordering and fails; its own test comment states that ordering is unspecified by the standard, so this remains an implementation scheduling difference, not proof of a normative violation. Async/defer task scheduling beyond this case remains incomplete.

**Form collections follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** the live form DOM bridge now exposes `Document.forms` and `HTMLFormElement.elements` with stable collection identity, form-owner association across light and shadow tree scopes, indexed access, named lookup, duplicate-name `RadioNodeList`, and exclusion of image inputs. The pinned `form-elements-matches.html` and `form-elements-nameditem-01.html` pass 2/2. A mutation disabling named-property lookup makes the numeric-name WPT fail and passes again after restoration. The full curated `html/semantics/forms/the-form-element` directory currently passes 8/21, with 11 failures and 2 runner errors; reflected form attributes, form-level named properties, validation, submission, and click behavior remain open. The refreshed pinned parser directory separately stands at 60/62 (the remaining failure is the tentative task-order test noted above). This increment does not establish general form or HTML conformance.

**Form reflection and form-element property follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `HTMLFormElement` now reflects its action, method, encoding, target, name, autocomplete, and no-validate attributes; `Document.URL` and `documentURI` are exposed. Form action uses the document URL when absent or empty and resolves non-empty values against the base URL. Input/select/textarea autocomplete parsing now normalizes and validates the autocomplete token grammar. `Document.getElementsByName()` returns a live NodeList. Form wrappers expose live indexed and named getters, duplicate names share a `RadioNodeList`, built-in collisions resolve to named controls, and past names remain mapped while their control remains associated. Nine pinned WPT files covering autocomplete, action reflection, indexed form properties, collection lookup, and named form properties pass. Mutating the form named getter causes `form-nameditem.html` to fail 11/17 subtests, then it passes 17/17 after restoration. The full form-element directory is now 15/21 (4 failures, 2 runner errors); remaining cases cover `.click()`, `checkValidity()`, declarative-shadow fixture support, and `requestSubmit()`. Go JS/engine tests, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. This remains partial form and HTML conformance.

**Constraint-validation follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `HTMLFormElement.checkValidity()` now evaluates required input, radio, checkbox, select, and textarea controls, skips barred controls (including hidden inputs), dispatches a non-bubbling `invalid` event on each failing control, and returns the aggregate result. `reportValidity()` currently shares this behavior but has no browser validation UI. A Go regression verifies invalid and valid transitions plus `invalid` dispatch; a mutation that forces validation to return true is caught by the test and was restored. The pinned `form-checkvalidity.html` passes. The full form-element directory is now 16/21 (3 failures, 2 errors); `.click()`, `requestSubmit()`, and declarative-shadow fixture support remain open. JS/engine tests, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. This is only an initial constraint-validation subset; the remaining validity states and submission algorithms are still incomplete.

**Form submission and event-listener follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `requestSubmit()` now validates submitter type and form ownership, honors `novalidate`/`formnovalidate`, blocks reentrant submission and validation, dispatches cancelable `submit`, and submits successful controls to a named iframe for GET navigation. `HTMLElement.click()` dispatches click and activates submit buttons. `FormData(form)` now collects successful controls and omits submit buttons unless supplied as submitter; form-associated image inputs have an owner while remaining absent from `form.elements`. `:required`, `:optional`, `:valid`, and `:invalid` matching now covers the required-control cases exercised here. `addEventListener` parses options objects and removes `{once:true}` listeners before invocation. DOM errors now use the runtime's DOMException constructor. The pinned `form-requestsubmit.html` passes all 10 test groups, and `form-attribute-form-owner.html` passes. Mutating the request path to suppress submission causes the requestSubmit WPT to time out/fail, then it passes after restoration. The full form-element directory now passes 18/21 (1 failure, 2 errors); the remaining visible failure is the declarative-shadow fixture's unavailable global `first`, while runner errors remain. JS/engine tests, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. General navigation, POST encoding, full constraint validation, and broad event-option semantics remain incomplete.

**Parser-created Window named properties checked 2026-10-27:** the final visible failure in the form directory was not declarative shadow-root attachment—the parser already creates that root—but the HTML Window named-property binding for IDs discovered after parsing began. `UpgradeParserElement` now registers parser-created IDs, and a Go regression covers that path. The pinned `form-indexed-element-shadow.html` now passes; mutating away parser-time registration reproduces its `ReferenceError: first is not defined`, then it passes after restoration. The full `html/semantics/forms/the-form-element` directory reports 19/21 passed, 0 assertion failures, and 2 runner errors. JS/engine tests, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. The two runner errors remain unclassified, and the broader HTML conformance objective remains open.

**Form navigation and WPT `.sub.html` processing checked 2026-10-27:** `HTMLFormElement.submit()` now performs GET submission into the owning iframe, serializes successful controls, replaces the action query, and uses the document URL for missing/empty `action` while resolving non-empty actions against the document base URL. The WPT local server now expands `{{GET[name]}}` placeholders in `.sub.*` fixtures using HTML-escaped decoded query values; the previous 2/21 runner errors were caused by serving literal `{{GET[action]}}` text. The two `form-action-submission*.html` WPTs pass, and the complete pinned `html/semantics/forms/the-form-element` directory now passes 21/21 with no skips, assertion failures, or runner errors. A mutation disabling `.sub` expansion makes its server regression fail, then passes after restoration. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. Form POST, redirects, browsing-context lifecycle, full form algorithms, and overall HTML Living Standard conformance remain incomplete.

**Form submission coverage and SubmitEvent follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** the default WPT gate now discovers `html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0` and `the-form-element`. Its 31-test submission baseline was 3 passed, 15 failed, and 13 errored; failures cluster around missing SubmitEvent/FormData fields and algorithms, files, POST/navigation ordering, and shadow-tree behavior. `requestSubmit()` now dispatches a `SubmitEvent` whose `submitter` is the requested/clicked control or `null`; the interface constructor and prototype inheritance are exposed, and script-created SubmitEvents can be dispatched through the same subtype path. The pinned `submit-event.html` passes all three subtests. Mutating wrapper delivery to always expose a null submitter makes the new Go regression fail; the implementation was restored. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. The 31-case suite baseline remains largely failing, and form submission plus overall HTML conformance are incomplete.

**FormData events and iframe document URLs checked 2026-10-27:** `FormData(form[, submitter])` now dispatches a bubbling, non-cancelable `FormDataEvent` with the live FormData object, so listener changes are returned to callers and applied to GET submissions. FormData supports ordered entry iteration; `_charset_` values follow the form-data encoding path. GET submission preserves entry order, including entries appended by `formdata` listeners. `Document.URL`, `documentURI`, and `baseURI` now resolve against a loaded iframe's own browsing-context URL/document. In pinned WPT `constructing-form-data-set.html`, 10/13 subtests now pass; the file remains failed on the three `File is not defined` cases. The same WPT also verifies submitted `_charset_`, submit-button exclusion, and listener-appended entries. `submit-event.html` remains 1/1 files passed, and the full `the-form-element` directory remains 21/21. A mutation removing FormDataEvent dispatch is caught by `TestFormDataEventBubblesAndSharesIterableFormData`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt -count=1`, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. File inputs, File/Blob/DataTransfer, POST encodings, and broader HTML conformance remain incomplete.

**File form-data support checked 2026-10-27:** JavaScript now exposes `Blob`, `File`, `FileList`, file-input `files`, and `DataTransfer.files/items.add()`. FormData preserves File object identity through append/set/get/iteration and includes selected file-input values. Blob/File construction is bounded to 64 MiB of content per value. Go regressions cover file metadata, DataTransfer selection, `input.files` identity, and FormData identity; a mutation disabling `input.files` assignment is caught by the regression. Focused WPT `constructing-form-data-set.html` now passes all 13 subtests, and `submit-event.html` passes. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine`, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. The full `html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0` WPT run exceeded its 10-minute test timeout before producing a suite summary; the timeout stack shows a testharness parser script executing through form submission. The suite therefore remains unmeasured after this increment. Broader File/Blob semantics, multipart POST submission, and overall HTML Living Standard conformance remain incomplete.

**Blob/File constructor algorithms and WPT `.any.js` checked 2026-10-27:** Blob/File constructors now consume Web IDL iterable sequences, copy ArrayBuffer and typed-array view bytes before option getters run, stringify non-buffer parts, convert string-part line endings, preserve constructed File names and subclass prototype chains, enforce constructor and dictionary errors, and follow the required options getter order. `Blob.slice()` now uses Web IDL integer rounding and treats omitted/undefined type as empty. The WPT runner discovers `.any.js` files and wraps their Window variant with `testharness.js` plus declared `META: script` dependencies; FileAPI Blob and File directories are now in the curated/default WPT selection. A Float16Array compatibility constructor stores binary16 values in a Uint16Array backing view, and MessageChannel/MessagePort now provide local asynchronous delivery, `onmessage`/listener startup, closure, and same-runtime port transfer with preserved entanglement. On pinned commit `11ae8c1179be2dd38070846b01c2e9338ef81770`, focused `Blob-constructor.any.js`, `Blob-constructor-endings.any.js`, `Blob-slice.any.js`, and `File-constructor.any.js` measure 4/4 files passing (including 73/73 Blob constructor subtests). Blob `text()`, `bytes()`, and `arrayBuffer()` return fresh instances of the runtime Promise class; text uses UTF-8 decoding with replacement for malformed input, `bytes()` resolves to a fresh Uint8Array, and `stream()` returns a byte stream with default and BYOB reader paths; `TextEncoder.encode()` returns a Uint8Array so its output follows the BufferSource path. The pinned `Blob-text.any.js`, `Blob-bytes.any.js`, `Blob-stream.any.js`, and `Blob-newobject.any.js` selection passes 4/4 files; Blob stream coverage includes empty and non-Unicode data, repeated reads, BYOB, and object freshness. A Go regression checks Promise identity and Blob stream reader setup. The `ReadableStream` surface added for Blob streams does not yet implement general underlying-source queuing and pull/cancel algorithms. A mutation disabling BufferSource recognition failed the regression on byte and File content; a mutation dropping transferred ports failed the MessageChannel regression; both passed after restoration. The runner currently measures only the Window variant of `.any.js`; worker-global variants are not yet implemented. Float16Array only has the currently exercised typed-array constructor surface; full resizable/shared-buffer and iterator semantics need broader checks. Multipart POST, broader File API methods, and overall HTML conformance remain incomplete.

**Blob read APIs, FileReader data-isolation coverage, and transferable buffers checked 2026-10-27:** Blob now exposes `textStream()`, returning a fresh `ReadableStream` whose reads yield UTF-8-decoded string data; the pinned `Blob-textStream.any.js` passes 8/8 cases, including empty content, charset ignoring, multipart blobs, and repeated calls. FileReader provides asynchronous `readAsArrayBuffer`, `readAsText`, `readAsDataURL`, `readAsBinaryString`, and abort/event state handling sufficient for the pinned Blob data-isolation fixture. MessagePort transfer now detaches transferred ArrayBuffers and delivers their copied bytes to the receiver; Blob construction ignores detached BufferSources. A regression asserts source detachment and recipient byte preservation. The full pinned `FileAPI/blob` directory measures 12/13 passed, 0 assertion failures, 0 skips, and 1 runner error: `Blob-constructor-endings.html` cannot execute because the harness's `webIDL2` dependency is undefined; the `.any.js` endings test passes. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js` passes. The custom ReadableStream still lacks general source algorithms; FileReader encoding coverage and worker-global variants remain limited, and overall HTML Living Standard conformance is still incomplete.

**Form POST checks, click submissions, and composed SubmitEvent identity refreshed 2026-10-28:** the pinned WPT server executes the bounded `form-submission.py` body checks for URL-encoded, multipart, and text/plain POSTs; image submitters contribute zero-coordinate entries. Listener callbacks expose legacy `window.event`, and a dispatched event reuses the original JS event object in listeners. Form navigation tasks supersede per target frame, and submit-button default actions cancel submissions planned by click handlers only after the handler completes, preserving immediate FormData snapshots when `preventDefault()` is used. The exact two previously failing cases, `form-double-submit-multiple-targets.html` and `form-double-submit-requestsubmit.html`, now pass in a fresh run. Re-running the recorded 21-file selection from `form-submission-0` reports 21 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, and 0 errored at pinned commit `11ae8c1179be2dd38070846b01c2e9338ef81770`; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-form-current-21/results.json`. This selection does not measure the full form-submission directory or general navigation semantics; TASK-055 remains incomplete.

**File upload WPT baseline checked 2026-10-27:** the pinned `FileAPI/file` directory currently measures 3/15 passed, 11 failed, and 1 runner error. Failures cluster around missing multipart FormData/fetch or form submission behavior and file input filename sanitization for control characters. The requested `FileAPI/File` directory name contains no tests in this checkout; the test directory is lowercase `FileAPI/file`. This baseline confirms that File construction and file metadata coverage do not yet amount to file-upload conformance.

**Fetch FormData multipart upload follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `fetch(url, {method, body: formData})` now emits a bounded multipart body with a generated boundary, UTF-8 fields, CRLF-normalized field values, encoded field names and filenames, file bytes, and file content types. The pinned WPT server implements the request-body echo endpoints used by these upload tests, including their escaped response form. File input `.value` now exposes the `C:\\fakepath\\` prefix for selected files, clears selection only when assigned an empty string, and rejects non-empty assignments with `InvalidStateError`. A Go regression covers multipart field/file encoding and the WPT server regression covers raw and escaped echo behavior. The four pinned fetch-based upload files (`send-file-formdata*.any.js`) pass 4/4. The latest full `FileAPI/file` directory run reports 7/15 passing, 1 assertion-failing file, and 7 runner errors; the long-running form-navigation cases do not complete within the harness timeout, and the remaining error is the HTML `webIDL2` harness issue. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/wpt` passes. Form POST navigation and full File API conformance remain incomplete.

**Form POST charset encoding, text/plain iframe response, and navigator.platform follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** multipart form navigation now selects the first supported `accept-charset` label, updates successful `_charset_` controls, and applies the WHATWG HTML encoder to field names, field values, and filenames; unrepresentable characters use numeric character references. ISO-2022-JP output maps yen and overline through the JIS Roman escape state and maps halfwidth middle dot to the JIS X 0208 middle dot. `text/plain` iframe responses now build a plain text document with normalized line endings, so form echo output containing markup-like text and numeric character references is not reparsed as HTML. `navigator.platform` now reports `MacIntel`, matching Goosie's existing macOS user-agent string; this lets the File constructor endings fixture choose its native line ending. Go regressions verify charset marker/fallback encoding, plain-text preservation, and platform reflection; mutating the charset encoder to return UTF-8 makes the charset regression fail. The four targeted upload WPT files (UTF-8, Windows-1252, ISO-2022-JP, x-user-defined) pass 4/4, and a clean pinned full `FileAPI/file` run passes 15/15 with no failures, skips, or runner errors. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/wpt ./test/dom ./test/engine ./cmd/goosie -count=1`, architecture checks, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes the measured FileAPI/file upload directory only; broader File API and overall HTML Living Standard conformance remain incomplete.

**MessagePort algorithm and WPT coverage checked 2026-10-27:** the curated/default WPT gate now includes pinned `webmessaging/message-channels`. MessagePort delivery queues stay disabled until `start()` or `onmessage` is set; adding an event listener alone does not start delivery. `postMessage` accepts a transfer-options dictionary, validates duplicate/closed MessagePort transfer entries, accepts ArrayBuffer transfer entries, and DataCloneError uses DOMException name/code shape. `MessageEvent.ports` is a frozen JavaScript array, and Window exposes `frames`, `length`, and indexed child browsing-context properties. Parent-to-external-iframe transfer, return delivery, and sibling-frame relay have engine regressions; separate runtime tests verify transferred ports preserve queued peer messaging. On pinned WPT commit `11ae8c1179be2dd38070846b01c2e9338ef81770`, the standard 10-file MessageChannel selection measures 9 passed, 1 failed, 0 skipped, 0 errored. The remaining failure is WeakRef/garbage-collected close. The `addEventListener`-starts-port mutation made its regression fail before restoration. Same-runtime message payloads are still not fully structured-cloned; transferred ArrayBuffers are accepted but are not detached. This is partial behavior evidence, not HTML messaging conformance.

**MessagePort listener and IDL follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `MessagePort.prototype.onmessage` is now an accessor-backed IDL attribute, so setting it starts the port without creating an own shadowing property. `addEventListener` deduplicates by callback and capture, supports function and `handleEvent` object listeners, and `removeEventListener` matches callback identity and capture. Go regressions verify prototype descriptors, queued-message startup, and capture-sensitive removal. The pinned `basics.any.js` WPT passes (1/1 file); the refreshed full MessageChannel suite result is recorded in the sibling-frame checkpoint below.

**Sibling-frame MessagePort transfer follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** child global synchronization was re-importing a child’s `onmessage` handler through its parent WindowProxy accessor, replacing the child-realm handler with a wrapper and exposing transferred ports as plain proxies. `syncIFrameGlobals()` now leaves the accessor-backed `onmessage` slot alone; the WindowProxy getter already reads the child value. A regression transfers both channel endpoints to separate sibling iframe runtimes and checks MessagePort prototype identity plus the relayed payload. That regression and the pinned `cross-document.html` pass.

**MessagePort user-activation metadata checked 2026-10-27:** transfer options with `includeUserActivation: true` now attach an inactive snapshot (`isActive` and `hasBeenActive` are false) to the queued MessageEvent when the runtime has no transient or sticky activation. Messages without the option expose `userActivation: null`. A Go regression verifies both paths; the pinned tentative `user-activation.tentative.any.js` passes.

**Blob-backed Worker and MessageChannel behavior checked 2026-10-27:** runtimes now expose `URL.createObjectURL()` and `revokeObjectURL()` for Blob values, with per-document object URL count and byte caps and cleanup on runtime close. When no host WorkerDialer is supplied, `Worker` uses an isolated in-process JS runtime for Blob URL scripts. Worker messages preserve primitive structured-clone types, queued startup messages are delivered after worker initialization, parent termination suppresses queued messages, and worker `close()` finishes the current task while preventing subsequent MessagePort tasks from running. Go coverage exercises a Blob worker that closes itself, creates a MessageChannel, and posts boolean `true`. The pinned `worker.any.js` and `worker-post-after-close.any.js` WPTs both pass; the refreshed 10-file MessageChannel selection reports 9/10 passing, with only WeakRef-based garbage-collected close failing. This implementation does not yet fetch external worker scripts or support full structured cloning, worker port transfers, SharedWorker, or complete worker event-loop semantics.

**MessagePort payload snapshot follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** same-runtime `MessagePort.postMessage()` now clones ordinary JSON-compatible payloads when queued, so receiver data is independent from sender references and reflects the value at post time. Transfer-listed ArrayBuffers retain their separate transfer path. The Go regression checks nested object identity and verifies that later sender mutations do not affect the queued message. Changing the clone condition back to cross-realm-only made the regression fail on both object identity and mutation timing; restoring same-runtime cloning made it pass. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. A fresh pinned 10-file MessageChannel WPT run remains 9/10: all 9 non-tentative files pass, and the sole tentative failure is `WeakRef is not defined` in the garbage-collected close-event test. Circular values, platform objects, full structured-clone transfer semantics, and WeakRef/GC support remain open.

**Gap audit against this document's historical tables checked 2026-10-27, IndexedDB status refreshed 2026-10-29:** many entries in sections 1.1-1.6 and the phase task list describe work the current tree already contains, so those tables are historical inventories, not open-gap lists. Verified present and covered by code plus tests at this audit: `:is()`/`:where()` parsing, matching, and specificity (`internal/css/selector.go`); `@layer` parsing and cascade layer precedence (`internal/css/parser.go` `parseLayerRule`, `cascadeEntry` `layerOrder`); `::first-line` (GOOSIE-PSEUDO-001 closed; `internal/style/firstline.go`, `internal/layout/inline.go` `applyFirstLineRuns`); `@container` queries and container units (`internal/css/parser.go` `parseContainerRule`, `css/calc.go`); `flex-wrap`/`wrap-reverse` multi-line flex (`internal/layout/block.go`); `aspect-ratio` parsing and layout application (`internal/style/style.go` `parseAspectRatio`, `internal/layout/block.go`); `min-content`/`max-content`/`fit-content` intrinsic sizing (flex, grid, and table paths); Range/Selection (`internal/js/range.go`); TreeWalker/NodeIterator (`internal/js/treewalker.go`); DOMParser including XML types (`internal/js/domparser.go`); BroadcastChannel (`internal/js/broadcast.go`); Web Animations `element.animate` (`internal/js/animations.go`); Canvas 2D drawing ops with pixel readback (`internal/js/canvas.go`); Popover API with auto/manual, light dismiss, and invoker wiring (`internal/engine/popover.go`); dialog `showModal`/focus handling; details/summary activation; CSS Grid and CSS Animations enabled in the WPT curator (`test/wpt/curate.go` `CSSGrid`/`CSSAnimations` true); and `removeEventListener` closure matching via keyed listeners (`dom.RemoveEventListenerByKey` plus `fnVal` identity on window listeners), closing GOOSIE-RMLISTENER-001 as recorded. Remaining genuine gaps at this audit: IndexedDB database APIs beyond the tested IDBFactory `cmp()` and `IDBKeyRange` key primitives, text shaping (complex scripts), full UAX #9 depth, full nested-contenteditable editing, XML-serialization conformance beyond DOMParser, and the testing-infrastructure expansions in section 7.

**Microdata DOM API checked 2026-10-27:** `itemscope` (boolean), `itemtype`, `itemid`, `itemprop`, `itemref` now reflect as IDL properties in both the content-attribute and camelCase spellings, installed per element wrapper; `element.properties` returns a live HTMLPropertiesCollection-like surface (`length`, `item(i)`, indexed access, `names`) that walks descendants in tree order without descending into nested items; `document.getItems(type)` returns top-level items whose `itemtype` tokens include every requested type, all types when the argument is empty. Seven Go regressions cover reflection reads/writes, collection liveness across DOM mutation, nested-item scoping, getItems filtering, and read-only `properties`. `go test ./test/js -count=1`, `go test ./internal/js -count=1`, and `go test ./... -count=1` pass (the pre-existing `test/qc` traceability golden mismatch from the uncommitted in-progress tree is unchanged by this increment), plus `go vet -all ./internal/js`, architecture checks, and `gofmt` clean. This is a functional Microdata subset; the full property-collection spec (DOMStringList `names` interface shape, `itemValue` algorithm) remains open.

**Drag and Drop API checked 2026-10-27:** `DataTransfer` is now a real data store with insertion-ordered `types` (frozen array), case-insensitive formats with the `text`→`text/plain` legacy alias, validated `dropEffect`/`effectAllowed`, and a no-op `setDragImage`; `DataTransferItemList` handles both File entries (existing behavior preserved) and string entries, with `item(i)`, `remove(i)`, `clear()`, and a combined `length`. `element.draggable` reflects through a `dom.IsDraggable` resolver honoring the img/`a[href]` auto defaults and explicit `draggable="false"`. The engine gains a programmatic drag pipeline (`Session.StartDrag/DragTo/EndDrag/CancelDrag` in `internal/engine/drag.go`, guarded by `opMu`): hit-test requires `IsDraggable`, canceled `dragstart` starts no drag, each move fires `dragleave`→`dragenter`→`dragover`→`drag`, drop is valid only when the target's `dragover` was canceled or the target is draggable, and `drop` (valid) then `dragend` close the sequence. Each event has its own DataTransfer object backed by the operation's shared store; later event handlers see data written at `dragstart`, while saved event objects are disassociated after dispatch. The selected Go regressions cover store semantics, effect validation, reflection, full event ordering, target validation, cancellation, and cross-event data transfer. Drag image rendering and OS-integration dragging remain out of scope for the headless engine.

**Basic Unicode bidi checked 2026-10-27:** ComputedStyle now stores `unicode-bidi` (`normal`, `embed`, `isolate`, `bidi-override`, `plaintext`), not inherited. `internal/layout/bidi.go` classifies Hebrew/Arabic blocks plus Arabic-Indic digits as level 1 and European/full-width digits as level 0 even in RTL base, merges maximal same-level runs, supports `bidi-override` forcing, and derives the paragraph level from the first strong character under `plaintext`. Inline line placement now reorders word runs for RTL containers through this resolver, flipping to logical order when `plaintext` resolves LTR. Verified: pure LTR unchanged, pure RTL reversed, digits keep logical LTR order in RTL context, Hebrew words flow right-to-left, and `TestRTLWordPlacement` now asserts the bidi-correct behavior. `internal/layout/bidi_test.go` and `test/layout/bidi_test.go` cover classification, run tiling, plaintext levels, override, and session-level placement; `test/style/unicode_bidi_test.go` pins property parsing and defaults. `go test ./internal/layout ./internal/style ./test/layout ./test/style -count=1`, `go vet -all ./internal/layout ./internal/style`, and `gofmt` pass. Limitations recorded in code: reorder is word-level (glyphs inside one RTL run paint in logical order — full RTL glyph rendering needs paint-path shaping), simplified L2 (an LTR stretch keeps its logical position instead of re-embedding a level deeper), and range-based classification covers Hebrew/Arabic only (Thaana, Syriac, NKo classify as LTR).

**XMLSerializer conformance follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** XML serialization now performs namespace fixup for HTML/SVG/XML nodes, resets default namespaces, chooses or generates prefixes for namespaced attributes, preserves doctype public/system identifiers, emits XML empty-element syntax, and exposes the serializer method on the shared interface prototype. XML parsing now records namespace scopes and doctype identifiers; detached `Document`/`DocumentFragment` construction and `createElementNS`/`createAttribute` paths cover the corresponding WPT cases. The pinned `domparsing/XMLSerializer-serializeToString.html` passes 33/33 subtests. `go test ./test/js`, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, and scoped `go vet -all` pass. Disabling the empty-element serialization branch makes `TestXMLSerializerSelfClosingXML` fail; the implementation was restored and the test passes. This closes that WPT file only; the broader DOM Parsing corpus and overall HTML conformance remain open.

**DOM node transfer follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** active and detached documents now expose `importNode()` and `adoptNode()` through shared bindings. Import creates a shallow clone by default or a deep clone when requested, and rejects a `Document` with `NotSupportedError`; adoption removes the original node from its parent, preserves JS wrapper identity, and changes its owner document. Detached DOMParser/createHTMLDocument results also expose `rootElement`, which this pinned DOMParser scripting-support WPT fixture uses. Go regressions cover shallow/deep import, ownership, identity, removal, and the document rejection. The pinned `domparsing/DOMParser-parseFromString-xml-scripting-support-disabled.html` passes all 13 subtests. This does not establish general DOM transfer, complete script execution semantics, or HTML conformance. A refreshed broad `domparsing` result is recorded below.

**Range contextual-fragment follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** `Range.createContextualFragment()` now selects an element context from the Range start boundary (falling back to the document body), parses within that context, and returns a fragment owned by that document. Detached parser documents mark their scripts already started; clones preserve the script one-shot state, while a fresh classic inline script from a contextual fragment runs only when the fragment is inserted into the active document. `Node.isEqualNode()` and `isSameNode()` are exposed, and the fragment insertion path now visits the nodes that actually moved out of the fragment. The pinned `domparsing/createContextualFragment.html` WPT passes all 35 subtests, including owner-document equality and script activation. This closes that HTML fixture only; XHTML contextual parsing and the rest of Range/DOM Parsing conformance remain open.

**HTML markup setter and insertion errors checked 2026-10-27:** Element `innerHTML` and `outerHTML` setters now convert `null` to the empty string while preserving normal string conversion for other values. `insertAdjacentHTML()` now throws `SyntaxError` for an unsupported position and `NoModificationAllowedError` when `beforebegin`/`afterend` targets a root whose parent cannot be modified. A Go regression covers clearing via both setters; the pinned `innerhtml-07.html`, `outerhtml-02.html`, and `insert-adjacent-parent-errors.html` WPTs all pass.

**`domparsing` suite refreshed after node transfer, contextual fragments, and markup setter/error fixes, 2026-10-27:** the pinned full directory run reports 30/78 passing, 33 failing, 15 errored, 0 skipped (38.5%). The non-tentative files are 29/43 passing (9 failing, 5 errored); tentative remains 1/35 (24 failing, 10 errored). This is up from the prior 25/78 checkpoint. Passing checks include DOMParser scripting support (13 subtests), `Range.createContextualFragment()` (35), markup null conversion, and adjacent-insertion errors. This partial directory result is far below TASK-055 acceptance; streaming markup, XHTML parsing, and many DOM Parsing edge cases remain open.

**Disconnected subtree selector follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** selector queries rooted at a disconnected element now walk that subtree instead of treating a miss in the owner document's indexes as final. A Go regression parses adjacent list items into a detached element and verifies the root and both list-item descendants; the pinned `domparsing/innerhtml-li-autoclosing.html` WPT now passes all 7 subtests. This fixture's failures came from detached-root query lookup, and did not require changing the list-item tree builder. A broad `domparsing` refresh after this fix is pending below.

**OuterHTML parent handling checked 2026-10-27:** `outerHTML` now throws `NoModificationAllowedError` when asked to replace an element whose parent is the `Document`, while an element whose parent is a `DocumentFragment` parses with a temporary HTML body context and replaces the original. A Go regression covers both cases; pinned `outerhtml-01.html` and `outerhtml-03.html` pass.

**HTML root fragment handling checked 2026-10-27:** `insertAdjacentHTML()` now uses a body-mode fragment context when insertion is adjacent to an HTML `<html>` root, avoiding implicit duplicate `<head>`/`<body>` elements; the pinned `insert_adjacent_html.html` WPT passes 31/31. Clearing `innerHTML` on an HTML element leaves its required body child, verified by a Go regression and the pinned `innerhtml-empty-document-element.html` WPT. The updated broad-suite result is pending below.

**Inline CSSOM serialization checked 2026-10-27:** `CSSStyleDeclaration.cssText` now serializes parsed declarations with canonical spacing and a trailing semicolon; invalid color declarations are absent from CSSOM reads while the source `style` attribute stays unchanged. Go regressions and the pinned `style_attribute_html.html` WPT pass all four subtests. Broader CSSOM parsing and mutation conformance remain open.

**`domparsing` refreshed after HTML-root, outerHTML, and inline CSSOM fixes, 2026-10-27:** the pinned full directory run reports 36/78 passing, 27 failing, 15 errored, 0 skipped (46.2%). Non-tentative files are 35/43 passing (3 failing, 5 errored); tentative remains 1/35 (24 failing, 10 errored). The new green files include `style_attribute_html.html`, `innerhtml-empty-document-element.html`, `insert_adjacent_html.html`, and the outerHTML parent cases. This run was started before the latest SVG-template namespace fix, which is being verified separately.

**`domparsing` refreshed after SVG template namespace fix, 2026-10-27:** the pinned full directory run now reports 37/78 passing, 26 failing, 15 errored, 0 skipped (47.4%). Non-tentative files are 36/43 passing (2 failing, 5 errored); tentative remains 1/35. `svg-template.html` passes after HTML template content behavior was restricted to HTML-namespace templates. The remaining suite is far below full HTML Living Standard acceptance; the subsequent URL path serialization change is being checked against its MXSS WPT separately.

**URL path serialization and `.sub.html` WPT expansion checked 2026-10-27:** resolved URLs now percent-encode Unicode path bytes and treat a Unicode-leading first path segment containing a colon as a relative path. The WPT server expands `{{host}}` and `{{ports[http][0]}}` in `.sub.` resources to its loopback origin, enabling the MXSS test's origin expectation to execute correctly. A Go URL regression and server-template regression pass; pinned `innerhtml-mxss.sub.html` passes all 60 subtests. A broad refresh after this change is pending below.

**`domparsing` refreshed after URL and `.sub.` fixes, 2026-10-27:** the pinned directory run reports 38/78 passing, 25 failing, 15 errored, 0 skipped (48.7%). The non-tentative cases are 37/43 passing (1 failure, 5 errors); tentative cases are 1/35 (24 failures, 10 errors). `innerhtml-mxss.sub.html` is now green. The one failing non-tentative file is `innerhtml-01.xhtml`; the five errors are XHTML files that the WPT harness currently sends through the HTML parser, so XHTML script/CDATA behavior prevents testharness from reporting tests. Most remaining failures are tentative streaming-markup APIs. Focused Go tests for `test/js`, `test/dom`, and `test/wpt`, `internal/archtest`, scoped `go vet -all ./internal/js ./internal/dom ./test/js ./test/dom ./test/wpt`, and `git diff --check` pass. This is a directory-specific checkpoint; XHTML runner support, streaming-markup APIs, and the rest of HTML Living Standard conformance remain open.

**XHTML/XML parsing and serialization follow-up checked 2026-10-27:** the WPT server now serves `.xhtml`/`.xht` with `application/xhtml+xml`; the WPT harness uses a bounded XML parser and supplies its parsed document to the engine instead of HTML-reparsing it. XML limits are checked during strict tokenization before tree construction, and the engine rechecks the supplied tree against document limits. XHTML `innerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, and Range contextual fragments now use XML fragment parsing; XML `innerHTML` serialization reports invalid XML state; active document title reflects the title element; processing instructions survive XML parsing and serialize with node/data interface support; XML doctype identifiers retain their literal quote characters. The XHTML data-URL iframe fixture is decoded with byte caps and MIME-aware parsing. All six focused XHTML WPT files pass (6/6). A mutation that restored XML escaping for doctype public identifiers made `TestXMLSerializerProcessingInstructionsAndDoctypeQuotes` fail; restoring literal identifier output made it pass. The latest pinned full `domparsing` run reports 44/78 passing, 24 failing, 10 errored, 0 skipped (56.4%); all 43 non-tentative cases pass, while tentative coverage remains 1/35 passing (24 failures, 10 errors), primarily streaming-markup APIs. `go test ./test/js ./test/dom ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/`, focused WPT harness tests, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes only the measured XHTML/XML cluster; TASK-055 acceptance remains unmet.

**`domparsing` refreshed after disconnected-root selector fix, 2026-10-27:** the pinned directory run reports 31/78 passing, 32 failing, 15 errored, 0 skipped (39.7%). Non-tentative files are 30/43 passing (8 failing, 5 errored); tentative remains 1/35 (24 failing, 10 errored). This is up from 30/78 and includes the now-green adjacent-list-item fixture. This run used a binary built before the outerHTML parent fix, so the two focused outerHTML WPT passes are not reflected in this count. The full Living Standard acceptance remains unmet.

## 0. Initial Inventory (Historical)

This table records the plan’s pre-implementation snapshot. It is not the current status; several listed gaps have since received in-progress code and tests. Re-audit each row before using it to plan implementation.

| Layer | What exists | What's missing |
|-------|-------------|----------------|
| **HTML Parser** | ~100 element types, void elements, SVG namespace, template/shadow DOM, bounded parsing | No formal insertion mode state machine, no adoption agency algorithm, no `document.write`, no `noscript`/`iframe`/`noframes` special handling, no active formatting elements list, no MathML namespace |
| **DOM Core** | Node tree mutation, query APIs, events (capture/bubble), Shadow DOM, Custom Elements | No `LiveNodeList`, `childNodes` returns snapshot, no `document.write/writeln`, no encoding detection |
| **JS Bindings** | 30+ Web APIs (fetch, XHR, WebSocket, Worker, Storage, Observers, Canvas stub, etc.) | No element interface prototypes (TASK-052), no IDL reflection, no `Image` constructor, `className` is dead snapshot, `removeEventListener` cannot match closures |
| **Layout** | Block, inline, float (inside block), flex (single-line, inside block), grid (subset, inside block), table (inside block), positioning | Flex/grid/table are branches inside `block.go`, not independent formatting contexts; no multi-line flex; no text shaping/bidi |
| **CSS** | Cascade, selectors (incl. `:has()`), custom properties, `calc()`, `var()`, modern color spaces, gradients, transitions, transforms, filters, `@font-face`/`@keyframes`/`@media`/`@supports` | No `::first-line`, CSS Grid marked `false` in WPT, CSS Animations marked `false` |
| **WPT Coverage** | 97.6% pass on `css/css-color` + `css/selectors` (457/468) | All `testharness.js` tests skipped; Canvas/SVG/WebGL/Grid/Animations/Gradients/Pseudo-elements all disabled |
| **Render Parity** | 96.09% avg pixel match on 80 fixtures (macOS) | Font substitution accounts for ~83% of gaps; 14 CI fixtures fail |

## 1. Initial Gap Analysis (Historical)

The rows below are the original gaps recorded before the current in-progress changes. They are leads for the requirement audit, not verified current defects or a complete inventory.

### 1.0 Living Standard Coverage Map

This is the top-level checklist for full scope. It follows the current standard’s chapter structure; each chapter must be expanded into individual user-agent requirements, implementation owners, and WPT/manual evidence before that area can be called complete.

| HTML Living Standard chapter | Required coverage | Current evidence status |
|---|---|---|
| Common infrastructure | URL/encoding primitives, origins, policy containers, event loops, tasks and microtasks, queues, timers, structured data | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| Semantics, structure, and APIs of HTML documents | Document lifecycle, metadata, base URL, navigation-facing document APIs, custom data, document structure | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| The elements of HTML | Every conforming element, content model, reflected IDL, algorithms, events, form association, rendered behavior | Partial; element-by-element audit needed |
| Microdata | DOM extraction algorithms and item/property relationships | Audit and implementation needed |
| User interaction | Focus, activation behavior, editing, drag-and-drop, selection, popovers, dialogs, user-agent input integration | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| Loading web pages | Fetch integration, navigation, history, document readiness, resource loading, page lifecycle, policy checks | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| Web application APIs | APIs defined by HTML, including canvas contexts, messaging, workers/worklets, storage integration | Partial; API-by-API audit needed |
| Communication | Cross-document and cross-context messaging algorithms and origin checks | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| Web workers | Worker creation, script loading, lifecycle, messaging, termination, worker global scopes | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| Worklets | Worklet global scopes, module loading, lifecycle integration | Audit and implementation needed |
| Web storage | `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`, storage events, persistence, origin scoping | Partial; requirement audit needed |
| The HTML syntax | Byte-stream decoding, preprocessing, tokenization, tree construction, scripting hooks, serialization, error recovery | Partial; parser conformance not established |
| The XML syntax | XML/XHTML parsing and serialization for applicable MIME types | Audit and implementation needed |
| Rendering | User-agent rendering requirements for HTML elements and states integrated with CSS/layout/paint | Partial; parity corpus is not conformance proof |
| Obsolete features | Required legacy parsing/rendering behavior that remains specified for user agents | Audit and implementation needed |

“Partial” is not a completion state. A chapter closes only when its requirement inventory is linked to passing automated or documented manual checks and all applicable failures are resolved or proven non-applicable by the standard.

### 1.1 HTML Parsing (HIGH — affects every page)

| Gap | Spec reference | Impact | Effort |
|-----|---------------|--------|--------|
| No formal insertion mode state machine | HTML5 §13.2.6 | Misnested content in tables, selects, forms produces wrong tree | L |
| No adoption agency algorithm | HTML5 §13.2.6.2 | `<b>...<i>...</b>...</i>` produces wrong DOM | M |
| No active formatting elements list | HTML5 §13.2.6 | Formatting reconstruction broken for misnested tags | M |
| No "in select" insertion mode | HTML5 §13.2.6.4.10 | `<select>` content model violations silently accepted | S |
| No "in table" / "in table body" / "in row" / "in cell" modes | HTML5 §13.2.6.4 | Table foster parenting works but edge cases differ from spec | M |
| No `document.write()` / `document.writeln()` | HTML5 §13.2 | Legacy pages using `document.write` break entirely | M |
| No `noscript` handling | HTML5 §13.2.6.4.7 | Scripting-enabled vs disabled parsing not differentiated | S |
| No `iframe`/`noembed`/`noframes` special handling | HTML5 §13.2.6.4 | These elements' content parsed instead of ignored | S |
| No form association during parsing | HTML5 §13.2.6.4 | Form elements not associated with form owner at parse time | S |
| No MathML namespace handling | HTML5 §13.2.6.4 | MathML elements parsed but namespace not set correctly | S |
| No character reference handling in RCDATA | HTML5 §13.2.6.4 | `<title>`, `<textarea>` don't decode entities | S |

### 1.2 DOM APIs (HIGH — affects JS libraries)

| Gap | Spec reference | Impact | Effort |
|-----|---------------|--------|--------|
| No element interface prototypes | DOM §4 / HTML §5 | `el instanceof HTMLElement` fails; all element objects are plain JS objects | L (TASK-052) |
| No IDL attribute reflection | HTML §2.3 | `a.href`, `img.src`, `div.className` don't work as specified | L (TASK-052) |
| No `Image` constructor | HTML §4.8.4.4 | `new Image()` throws ReferenceError | M (TASK-052) |
| `childNodes` returns snapshot, not live NodeList | DOM §3.3 | Code expecting live lists gets stale results | M |
| No `HTMLCollection` live interface | DOM §3.4 | `getElementsByTagName` returns snapshot | M |
| No `NamedNodeMap` for `attributes` | DOM §3.4 | `element.attributes` likely returns plain object | S |
| No `document.write/writeln` | HTML5 §13.2 | Legacy pages break | M |
| No `Range` / `Selection` API | DOM §3.6-3.7 | Text selection, contenteditable, many libraries depend on this | L |
| No `TreeWalker` / `NodeIterator` | DOM §3.5 | Some libraries iterate DOM this way | S |
| No `DOMParser` | DOM §4 | `new DOMParser().parseFromString()` not available | S |
| No `XMLSerializer` | DOM §4 | `new XMLSerializer().serializeToString()` not available | S |
| `removeEventListener` cannot match closures | DOM §3.2 | Listeners added with anonymous functions can't be removed | M |
| No `dataset` full DOMStringMap behavior | HTML §3.2 | Partial implementation via Proxy; edge cases may differ | S |
| No `scrollIntoView`, `scrollTop`, `scrollLeft` | CSSOM View | Scroll position APIs missing | M |
| No `getBoundingClientRect`, `getClientRects` | CSSOM View | Layout measurement APIs missing | M |
| No `Element.closest()` ancestor search | DOM §4.3 | Already implemented (TASK-051) | — |
| No `MutationRecord.oldValue` / `attributeNamespace` | DOM §3.3 | MutationObserver incomplete | S |

### 1.3 HTML Elements and Attributes (MEDIUM — affects specific pages)

| Gap | Spec reference | Impact | Effort |
|-----|---------------|--------|--------|
| No `<canvas>` 2D rendering | HTML §4.12.4 | Canvas API is a stub | L |
| No `<video>`/`<audio>` playback | HTML §4.8 | Media elements parsed but no playback | L |
| No `<details>`/`<summary>` toggle behavior | HTML §4.11.1 | Element exists but may not toggle correctly | S |
| No `<dialog>` return value propagation | HTML §4.12.3 | Dialog exists but return value may not propagate to opener | S |
| No `<slot>` distribution | HTML §4.8 | Shadow DOM slots may not distribute content | M |
| Partial `<output>` interface and reset behavior | HTML §4.10.12 | Value/default-value state, labels, and form reset semantics follow the output algorithms; referenced controls are not automatically computed by the UA | S |
| No `<meter>`/`<progress>` rendering | HTML §4.10.18-19 | Elements exist but no visual rendering | M |
| No `contenteditable` attribute | HTML §4.6.3 | Rich text editing not supported | L |
| No `draggable` / drag-and-drop API | HTML §7.6 | Drag events exist but full DnD API incomplete | M |
| No `<map>`/`<area>` image map hit testing | HTML §4.8.13 | Image maps parsed but not interactive | S |
| No `<track>` for media subtitles | HTML §4.8.6 | Track element parsed but no subtitle rendering | S |
| No microdata / RDFa / JSON-LD processing | HTML §3.4 | Structured data not extracted | S |

### 1.4 CSS Gaps Affecting HTML5 Rendering (MEDIUM)

| Gap | Spec reference | Impact | Effort |
|-----|---------------|--------|--------|
| No `::first-line` pseudo-element | CSS §7.2 | First-line styling broken (GOOSIE-PSEUDO-001) | M |
| CSS Grid not enabled in WPT | CSS Grid L2 | Grid support exists but not validated | M |
| CSS Animations not enabled | CSS Animations L1 | `@keyframes` parsed but animation not validated via WPT | M |
| No `::backdrop` pseudo-element | CSS Positioned L4 | `<dialog>` backdrop not styled | S |
| No `:focus-visible` heuristics | CSS UI L4 | Implemented but may not match Chromium's behavior | S |
| No `:is()` / `:where()` selector | CSS Selectors L4 | Forgiving selector list not supported | M |
| No `:has()` with complex arguments | CSS Selectors L4 | `:has()` implemented but may not handle all combinator cases | S |
| No container queries | CSS Contain L3 | `@container` not supported | L |
| No `@layer` cascade layers | CSS Cascade L5 | Layer ordering not supported | M |
| No `@scope` | CSS Cascade L6 | Scoped styles not supported | M |
| No scroll-driven animations | CSS Scroll-driven Animations | `animation-timeline: scroll()` not supported | L |

### 1.5 Layout and Rendering Gaps (MEDIUM-HIGH)

| Gap | Spec reference | Impact | Effort |
|-----|---------------|--------|--------|
| Flex/grid/table not independent formatting contexts | CSS Sizing L3 | Cannot fix layout issues in isolation | L |
| No multi-line flexbox | CSS Flexbox L1 | `flex-wrap: wrap` not supported | M |
| No text shaping (HarfBuzz/ICU) | Unicode | Complex scripts (Arabic, Devanagari, Thai) render incorrectly | L |
| No bidi algorithm | Unicode §3 | Bidirectional text not supported | L |
| No intrinsic sizing (`min-content`, `max-content`, `fit-content`) | CSS Sizing L3 | Width/height keywords not supported | M |
| No `aspect-ratio` | CSS Sizing L4 | Aspect ratio constraint not supported | S |
| No `container-type` | CSS Contain L3 | Container queries prerequisite missing | M |
| No `content-visibility: auto` | CSS Contain L3 | Render optimization missing | M |
| No `will-change` | CSS Will Change | Compositing hints not supported | S |
| No subpixel positioning | CSS Display L3 | Text and layout may have rounding artifacts | M |

### 1.6 Web APIs Beyond DOM (MEDIUM)

| Gap | Spec reference | Impact | Effort |
|-----|---------------|--------|--------|
| No `IntersectionObserver` root margin | Intersection Observer | Already implemented but may not handle all margin syntax | S |
| No `ResizeObserver` border-box/content-box | Resize Observer | Box model selection may not match spec | S |
| Partial `BroadcastChannel` | HTML §9.5 | Basic channel messaging exists; origin scoping and sender-origin reporting are covered, but structured cloning and storage-key partitioning remain partial | S |
| No `SharedWorker` | Web Workers | Shared worker not supported | M |
| No `Payment Request API` | Payment Request | E-commerce checkout not supported | L |
| No `Web Animations API` | Web Animations | Programmatic animation control missing | L |
| No `Web Components` full lifecycle | Custom Elements L1 | `connectedCallback` etc. may not fire at correct times | M |
| No `Popover API` full behavior | HTML §4.12.5 | Popover exists but light-dismiss and nesting may differ | M |
| Partial `navigator.clipboard` | Clipboard API | App pages transfer one `ClipboardItem` with direct or promised `text/plain`/`text/html` Blob representations after secure-context and transient-activation checks; permission-query/prompt integration, multiple items, and broader formats remain missing | M |
| No `navigator.geolocation` | Geolocation | Location API not supported | S |
| Partial `navigator.permissions` | Permissions | Clipboard `query()` exposes per-origin `clipboard-read`/`clipboard-write` state; other permission names, status events, and full Permissions API behavior remain unsupported | S |
| No `navigator.mediaDevices` | Media Capture | Camera/microphone not supported | L |
| Partial `IndexedDB` | IndexedDB | `indexedDB.cmp()` and the IDBFactory singleton are covered; opening and persisting databases, requests, object stores, indexes, and transactions remain unsupported | L |
| No `Cache API` / Service Worker fetch | Service Worker | Offline support missing | L |
