## 2. Implementation Phases

### Phase 1: Foundation (TASK-052 completion + parser hardening)

**Goal:** Fix the two highest-impact gaps — element interfaces and parser correctness.

#### Task 1.1: Complete TASK-052 — Element Interfaces
- **File:** `internal/js/js.go`, new `internal/js/prototypes.go`
- **What:** Install prototype chain `HTMLElement.prototype` → `Element.prototype` → `Node.prototype` → `Object.prototype`, with per-tag interfaces (`HTMLAnchorElement`, `HTMLImageElement`, `HTMLInputElement`, etc.)
- **IDL reflection rules:**
  - `reflect` (DOMString): getter returns attribute or `""`, setter calls `setAttribute`
  - `reflect boolean`: getter returns `hasAttribute`, setter calls `setAttribute`/`removeAttribute`
  - `reflect long`: getter parses attribute as integer with default, setter serializes
  - `reflect limited to only known values`: getter returns attribute if in allowed set else default, setter validates
  - `reflect URL`: getter resolves against document base, setter serializes
- **Priority interfaces (by live-site frequency):**
  1. `HTMLElement` — `hidden`, `title`, `lang`, `dir`, `tabIndex`, `contentEditable`, `draggable`, `spellcheck`, `translate`
  2. `HTMLAnchorElement` — `href`, `hostname`, `pathname`, `search`, `hash`, `origin`, `protocol`, `port`, `target`, `rel`, `relList`, `type`, `text`, `download`, `hreflang`, `referrerPolicy`, `ping`
  3. `HTMLImageElement` — `src`, `srcset`, `sizes`, `alt`, `width`, `height`, `naturalWidth`, `naturalHeight`, `complete`, `currentSrc`, `loading`, `decoding`, `crossOrigin`, `useMap`, `isMap`, `name`, `referrerPolicy`, `x`, `y`, `decode()`
  4. `HTMLInputElement` — `value` (with dirty flag), `defaultValue`, `type`, `name`, `disabled`, `readonly`, `required`, `checked`, `defaultChecked`, `indeterminate`, `placeholder`, `min`, `max`, `step`, `pattern`, `selectionStart`, `selectionEnd`, `selectionDirection`, `setSelectionRange()`, `files`, `form`, `labels`, `validity`, `validationMessage`, `willValidate`, `checkValidity()`, `reportValidity()`, `stepUp()`, `stepDown()`
  5. `HTMLFormElement` — `action`, `method`, `enctype`, `target`, `elements`, `length`, `submit()`, `reset()`, `checkValidity()`, `reportValidity()`
  6. `HTMLTextAreaElement` — `value` (with dirty flag), `defaultValue`, `rows`, `cols`, `wrap`, `placeholder`, `selectionStart`, `selectionEnd`, `setSelectionRange()`
  7. `HTMLSelectElement` — `value`, `selectedIndex`, `selectedOptions`, `options`, `length`, core `multiple`/`size`, required `valueMissing` validity, `checkValidity()`, `reportValidity()`, `setCustomValidity()`, `add()`, `remove()`, `item()`, and `namedItem()` now have subset coverage; exhaustive WebIDL behavior and selection notification timing remain open.
  8. `HTMLButtonElement` — `type`, `value`, `disabled`, `form`, `formAction`, `formMethod`, `formTarget`, `formEnctype`, `formNoValidate`
  9. `HTMLLinkElement` — `href`, `rel`, `type`, `media`, `crossOrigin`, `disabled`, `sizes`, `hreflang`, `as`, `fetchPriority`
  10. `HTMLScriptElement` — `src`, `type`, `async`, `defer`, `crossOrigin`, `integrity`, `noModule`, `nonce`, `text`
  11. `HTMLMetaElement` — `name`, `content`, `httpEquiv`, `charset`
  12. `HTMLStyleElement` — `media`, `type`, `disabled`
  13. `HTMLCanvasElement` — `width`, `height`, `getContext()` (already exists)
  14. `HTMLVideoElement` / `HTMLAudioElement` — basic properties
  15. `HTMLTableElement` and related — `rows`, `cells`, `insertRow()`, `deleteRow()`, `insertCell()`
  16. `HTMLDivElement`, `HTMLSpanElement`, `HTMLParagraphElement`, `HTMLHeadingElement` — minimal (just prototype chain presence)
  17. `HTMLOListElement`, `HTMLUListElement`, `HTMLLIElement` — `start`, `reversed`, `type`, `value`
  18. `HTMLDialogElement` — already has `show/showModal/close/open/returnValue`
  19. `HTMLDetailsElement` — `open`
  20. `HTMLSlotElement` — `name`, `assignedNodes()`, `assignedElements()`
- **`Image` constructor:** `new Image()`, `new Image(width, height)`, with `src` triggering decode, `complete`/`naturalWidth`/`naturalHeight`/`currentSrc` reflecting load state, `onload`/`onerror` events, `decode()` returning promise. Must pass same admission caps as parsed `<img>`.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `Object.getPrototypeOf(document.createElement('div')) !== Object.prototype`
  - `el instanceof HTMLElement` holds for parsed and created elements
  - `a.href` resolves against document base (measure against Chromium for `/p?q=1`, `./x`, `#f`, `data:…`, unparseable)
  - `className` write reaches `getAttribute('class')` and vice versa (both directions)
  - `input.value` has dirty flag (property ≠ attribute after user edit)
  - Form semantics from TASK-030/031/037 unchanged
  - `Image` admission caps tested (loop of `new Image()` cannot exceed limits)
- **Defects:** GOOSIE-IDLREFLECT-001, GOOSIE-IMAGE-001

#### Task 1.2: HTML5 Parser — Insertion Mode State Machine
- **File:** `internal/dom/parser.go`
- **What:** Replace the simplified tag-dispatch approach with the HTML5 spec's formal insertion mode state machine.
- **Insertion modes to implement (in spec order):**
  1. "initial" — doctype processing, quirks mode
  2. "before html" — create `<html>`, handle leading whitespace/comments
  3. "before head" — create `<head>`
  4. "in head" — `<meta>`, `<title>`, `<style>`, `<script>`, `<link>`, `<base>`, `<noscript>`
  5. "in head noscript" — scripting-enabled state
  6. "after head" — create `<body>`, handle whitespace
  7. "in body" — main content processing (the largest mode)
  8. "text" — raw text elements (`<script>`, `<style>`, `<title>`, `<textarea>`)
  9. "in table" — `<caption>`, `<colgroup>`, `<tbody>`, `<thead>`, `<tfoot>`, `<tr>`, `<td>`, `<th>`, foster parenting
  10. "in table text" — text in table context
  11. "in caption" — caption content
  12. "in column group" — `<col>` processing
  13. "in table body" — `<tr>` in tbody/thead/tfoot
  14. "in row" — `<td>`, `<th>` in tr
  15. "in cell" — cell content
  16. "in select" — `<option>`, `<optgroup>`, `<hr>`, `<template>` in select
  17. "in select in table" — table elements inside select
  18. "in template" — template content
  19. "after body" — post-body content
  20. "in frameset" — frameset content (legacy)
  21. "after frameset" — post-frameset
  22. "after after body" — post-document
  23. "after after frameset" — post-frameset document
- **Key algorithms:**
  - Open elements stack management
  - Active formatting elements list (for reconstruction)
  - Adoption agency algorithm (for misnested formatting)
  - Foster parenting (already partially implemented)
  - Reconstruct active formatting elements
  - Push onto stack / pop off stack / pop until matching
  - Generate implied end tags / generate all implied end tags
  - Reset insertion mode appropriately
  - Close the cell
  - Form element pointer
- **Acceptance:**
  - All existing parser tests pass
  - New tests for misnested tags: `<b>...<i>...</b>...</i>`, `<p>...<div>...</p>`, table content outside table
  - WPT `html/syntax/` tests pass (add to curated set)
  - No regression in render parity (80 fixtures)
- **Dependencies:** None (can start in parallel with Task 1.1)

#### Task 1.3: Live NodeList and HTMLCollection
- **File:** `internal/js/js.go`, new `internal/js/nodelist.go`
- **What:** Replace snapshot arrays with live interfaces.
- **`NodeList` (live):** `childNodes`, `getElementsByTagName` (returns `HTMLCollection`), `getElementsByClassName` (returns `HTMLCollection`)
- **`HTMLCollection`:** `namedItem(name)`, `item(index)`, `length` — all live
- **Implementation approach:** Use a proxy or wrapper that re-queries the DOM on each access. Cache invalidation tied to `Document.MarkQueryDirty()`.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `var kids = div.childNodes; div.appendChild(newChild); kids.length` reflects the new child
  - `var items = document.getElementsByTagName('div'); document.body.appendChild(document.createElement('div')); items.length` increases
  - Existing tests that use snapshot semantics still pass (most code iterates once)
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-NODELIST-001

### Phase 2: DOM API Completion

#### Task 2.1: `document.write()` / `document.writeln()`
- **File:** `internal/js/js.go`
- **What:** Implement `document.write` and `document.writeln` that insert HTML at the current parser insertion point.
- **Constraints:**
  - Only works during parsing (after `DOMContentLoaded`, it clears the document or throws per spec)
  - Must respect admission limits (node count, depth, attribute caps)
  - Must not break the parser state machine (Task 1.2)
- **Acceptance:**
  - `document.write('<p>hello</p>')` during parse inserts the paragraph
  - `document.write` after load clears document (per spec)
  - Budget enforced: cannot exceed `MaxDocumentNodes`
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-DOCWRITE-001

#### Task 2.2: DOMParser and XMLSerializer
- **File:** new `internal/js/domparser.go`
- **What:** `new DOMParser().parseFromString(str, type)` and `new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(node)`
- **Supported types:** `"text/html"`, `"application/xhtml+xml"`, `"text/xml"`, `"application/xml"`, `"image/svg+xml"`
- **Acceptance:**
  - `new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>hello</div>', 'text/html').body.firstChild.tagName === 'DIV'`
  - `new XMLSerializer().serializeToString(doc.createElement('div'))` returns `<div></div>`
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-DOMPARSER-001

#### Task 2.3: Range and Selection API
- **File:** new `internal/js/range.go`
- **What:** `document.createRange()`, `Range` object with `setStart`, `setEnd`, `selectNode`, `selectNodeContents`, `collapse`, `cloneContents`, `extractContents`, `insertNode`, `deleteContents`, `getBoundingClientRect`, `getClientRects`, `compareBoundaryPoints`, `cloneRange`, `detach`
- **Selection:** `window.getSelection()`, `Selection` with `anchorNode`, `anchorOffset`, `focusNode`, `focusOffset`, `isCollapsed`, `rangeCount`, `getRangeAt`, `addRange`, `removeRange`, `removeAllRanges`, `collapse`, `extend`, `selectAllChildren`, `collapseToStart`, `collapseToEnd`
- **Acceptance:**
  - `document.createRange()` returns a Range object
  - `range.selectNode(element)` selects the element
  - `window.getSelection().toString()` returns selected text
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-RANGE-001

#### Task 2.4: CSSOM View — Layout Measurement APIs
- **File:** new `internal/js/cssom_view.go`
- **What:** `getBoundingClientRect()`, `getClientRects()`, `offsetParent`, `offsetTop`, `offsetLeft`, `offsetWidth`, `offsetHeight`, `scrollTop`, `scrollLeft`, `scrollWidth`, `scrollHeight`, `scrollIntoView()`, `scroll()`, `scrollBy()`, `scrollTo()`
- **Implementation:** Requires querying the layout engine for box geometry. The layout arena stores computed positions; expose them through a query interface.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `div.getBoundingClientRect()` returns correct x/y/width/height/top/right/bottom/left
  - `div.scrollTop = 100` scrolls the element
  - `div.scrollIntoView()` scrolls ancestor containers
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-CSSOMVIEW-001

#### Task 2.5: TreeWalker and NodeIterator
- **File:** `internal/js/js.go`
- **What:** `document.createTreeWalker(root, whatToShow, filter)`, `document.createNodeIterator(root, whatToShow, filter)`
- **Acceptance:**
  - `walker.nextNode()` iterates all descendants
  - `iterator.nextNode()` returns nodes in document order
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-TREEWALKER-001

#### Task 2.6: RemoveEventListener Fix
- **File:** `internal/js/js.go`, `internal/dom/events.go`
- **What:** Currently `removeEventListener` is a best-effort no-op because goja wrappers create new function objects each time. Fix by maintaining a mapping from goja callback to the original Go function.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `el.addEventListener('click', fn); el.removeEventListener('click', fn); dispatch('click')` — listener not called
  - Capture flag matching works
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-RMLISTENER-001

### Phase 3: CSS Gaps

#### Task 3.1: `::first-line` Pseudo-element
- **File:** `internal/style/`, `internal/layout/inline.go`
- **What:** Implement `::first-line` styling per CSS §7.2. Only certain properties apply: font, color, background, text-decoration, etc.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `p::first-line { font-weight: bold }` styles the first formatted line
  - GOOSIE-PSEUDO-001 closed
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-PSEUDO-001

#### Task 3.2: CSS Grid WPT Enablement
- **File:** `test/wpt/curate.go`
- **What:** Enable CSS Grid in WPT feature support (`CSSGrid: true`), run `css/css-grid/` tests, fix failures.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Grid reftests pass at ≥90% rate
  - No regression in existing tests
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-GRIDWPT-001

#### Task 3.3: CSS Animations WPT Enablement
- **File:** `test/wpt/curate.go`, `internal/css/`, `internal/engine/`
- **What:** Enable CSS Animations in WPT (`CSSAnimations: true`), ensure `@keyframes` and `animation-*` properties work correctly, fix failures.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Animation reftests pass at ≥80% rate
  - `animation-fill-mode`, `animation-direction`, `animation-iteration-count` work correctly
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-ANIMWPT-001

#### Task 3.4: `:is()` and `:where()` Selectors
- **File:** `internal/css/selector.go`
- **What:** `:is()` and `:where()` now discard invalid selector-list members. `:is()` takes specificity from the most specific valid member; `:where()` has zero specificity. An empty resulting list is valid and matches nothing. Broader Selectors 4 grammar and matching support is still tracked separately.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `:is(h1, h2, h3)` matches any heading
  - `:where(.foo, .bar)` has zero specificity
  - Invalid selectors in the list are ignored; an empty resulting list is valid and matches nothing
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-ISWHERE-001

#### Task 3.5: `@layer` Cascade Layers
- **File:** `internal/css/`, `internal/style/`
- **What:** Implement CSS Cascade Layers. `@layer` blocks assign rules to named layers; `@layer` statement declares layer order. Lower layers have lower precedence than higher layers, regardless of specificity.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `@layer base { p { color: red } } @layer theme { p { color: blue } }` — paragraph is blue
  - Layer order declared by `@layer theme, base;` reverses precedence
  - Unlayered styles beat all layered styles
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-LAYER-001

#### Task 3.6: Container Queries
- **File:** `internal/css/`, `internal/style/`, `internal/layout/`
- **What:** Implement `@container` queries. `container-type: inline-size` on parent, `@container (min-width: 400px)` on child.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Container query matches when container width exceeds threshold
  - `container-name` scopes queries
  - Style recalculation when container resizes
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-CONTAINER-001

### Phase 4: Layout and Rendering

#### Task 4.1: Multi-line Flexbox
- **File:** `internal/layout/block.go` (flex section)
- **What:** Implement `flex-wrap: wrap` and `wrap-reverse`. Currently only single-line flex is supported.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Items wrap to new lines when container width exceeded
  - `align-content` distributes lines
  - `flex-wrap: wrap-reverse` reverses line order
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-FLEXWRAP-001

#### Task 4.2: Intrinsic Sizing Keywords
- **File:** `internal/style/`, `internal/layout/`
- **What:** Implement `width: min-content`, `max-content`, `fit-content()`. These require the layout engine to compute intrinsic sizes.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `width: min-content` shrinks to narrowest content
  - `width: max-content` expands to widest content
  - `width: fit-content(200px)` uses available space up to 200px
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-INTRINSIC-001

#### Task 4.3: `aspect-ratio` Property
- **File:** `internal/style/`, `internal/layout/`
- **What:** Implement `aspect-ratio` per CSS Sizing L4. When one dimension is auto, compute it from the other dimension and the aspect ratio.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `aspect-ratio: 16/9` with `width: 320px` → `height: 180px`
  - `aspect-ratio: auto` uses intrinsic aspect ratio (e.g., from image)
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-ASPECTRATIO-001

#### Task 4.4: Text Shaping (HarfBuzz Integration)
- **File:** new `internal/text/` or extend `internal/raster/`
- **What:** Integrate HarfBuzz (via cgo or pure-Go shaper) for complex text shaping. This is needed for Arabic, Devanagari, Thai, and other scripts that require glyph reordering and ligature substitution.
- **Constraints:** cgo only in `internal/platform/darwin` per arch rules. Consider a pure-Go shaper or a platform-independent approach.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Arabic text renders with correct glyph forms and joining
  - Thai text renders with correct positioning
  - No regression in Latin/CJK text rendering
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-SHAPING-001

#### Task 4.5: Bidirectional Text (Unicode Bidi Algorithm)
- **File:** new `internal/text/bidi.go` or extend `internal/layout/inline.go`
- **What:** Implement Unicode Bidirectional Algorithm (UAX #9). Determine paragraph direction, split into directional runs, reorder for display.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Mixed LTR/RTL text displays correctly
  - `direction: rtl` and `direction: ltr` CSS properties work
  - `unicode-bidi: isolate`, `embed`, `plaintext` work
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-BIDI-001

#### Task 4.6: Independent Formatting Contexts
- **File:** `internal/layout/`
- **What:** Refactor flex, grid, and table from branches inside `block.go` into independent formatting context implementations. Each should be a self-contained layout root that can be fixed and tested independently.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `layout.Flex()`, `layout.Grid()`, `layout.Table()` are callable independently
  - Each has its own test suite
  - No regression in existing layout tests
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-FMTCTX-001

### Phase 5: HTML Element Behavior

#### Task 5.1: `<canvas>` 2D Rendering
- **File:** `internal/js/canvas.go`, extend `internal/paint/`
- **What:** Implement Canvas 2D Context API. Currently a stub.
- **Required methods:** `fillRect`, `strokeRect`, `clearRect`, `fillText`, `strokeText`, `measureText`, `beginPath`, `closePath`, `moveTo`, `lineTo`, `arc`, `arcTo`, `bezierCurveTo`, `quadraticCurveTo`, `rect`, `fill`, `stroke`, `clip`, `drawImage`, `createLinearGradient`, `createRadialGradient`, `createPattern`, `setTransform`, `translate`, `rotate`, `scale`, `save`, `restore`, `getImageData`, `putImageData`, `createImageData`
- **Properties:** `fillStyle`, `strokeStyle`, `lineWidth`, `lineCap`, `lineJoin`, `miterLimit`, `shadowOffsetX`, `shadowOffsetY`, `shadowBlur`, `shadowColor`, `font`, `textAlign`, `textBaseline`, `globalAlpha`, `globalCompositeOperation`
- **Acceptance:**
  - Canvas renders 2D graphics correctly
  - Headless comparison against Chromium for basic canvas operations
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-CANVAS-001

#### Task 5.2: `<details>`/`<summary>` Toggle Behavior
- **File:** `internal/engine/`
- **What:** Ensure `<details>` toggles open/closed on click, `<summary>` is the activation trigger, and the `open` attribute reflects state.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Click on `<summary>` toggles `<details>` open/closed
  - `details.open` reflects the state
  - Only the first `<summary>` child is the activation trigger
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-DETAILS-001

#### Task 5.3: `<dialog>` Full Behavior
- **File:** `internal/engine/`
- **What:** Complete dialog behavior: modal backdrop, focus trap, Escape key dismissal, return value propagation, `returnValue` attribute.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `dialog.showModal()` traps focus
  - Escape key closes modal dialog
  - `dialog.close('result')` sets `returnValue` and fires `close` event
  - `::backdrop` pseudo-element styles the overlay
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-DIALOG-001

#### Task 5.4: `<slot>` Content Distribution
- **File:** `internal/dom/`, `internal/engine/`
- **What:** Implement slot distribution for Shadow DOM. `<slot name="x">` receives nodes with `slot="x"` from the light DOM.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Default slot receives unassigned light DOM children
  - Named slots receive children with matching `slot` attribute
  - `slotchange` event fires when distribution changes
  - `HTMLSlotElement.assignedNodes()` returns distributed nodes
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-SLOT-001

#### Task 5.5: `contenteditable` Attribute
- **File:** `internal/engine/`, `internal/layout/`
- **What:** Implement `contenteditable="true"` for rich text editing. Requires IME integration, selection/cursor rendering, and Range/Selection API (Task 2.3).
- **Acceptance:**
  - `contenteditable="true"` element accepts text input
  - Cursor is visible and positioned correctly
  - Basic editing: insert, delete, newline
  - `document.execCommand` works for bold/italic/underline (legacy but widely used)
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-EDITABLE-001

#### Task 5.6: Drag and Drop API
- **File:** `internal/engine/`, `internal/js/`
- **What:** Complete drag-and-drop: `draggable` attribute, `dragstart`, `drag`, `dragenter`, `dragover`, `dragleave`, `drop`, `dragend` events. `DataTransfer` with full MIME-type support.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `draggable="true"` element can be dragged
  - Drop target receives `drop` event with `DataTransfer` data
  - `event.preventDefault()` on `dragover` allows drop
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-DND-001

### Phase 6: Web APIs

#### Task 6.1: Web Animations API
- **File:** new `internal/js/animations.go`
- **What:** `element.animate(keyframes, options)`, `Animation` object with `play()`, `pause()`, `cancel()`, `finish()`, `reverse()`, `playbackRate`, `currentTime`, `playState`, `finished` promise, `onfinish` event.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `element.animate({opacity: [0, 1]}, 1000)` animates opacity
  - `animation.pause()` pauses the animation
  - `animation.finished` resolves when animation completes
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-WEBANIM-001

#### Task 6.2: Clipboard API
- **File:** new `internal/js/clipboard.go`
- **What:** `navigator.clipboard.writeText()`, `navigator.clipboard.readText()`, `navigator.clipboard.write()`, `navigator.clipboard.read()`. Requires permission integration.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `navigator.clipboard.writeText('hello')` copies to system clipboard
  - `navigator.clipboard.readText()` reads from system clipboard
  - Permission prompt or denial handled correctly
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-CLIPBOARD-001

#### Task 6.3: IndexedDB
- **File:** new `internal/js/indexeddb.go`
- **What:** `indexedDB.open()`, `IDBDatabase`, `IDBTransaction`, `IDBObjectStore`, `IDBRequest`, `IDBCursor`, `IDBKeyRange`. Full async API with transactions.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `indexedDB.open('test', 1)` creates/upgrades database
  - Object stores can be created in `upgradeneeded` event
  - `put`, `get`, `delete`, `getAll` operations work
  - Transactions commit/abort correctly
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-IDB-001

#### Task 6.4: Popover API Completion
- **File:** `internal/engine/`
- **What:** Complete popover behavior: light-dismiss (click outside closes), nesting (parent popover stays open when child opens), `popover` attribute values (`auto`, `manual`), `toggle` event.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `showPopover()` opens popover
  - Click outside closes `popover="auto"` but not `popover="manual"`
  - Nested popovers: closing child doesn't close parent
  - `toggle` event fires with `oldState` and `newState`
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-POPOVER-001

#### Task 6.5: BroadcastChannel
- **File:** new `internal/js/broadcast.go`
- **What:** `new BroadcastChannel(name)`, `postMessage()`, `onmessage`, `close()`. Cross-tab communication within same origin.
- **Acceptance:**
  - Two BroadcastChannels with same name can exchange messages
  - `close()` stops receiving messages
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-BROADCAST-001

### Phase 7: Testing and Validation

#### Task 7.1: WPT testharness.js Support
- **File:** `test/wpt/`
- **Status:** Partial implementation. The runner executes selected `testharness.js` files and collects pass/fail/timeout results, alongside reftests. Broader test coverage and nested browsing-context lifecycle support remain open.
- **What:** Extend testharness execution and result collection to support applicable `html/dom/`, `html/semantics/`, and `dom/` tests across the required browser contexts. Preserve each test's own completion behavior and report pass/fail/timeout/status.
- **Implementation:** Inject a test result collector that intercepts `add_result_callback` or the internal result reporting. Collect pass/fail/timeout/status for each assertion.
- **Acceptance:**
  - `testharness.js` tests in `html/dom/`, `html/semantics/`, `dom/` can be run
  - Results reported in the same format as reftests
  - CI can run both reftests and testharness tests
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-WPTHARNESS-001

#### Task 7.2: Expand WPT Coverage
- **File:** `test/wpt/curate.go`
- **What:** Enable and run WPT suites for:
  - `html/dom/` — DOM API conformance
  - `html/semantics/` — HTML element behavior
  - `css/css-grid/` — Grid layout (after Task 3.2)
  - `css/css-animations/` — Animations (after Task 3.3)
  - `css/css-pseudo/` — Pseudo-elements (after Task 3.1)
  - `css/css-images/` — Gradients (enable in curate.go)
  - `dom/` — DOM core
- **Acceptance:**
  - Each suite has a published pass rate
  - Failures are triaged and tracked as defects
  - Pass rate ratchets upward over time
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-WPTCOV-001

#### Task 7.3: Render Parity Expansion
- **File:** `testdata/`, `testdata/parity.py`
- **What:** Expand the render fixture corpus from 80 to 200+ fixtures covering:
  - Complex layouts (multi-column, nested flex/grid)
  - Form-heavy pages
  - Media-rich pages (images, video posters, canvas)
  - RTL/bidi text
  - Complex selectors and pseudo-elements
  - Responsive designs at multiple viewport sizes
- **Acceptance:**
  - All fixtures have Chromium reference images
  - Parity score published per fixture
  - CI runs full corpus on each commit
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-PARITY-001

#### Task 7.4: Live Website Sweep
- **File:** `docs/qa-live-parity/`
- **What:** Expand live website testing from 100 to 500+ real-world pages. Categorize failures by root cause (HTML/DOM/CSS/JS/layout/paint/network).
- **Acceptance:**
  - 500+ pages tested with automated comparison
  - Failure categories quantified (e.g., "23% fail due to missing JS APIs")
  - Top 10 failure causes identified and prioritized
- **Defect:** GOOSIE-LIVESWEEP-001

## 4. Priority Ordering (by live-site impact)

| Priority | Tasks | Rationale |
|----------|-------|-----------|
| **P0** | 1.1, 1.2, 1.3 | Element interfaces and parser correctness affect every page. Live NodeList affects every JS library. |
| **P1** | 2.1, 2.3, 2.4, 2.6 | `document.write` needed for legacy pages. Range/Selection needed for rich text. CSSOM View needed for layout measurement. RemoveEventListener needed for proper event cleanup. |
| **P2** | 3.1, 3.2, 3.3, 3.4, 4.1, 4.2, 4.3 | CSS gaps that cause visible rendering differences. Multi-line flex and intrinsic sizing are commonly used. |
| **P3** | 5.1, 5.2, 5.3, 5.4, 6.4 | Element behavior gaps. Canvas is large but affects specific pages. Dialog/details/popover are smaller. |
| **P4** | 2.2, 2.5, 3.5, 3.6, 4.6, 5.5, 5.6, 6.1, 6.2, 6.5 | Useful but less commonly blocking. DOMParser, TreeWalker, @layer, container queries, contenteditable, DnD, Web Animations, Clipboard, BroadcastChannel. |
| **P5** | 4.4, 4.5, 6.3 | Large efforts with narrower impact. Text shaping and bidi are critical for non-Latin scripts but don't affect Latin-script pages. IndexedDB is large. |
| **P6** | 7.1, 7.2, 7.3, 7.4 | Testing infrastructure. Important for measuring progress but don't directly fix user-facing issues. |

## 5. Effort Estimates

| Phase | Tasks | Estimated effort | Key risk |
|-------|-------|-----------------|----------|
| 1 | 1.1, 1.2, 1.3 | 3-4 weeks | Parser state machine is complex; goja prototype chain may have limitations |
| 2 | 2.1-2.6 | 2-3 weeks | `document.write` integration with parser is tricky; Range API is large |
| 3 | 3.1-3.6 | 2-3 weeks | Container queries require layout integration; @layer changes cascade |
| 4 | 4.1-4.6 | 4-6 weeks | Text shaping and bidi are deep rabbit holes; formatting context refactor is architectural |
| 5 | 5.1-5.6 | 3-4 weeks | Canvas 2D is a large API surface; contenteditable requires IME integration |
| 6 | 6.1-6.5 | 2-3 weeks | IndexedDB is large; others are moderate |
| 7 | 7.1-7.4 | 2-3 weeks | testharness.js support requires JS execution in test harness |
| **Total** | **27 tasks** | **18-26 weeks** | |

## 6. Scope Closure

There are no feature exclusions from the HTML Living Standard conformance goal. Features that need platform services or other specifications remain in scope when the HTML Standard depends on them. This includes, at minimum, media elements and tracks, canvas contexts (2D and WebGL), workers and worklets, storage, service-worker integration, navigation and document lifecycle, forms and validation, dialogs/popovers, custom elements and shadow DOM, and the parsing/DOM integration rules for SVG and MathML.

Payment, WebRTC, Web Audio, Bluetooth/USB, Gamepad, WebXR, PDF viewing, browser extensions, multi-process isolation, and GPU compositing are defined outside the HTML Standard or are browser product architecture choices. They are not criteria for this specifically scoped HTML Standard goal unless an HTML requirement depends on them. Any such dependency discovered during standards review must be recorded with its source and added to scope before declaring completion; it must not be silently treated as supported or skipped.

Implementation constraints (pure Go engine, no Fyne dependency, bounded untrusted input, architecture rules) are design constraints, not support exclusions. If a constraint prevents an HTML requirement from being implemented, the conflict must be surfaced and resolved rather than claiming full support.

---

## 7. Acceptance Criteria (Overall)

1. **Requirement traceability:** Every normative HTML Living Standard user-agent requirement in scope is mapped to implementation and an automated or documented manual conformance check. No requirement is marked supported based only on API presence or a screenshot.
2. **Parser conformance:** The HTML tokenizer, input preprocessing/encoding path, tree-construction algorithm, foreign-content integration, script execution hooks, and parser error recovery pass the applicable upstream html5lib parser corpus and WPT parsing tests. Test counts, skips, and failures are published by category.
3. **WPT coverage:** The runner executes both `testharness.js` and reftest tests. All applicable tests in the pinned `html/` and `dom/` suites, plus dependent suites required by HTML behavior, are reported. Completion requires no unexplained skips or failures; upstream expected failures and genuinely non-applicable tests need explicit, reviewable reasons.
4. **Behavioral/rendering parity:** HTML element, interaction, and rendering fixtures are compared with Chromium across the required viewport, input, and lifecycle states. A high aggregate pixel score cannot hide a failed semantic or interaction assertion.
5. **End-to-end coverage:** Representative pages exercise parser-created and script-mutated DOM, forms and validation, custom elements, shadow DOM, navigation/resource loading, storage, workers, media/canvas, focus/selection, and document lifecycle.
6. **Security:** All admission limits hold for parser, scripts, images, media, and other untrusted inputs; no new bypass vectors are introduced.
7. **Performance:** Existing frame-path counter gates and resource budgets remain green; new subsystems have bounded allocation, scheduling, and shutdown behavior.
8. **Architecture:** Import rules remain valid, cgo stays limited to its allowed package, package-level mutable globals remain absent, and all GUI-independent tests pass headlessly.

The default WPT config still selects a small curated subset across visual and `testharness.js` suites. The runner now accepts arbitrary configured WPT roots, and fails explicitly when any configured root is missing; `testdata/wpt-task55-full-config.json` selects the complete `html/`, `dom/`, and `domparsing/` roots plus dependent CSS, WebGL, media, encoding, URL, fetch, CORS, XHR, streams, messaging, worker, service-worker, storage, IndexedDB, File API, and WebIDL suites without exclusions; its pass-rate floor is 100%. HTML canvas tests are included under `html/`. That full profile has not yet been run, so it is an execution plan rather than conformance evidence. The latest pinned MessageChannel run passes all 9 non-tentative files; the sole failure is tentative coverage requiring unsupported `WeakRef`. Broader nested browsing-context lifecycle behavior remains incomplete. Full-run measurement and closing runner/engine gaps remain prerequisites for using conformance results as a completion gate.
