## 9. Rules

- **Do not touch** `docs/qa-test-catalog.md`, `docs/qa-open-defects.json`, `docs/qa-ratchet.json`, `test/qc/catalog.go`, `test/qc/traceability.golden.json`, `testdata/render-known-gaps.json`, `testdata/parity.py` without explicit proposal in the task's §8.
- `internal/archtest/rules.go:40-64` may be edited only if a task genuinely needs a new import path, with justification in the commit message.
- No `git push`, no merge, no PR until all acceptance criteria for a task are met.
- Every task must measure before writing; correct anything in this doc that data disproves.
- Each task must include mutation tests (kill at least one oracle per mutation, restore rc 0).
- Gate suite asserts counters, not durations — do not add timing assertions.
- All tests must pass on a machine with no display (headless).

**Activation behavior and inserted-subtree handler installation checked 2026-10-28:** script-driven `appendChild`, fragment insertion, `insertBefore`, `replaceChild`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, and shadow-root insertion now run the subtree activation step that installs event-handler content attributes as well as connection callbacks. A template-cloned checkbox regression verifies `.click()` toggles it and invokes its `oninput` handler. Anchor/area fragment clicks dispatch `hashchange` with `oldURL`/`newURL`; details summary clicks toggle the first summary's details element; label forwarding skips interactive descendants including links, areas, and summaries. The pinned `dom/events/Event-dispatch-single-activation-behavior.html` improved from 11/132 passing before this slice to 126/132 passing; the six remaining failures are nested-form submit-handler callbacks. The separate pinned `html/semantics/forms/form-submission-0/submit-event.html` passes 1/1, and the current HTML Standard initializes the submit event's bubbles flag to true, so the remaining result needs reconciliation with the fixture's nested-form activation expectation before changing propagation semantics. Focused JS, DOM, and architecture packages pass; scoped `go vet -all` and `git diff --check` are clean. This is a bounded activation slice, not TASK-055 acceptance; the broader WPT and feature audit remains open.

**Popover conformance increment checked 2026-10-28:** `popoverTargetElement` reflects `popovertarget`; targets resolve in the invoking shadow tree; clicks on descendants and labels use their button invoker; submit/reset controls preserve form activation; built-in `commandfor` show/hide/toggle-popover actions work through script clicks and engine pointer/keyboard activation. Imperative `togglePopover({force, source})` and `showPopover({source})` feed the active invoker chain and reject an invalid `source`. Opening a popover in a fully active iframe document is allowed, while `showPopover()` and opening `togglePopover()` on a detached document throw `InvalidStateError`. Auto-stack closure follows multiple invoker hops, auto/hint closure is covered, and a close during autofocus can complete while showing another popover during `beforetoggle` throws `InvalidStateError`. Opening a modal dialog closes open auto/hint popovers through their transition path. A canceled declarative activation no longer leaves stale invoker ancestry. `hidePopover()` is a no-op for a closed popover; unsupported and disconnected toggle calls use the required DOMExceptions. `ToggleEvent` constructor defaults, required type, readonly fields, `source`, and shadow retargeting are covered; transition sources now reach both `beforetoggle` and queued `toggle` events, including declarative and command invokers. Hit testing skips closed popover subtrees; whitespace-only text inside declarative-shadow hosts no longer expands inline hit bounds to the body. The `commandfor` invoker participates in light-dismiss checks, detached pointer targets are ignored, and non-primary pointer gestures do not light-dismiss; the testdriver dispatches `contextmenu` for right-click. The UA popover rule now uses auto insets and content-based shrink sizing; fixed-position auto-sized boxes support `place-self` centering. This brings `popover-alignment-001.html` to a 100% match and standard `light-dismiss-event-ordering.html` to pass; this is partial CSS sizing support; `position-area` currently supports physical side areas with `span-all`, while other area forms and anchor fallback behavior remain unsupported. The right-click behavior has a direct Go regression test. The focused pinned WPT selection passes 13/14, including `light-dismiss-event-ordering.html`, `light-dismiss-remove-target.html`, `popover-active-document.html`, `popover-alignment-001.html`, `popover-invoker-reset.html`, `popover-light-dismiss-command-edge-cases.html`, `popover-light-dismiss-command.html`, `popover-light-dismiss-contextmenu.html`, `popover-light-dismiss-flat-tree-nested.html`, `popover-light-dismiss.html`, `popover-open-in-beforetoggle.html`, `popover-removal-2.html`, and `popover-toggle-source.html`; the sole failure is `light-dismiss-event-ordering.tentative.html`, whose expected sequence conflicts with the standard fixture's ordering. The pinned `html/semantics/popovers` directory has 115 candidates: 67 pass, 47 fail, and 1 timed out; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-position-area-full/results.json`. This run includes 42 passing testharness files, 14 smoke tests, and 11 passing reftests; it has 41 failing testharness files, 6 failing reftests, and one testharness timeout. Remaining gaps include the tentative event-order disagreement, other `position-area` forms and anchor fallback behavior, hidden-popover UA rendering, top-layer/dialog/fullscreen interactions, focus navigation, and the still-partial hint stack. Mutation checks killed regressions in default popup sizing (standard event-order fixture), commandfor dismissal, removed-target connectivity, and non-primary pointer handling (Go regression test), as well as prior invoker-chain, closed-popover hit filtering, shadow-tree target lookup, `ToggleEvent` defaults, autofocus reentrant close, imperative/declarative source, active-document enforcement, `beforetoggle` reentrancy, and stale-invoker cleanup; all mutations were restored. Scoped Go packages and scoped `go vet -all` pass; `git diff --check` is clean. TASK-055 remains open.


**Popover anchor-area increment checked 2026-10-28:** `position-area` now parses physical `left`, `right`, `top`, `bottom`, and `center` placement with `span-all` alignment. An open popover's `showPopover({source})` element is passed to layout as its implicit anchor, and the area positions the popover against that anchor. The new layout regression test checks left adjacency and vertical centering and kills a mutation that disables left-side placement. The pinned `popover-alignment-002.html` reftest now passes at 99.16% pixel match; `popover-alignment-001.html` remains at 100%. The full pinned popover directory run is 67/115 (47 failures, 1 timeout), report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-position-area-full/results.json`. Scoped layout/style/engine/JS tests, architecture tests, `go vet -all` on the scoped packages, and `git diff --check` pass. This is a partial position-area implementation; logical axis values, complete grid semantics, collision fallback, and the broader TASK-055 acceptance criteria remain open.


**Details toggle, exclusive groups, keyboard activation, fragment navigation, and closed-content geometry checked 2026-10-28:** Added the details WPT directory to the explicit curator and a curator regression check. Details `open` changes through the reflected property, `setAttribute`, `removeAttribute`, and `toggleAttribute` enqueue trusted `ToggleEvent`s with `oldState`/`newState`, coalesce repeated changes within a task, and carry a `commandfor` source when activated by a command button. Opening a named details group closes peers in the same tree scope. Parser-created groups retain the first open member in tree order; DOM insertion APIs preserve an already open peer even when the inserted open member is inserted before it. Element sibling accessors skip non-element nodes and reflect live tree changes; this resolves 3 of the 5 `name-attribute.html` subtest failures caused by undefined sibling reads. `summary` is programmatically focusable, and the WPT testdriver's `send_keys` path performs Space activation on keyup while preserving key cancellation and active state. `location.hash` performs same-document fragment navigation, opens closed `<details>` ancestors of the target, and scrolls to its refreshed layout position; full-document `location.href`/path navigation remains unsupported. Closed details now use UA `content-visibility:hidden`: descendants retain layout geometry for CSSOM reads and the paint builder suppresses the closed subtree. The WPTs `details-toggle-source.html`, `details-toggle-source-commandfor.tentative.html`, `details-name-exclusivity-fragment-insertion.html`, `details.html`, `details-keyboard-activation.html`, `auto-expand-details-element-fragment.html`, and `closed-details-layout-apis.tentative.html` pass. The latest full pinned `html/semantics/interactive-elements/the-details-element` run has 20 candidates: 19 pass, 0 fail, 0 timeout, and 1 support file is skipped; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-details-final55/results.json`. `name-attribute.html` passes all 18 subtests, including parser-generated toggle-event ordering; `toggleEvent.html` passes all 11 subtests. Parser-inserted open details now queue their initial toggle when inserted, including after the runtime is bound. A later coalesced state change replaces the pending task so a displaced named-group peer is notified after the newly opened member. A Go regression checks this task order. Focused Go tests cover async ToggleEvent state/source/coalescing, group insertion, summary focus, closed-details hidden layout, paint suppression, and hash-driven reveal/scroll. Mutations disabling coalescing, the DOM-insertion group policy, summary focusability, fragment reveal, and the closed-details UA rule are killed by their respective regressions. Focused JS/engine/style/layout/paint/WPT/architecture packages and scoped vet pass; `git diff --check` is clean. TASK-055 remains open.



**Editing delete and WPT query-variant follow-up checked 2026-10-28:** The unfiltered pinned `editing/run/delete.html` test registers 7,842 assertions and did not complete within the runner timeout. The WPT test creates a command subtest followed by separate state and markup assertions. Initial query chunks `?1-10` and `?11-20` split the second case's delete command (subtest 10) from its markup assertion (subtest 12), so the latter reported unchanged markup because the edit had not run. Corrected non-overlapping chunks `?1-9` and `?10-20` both pass; the command and its follow-up assertions are now in the same chunk. The isolated upstream `<span>foo</span>{}<span>bar</span>` case also passes through the WPT `runConformanceTest` helper. `document.queryCommandIndeterm`, `queryCommandState`, and `queryCommandValue` return the required baseline values for the exercised delete cases, and `execCommand("delete")` removes the previous character at a collapsed caret, including surrogate pairs and inline-text boundaries. Direct JS regression now recreates the WPT marker removal and uses Selection `collapse()`/`extend()` at the inline boundary. Disabling collapsed-caret deletion kills the regression. Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-edit-delete-first-variant/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-edit-delete-coupled-results/results.json`, and `/tmp/goosie-task55-edit-delete-debug-case-results/results.json`. The remaining 7,822 registered assertions and broader editing algorithms remain unverified; TASK-055 remains open.

**Drag-and-drop pointer integration checked 2026-10-28:** Browser content pointer gestures now select draggable sources, wait for a five-pixel movement threshold, dispatch `dragstart` and movement/drop events through the session drag state, and preserve the normal click path if no drag starts. Escape cancels an active drag without firing `drop`. Drag transition events expose `relatedTarget`; initial `dragenter` starts with null, later `dragenter`/`dragleave` events identify the opposite target. User-agent click and drag events are trusted. The `draggable` reflected property now lives on `HTMLElement.prototype`, and changing its content attribute notifies mutation processing even when the effective default remains true. The WPT testdriver adapter now routes pointer down/move/up actions into the same engine drag state, translates element-relative offsets, emits `pointercancel`/`pointerout`/`pointerleave` when dragging starts, and suppresses later pointer events. The pinned `the-draggable-attribute` WPT pair passes 2/2; the selected `dragenter-dragleave-related-target.html` and `pointer-suppression.html` files pass 2/2; one legacy event page is skipped because it has no automated WPT marker. Host and engine regressions cover threshold behavior, shared `DataTransfer` payload, event order, trusted click fallback, related targets, and cancellation. Mutations removing the host threshold, erasing related targets, and disconnecting the testdriver pointer bridge were each caught by their regressions/WPT oracle; all were restored. Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-events-final5/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-wpt-final/results.json`; the bridge mutation timed out `pointer-suppression.html` at `/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-pointer-mutation/results.json`. Full DataTransfer access-mode enforcement, drag image painting, cross-document/file drops, and TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**DataTransfer access modes, event lifetime, and item identity checked 2026-10-28:** User-agent drag stores begin protected with `effectAllowed="uninitialized"`; script-created `DataTransfer` instances begin read/write with `effectAllowed="none"`. During dispatch, `dragstart` is read/write, `drop` is read-only, and every other drag event is protected. Each event receives a distinct `DataTransfer` view over the same operation store, and the view is disassociated after dispatch: saved views expose empty data, types, items, and files, and saved item kind/type become empty. `getData` is hidden in protected mode; `setData`, `clearData`, and `effectAllowed` updates do nothing outside read/write mode, `items.add()` returns null outside read/write mode, `items.remove()` throws `InvalidStateError` outside read/write mode, and `items.clear()` does nothing outside read/write mode. `types` remains enumerable and `dropEffect` remains writable. DataTransfer format replacement moves the replaced item to the end, and the `url` alias stores `text/uri-list` while `getData("url")` returns its first non-empty, non-comment line. String item addition rejects duplicate types, returns a string item with `getAsString()` callback support, and repeated indexed access preserves the same item object; removed/replaced items become disabled even if their type is later reused. File item objects likewise remain stable within a DataTransferItemList and become disabled when removed. Protected `files` access returns an empty live FileList. JS regressions cover drag-event access modes, URL alias behavior, type ordering, duplicate item types, cross-event object identity, shared payload access, post-dispatch disassociation, and item identity/disabled state. Mutations removing the protected-mode `getData` guard, the `url` alias, event-view disassociation, and string-item identity caching each fail their regressions; all mutations were restored. The selected pinned drag WPT set passes 4/4 (`/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-itemid-final/results.json`); focused DOM/JS/engine tests pass. A rerun of `go test ./... -count=1` passes every package except the existing `test/qc` traceability gate, which reports 59 automated cases awaiting QC sign-off and a stale protected golden file; the golden remains untouched. Scoped `go vet -all` passes. Remaining drag gaps include full file item behavior, drag image rendering, and cross-document/file drags. TASK-055 remains open.

**Drag operation selection checked 2026-10-28:** The [HTML Living Standard drag processing model](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/dnd.html#drag-and-drop-processing-model) derives the current operation from the post-dispatch `effectAllowed` and `dropEffect` values. Goosie now implements that compatibility matrix, only updates the current operation after `dragover`, suppresses `drop` when the result is `none`, sets `none` after canceled drags or uncanceled drops without a default action, and carries a canceled drop's chosen operation into `dragend`. Direct matrix coverage and an engine JS regression cover incompatible `move`/`copy`, valid combined effects, and drop/dragend feedback. The runtime mutation that bypasses the matrix makes the disallowed-copy case fire `drop` and fail its regression; the mutation was restored. `go test ./test/engine -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all` including architecture checks, and the selected pinned draggable/drag-event WPT set pass; WPT report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-effects-final/results.json` (4/4). This does not implement default text insertion, complete target selection behavior, drag images, or cross-document/file dragging; GOOSIE-DND-001 and TASK-055 remain open.

**Mixed `DataTransferItemList` ordering and indexed properties checked 2026-10-28:** File and string entries now share one insertion-ordered list, so `item(i)` and indexed access (`items[i]`) return the same stable item object as files and strings are interleaved. `DataTransfer.setData()` removes and re-appends the replaced string item in the list; `clearData()` removes string entries while preserving file entries, and `items.clear()` removes both kinds. Removing entries shifts indexed properties and disables removed item wrappers. A JS regression covers mixed insertion, removal, setData ordering, clearData versus list clear, stable file identity, and live indexed access. A mutation restoring file-first grouping fails the regression and was restored. Full `go test ./internal/js ./test/engine -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, architecture tests, and `git diff --check` pass. The selected pinned drag WPT set passes 4/4; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-order-final/results.json`. The DataTransfer item list and drag-and-drop model remain partial; TASK-055 stays open.

**`DataTransferItem.getAsFile()` snapshots checked 2026-10-28:** For a live file item in read/write or read-only mode, each `getAsFile()` call now creates a new File object carrying the underlying file's bytes, name, MIME type, and `lastModified`; removed items and protected/disabled access still return null. The FileList retains its own stable file object, as required for repeated reads from that FileList. A JS regression verifies separate object identities and a Go-side assertion checks byte contents and metadata. A mutation returning the original File object fails the regression and was restored. Full `go test ./internal/js ./test/engine -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, architecture tests, and `git diff --check` pass. The selected pinned drag WPT set passes 4/4; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-getasfile-final/results.json`. The broader file-item and drag-and-drop algorithms remain partial; GOOSIE-DND-001 and TASK-055 remain open.

**`DataTransferItemList` and data store WPTs checked 2026-10-28:** `DataTransferItemList` and `DataTransferItem` are exposed on the global and window objects, list instances share the interface prototype, and its prototype has exactly `add`, `clear`, `constructor`, `length`, and `remove` (no nonstandard `item()` operation). Indexed properties are enumerable, configurable, non-writable data properties containing stable `DataTransferItem` objects; removing entries updates the indices. `DataTransfer.types` returns a cached frozen array and replaces it when the ordered item list changes. `getData("url")` trims ASCII whitespace, matches the alias case-insensitively, and returns the first non-comment URI-list URL; requesting `text/uri-list` with MIME parameters returns the unconverted URI-list payload. Existing JS regressions now use standard indexed access, with Go coverage for URL alias and parameter handling. The pinned datastore directory passes all 8 runnable WPT files; 63 additional files are skipped by the harness: `/tmp/goosie-task55-datastore-final-results/results.json`. Mutations making indices writable and regenerating `DataTransfer.types` on every access each fail their WPT assertions and were restored. `go test ./internal/dom ./test/dom ./internal/js ./test/engine -count=1` passes. The broader drag-and-drop model and TASK-055 remain open.

**Editing boundary deletion and drag-and-drop WPT audit checked 2026-10-28:** Corrected the `editing/run/delete.html` variant split after tracing the apparent inline-boundary failure to an omitted command subtest; `?1-9` and `?10-20` both pass, while the full 7,842-assertion file remains unverified. The focused JS regression now reproduces the upstream `{}` marker removal and Selection `collapse()`/`extend()` setup. Pinned drag WPT inventory also passes every runnable curated case: `the-draggable-attribute` 2/2, `events` 2/2 (66 cases skipped by the harness), and `datastore` 8/8 (63 cases skipped). Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-edit-delete-first-variant/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-edit-delete-coupled-results/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-dnd-events-audit-results/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-draggable-audit-results/results.json`, and `/tmp/goosie-task55-datastore-final-results/results.json`. `go test ./test/js -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, architecture tests, and `git diff --check` pass. This audit does not close skipped drag tests, full editing behavior, or TASK-055.

**Anchor URL credential reflection checked 2026-10-28:** Anchors and areas share `HTMLHyperlinkElementUtils` URL component reflection, including `username` and `password`. Getters preserve URL percent-encoded serialization; setters update one credential while preserving the other and rewrite `href`. Go regressions cover anchor and area getters/setters and encoded credentials. The pinned upstream `htmlanchorelement_getter.html` and `htmlanchorelement_attribute-getter-setter.html` pass 2/2. A mutation returning an empty username fails the upstream setter/getter test and was restored. The full curated anchor directory has 4/13 testharness files pass, 9 errors or timeouts, and 6 support pages skipped; the remaining errors involve URL parsing with iframe document encoding, target-blank/opener behavior, and an unsupported crash-test runner flag. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-directory-results/results.json`; focused report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-final-results2/results.json`. Anchor credential reflection is covered; link navigation and full HTML conformance remain incomplete.

**Anchor document-encoding URL parsing checked 2026-10-28:** Anchor and area URL resolution now uses the owning document's base URL and character set. Non-UTF-8 document encodings are applied to query serialization (Windows-1252 `ß` → `%DF`), while path serialization remains UTF-8. Anchor/area link activation in an iframe navigates through the bounded iframe loader for default and `_self` targets; explicit non-self targets do not navigate the current frame. Go coverage checks href/search getters, the search setter, and UTF-8 path encoding. The pinned `anchor-src-encoding.html` WPT passes 1/1, including its iframe click and reload assertion; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-encoding-final/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. A full directory rerun was interrupted after 83 seconds without case output, so the earlier 4/13 aggregate remains the latest completed directory snapshot; target-blank/opener behavior and crash-test runner classification remain open.

**Anchor target-blank and BroadcastChannel activation checks checked 2026-10-28:** Anchor/area clicks now open hidden browsing contexts through the existing iframe loader. A `_blank` target gets a null opener by default; `rel=opener` preserves it, while `noopener`/`noreferrer` suppress it. The effective target inherits from `<base target>`. `window.open(url, name)` gives the context its browsing-context name; child scripts receive `window.name`, `window.close()` closes that context, and pre-load WindowProxy property writes are copied into the child realm. Browsing-context lookup now walks nested iframe runtimes. URL base lookup recognizes element nodes owned by iframe documents and inherits the embedding document's base URL for `srcdoc`, so relative links inside `srcdoc` navigate correctly. BroadcastChannel implements `addEventListener`/`removeEventListener`, capture identity, object listeners, and `once`, enabling the upstream opener tests to receive messages. Go regressions verify opener/no-opener, listener removal/once, child close/message delivery, and `_self` popup navigation. Pinned WPTs `target_blank_implicit_noopener.html` and `target_blank_implicit_noopener_base.html` pass 2/2; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-targetblank-final/results.json`. `htmlanchorelement_noopener.html` passes 1/1 file (all five testharness cases); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-noopener-final/results.json`. `anchor-src-encoding.html` passes 1/1; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-encoding-final/results.json`. `go test ./test/js ./internal/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. The latest completed full link-directory aggregate predates this increment; broader link navigation and full HTML conformance remain incomplete.

**Anchor transient activation and image-map pointer targeting checked 2026-10-28:** Opening a `_blank` anchor or area consumes transient activation in the source document while preserving sticky activation. WPT testdriver activation is now granted in the element's own `defaultView`, including popup realms; WindowProxy `navigator.userActivation` reads the child runtime's live activation state. The testdriver pointer adapter resolves image-map shapes at the action point and dispatches a click to the matching `<area>`, so mapped areas follow the same navigation path as direct element clicks. `window.open(url, "_self", features)` now navigates the current child browsing context and returns its WindowProxy; named-context lookup searches the live nested runtime tree. Go regressions check activation consumption and `window.open(..., "_self")`. The pinned `target_blank_useractivation.html` and `target_blank_useractivation_multi_globals.html` pass, along with noopener, implicit-noopener, and anchor-encoding coverage: 6/6 runnable files; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-link-activation-final/results.json`. After building the configured smoke-test binary, the refreshed full anchor directory passes 12/13 runnable tests, skips 6 support pages, and has one error in tentative `rel-opener-prevents-browsing-context-group-change.tentative.html`; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-directory-final2/results.json`. The `area-without-href-activation-crash.html` smoke test passes with the built binary. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. Link navigation and TASK-055 remain open.

**`window.open()` opener features and current anchor-directory result checked 2026-10-28:** The `noopener` and `noreferrer` feature tokens now force an unnamed context, suppress its opener, and make `window.open()` return `null`; a Go regression covers mixed feature casing, return value, `window.name`, and `window.opener`. The refreshed anchor directory remains 12/13 runnable tests passing, with six support skips and one error in tentative `rel-opener-prevents-browsing-context-group-change.tentative.html`; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-anchor-directory-features-final/results.json`. That tentative remote-context behavior remains open. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains incomplete.

**`window.open()` `_parent`/`_top` targeting and parser status refreshed 2026-10-28:** `window.open(url, "_parent")` and `window.open(url, "_top")` now reuse the selected existing context, navigate it, and return its stable WindowProxy. Go regressions cover both targets from an opened context and from a nested iframe targeting its popup. The focused link WPT selection still passes noopener, implicit-noopener, and both user-activation files; the tentative remote-context group-change file remains unresolved. The full pinned parser directory now reports 59/60 runnable files passing (29 support/manual/non-test skips); the sole failure is tentative `DOMContentLoaded-defer-task-order.tentative.html`. Its own WPT comment states that timer-task versus DOM-manipulation-task ordering is unspecified and that `DOMContentLoaded-defer.html` asserts the opposite; Goosie keeps the ordering covered by the non-tentative test, and `the-end.html` plus `DOMContentLoaded-defer.html` pass. Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-parser-directory-refresh/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-parser-lifecycle-current/results.json`, and `/tmp/goosie-task55-window-open-targets/results.json`. This records the tentative result with its standards rationale; parser and overall HTML conformance remain incomplete.

**Form-associated custom-element reset callbacks checked 2026-10-28:** `CustomElementRegistry.define()` now retains the form-associated lifecycle callbacks captured from the custom element prototype. Resetting a form resets built-in controls before invoking each listed form-associated custom element's `formResetCallback`; direct `form.reset()` delivers callbacks before returning, while reset-button activation queues them for the next microtask checkpoint. The pinned `custom-elements/form-associated/form-reset-callback.html` WPT passes all three tests, including callback timing and observing the reset output value: `/tmp/goosie-task55-form-reset-fixed/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. Other form-associated custom-element callbacks and full TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**Form-associated custom-element disabled state and callbacks checked 2026-10-28:** `:disabled` and `:enabled` now recognize form-associated custom elements and disabled fieldset inheritance, including the first-legend exception. The runtime tracks effective disabledness and invokes the captured `formDisabledCallback` when that state changes through the element's `disabled` attribute, an ancestor fieldset, disconnection/reconnection, or upgrade. The pinned `form-disabled-callback.html` and `form-reset-callback.html` pass 2/2; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-form-associated-callbacks/results.json`. Focused DOM, CSS, JS, WPT, and architecture packages pass, scoped `go vet -all` passes, and `git diff --check` is clean. Other form-associated APIs and the broader TASK-055 acceptance criteria remain open.

**Form-associated custom-element ownership callbacks checked 2026-10-28:** The runtime retains and invokes `formAssociatedCallback` when an upgraded custom element first gets a form owner or its owner changes, including association changes from `form`/`id` updates, parser insertion, and subtree moves/removal. Explicit `form` lookup now uses the element's actual tree scope, so a detached element no longer remains associated with a form elsewhere in its owner document. The pinned `form-associated-callback.html`, `form-disabled-callback.html`, and `form-reset-callback.html` pass 3/3; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-form-associated-final/results.json`. Focused DOM, CSS, JS, WPT, and architecture packages, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. Broader form-associated APIs and overall TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**Form-associated custom-element construction and legend changes checked 2026-10-28:** A form-associated custom element is excluded from `fieldset.elements` while its constructor is still running. A connected first-legend insertion or removal now refreshes effective disabledness for form-associated custom elements in the document, delivering `formDisabledCallback` transitions. Association refreshes now scan moved subtrees and use a document-wide scan only when a moved form can affect external `form`-attribute ownership. The pinned `fieldset-elements.html`, `fieldset-elements-after-upgrade.html`, `fieldset-elements-during-construction.html`, `form-disabled-legend.html`, `form-associated-callback.html`, `form-disabled-callback.html`, and `form-reset-callback.html` pass 7/7; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-form-associated-final3/results.json`. Focused DOM, CSS, JS, WPT, and architecture tests, scoped vet, and `git diff --check` pass. Remaining form-associated behavior and overall TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**Dynamic markup insertion counters and iframe URL navigation checked 2026-10-28:** `document.open(url, target, features)` now routes to the browsing context associated with its Document; the iframe `WindowProxy` exposes `open()` before its child Runtime exists. Same-context navigation removes the old `srcdoc` attribute so it cannot override the destination URL, and history snapshots are only cached for frames with an existing history entry. Closing an unchanged `document.write()` stream still runs the EOF parse when the open parse has not yet synthesized the document structure. Added a Go regression for child `WindowProxy.open()` navigation. The pinned `throw-on-dynamic-markup-insertion-counter-construct.html` and `throw-on-dynamic-markup-insertion-counter-reactions.html` WPTs pass 2/2; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-dynamic-markup-fixed/results.json`. After the attribute-node and table-reaction changes below, the refreshed pinned custom-elements directory selection has 116/187 passing, 64 failing, and 7 errored or timed out (62.0%); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-final2/results.json`. Overall TASK-055 acceptance remains open.

**Attribute-node reaction APIs checked 2026-10-28:** `Element.getAttributeNode()`/`getAttributeNodeNS()` and `NamedNodeMap.getNamedItemNS()`/`removeNamedItem()`/`removeNamedItemNS()` now return Attr-backed objects and route removal through the existing observed-attribute reaction path. NamedNodeMap item reads reuse live Attr wrappers, so value and ownerElement reflect current node state. The pinned `custom-elements/reactions/NamedNodeMap.html` and `Node.html` WPT files pass 2/2 (28 testharness assertions); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-attr-reactions/results.json`. Broader custom-element reaction coverage and TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**Table DOM mutation reactions checked 2026-10-28:** Table, table-section, and table-row interfaces now implement caption/head/foot creation and deletion, row and cell insertion/deletion, and caption/head/foot setters. Insertions and removals use the DOM mutation paths, preserving custom-element connected/disconnected reactions. The pinned `HTMLTableElement.html`, `HTMLTableRowElement.html`, and `HTMLTableSectionElement.html` WPT files pass 3/3; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-table-reactions-final/results.json`. Remaining custom-element reactions and overall TASK-055 conformance remain open.

**Element ARIAMixin attribute reactions checked 2026-10-28:** Element and HTML element prototypes now expose the standard reflected ARIA string attributes plus the Element-reference and Element-list properties. String setters use the common reflected-attribute path; reference-property replacements preserve their reference values while dispatching the required observed-attribute reaction when the serialized empty attribute remains unchanged. Pinned `AriaMixin-string-attributes.html`, `AriaMixin-string-attributes.tentative.html`, and `AriaMixin-element-attributes.html` pass 3/3 (104 testharness assertions); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-aria-final2/results.json`. Remaining HTML conformance gates and TASK-055 acceptance are still open.

**Parser-created custom elements and serialization checked 2026-10-28:** The HTML tree builder now constructs parser-created custom elements before attaching token attributes, reports parsed attributes afterward, and reports insertion before parsing child tokens. It supplies the token's `is` candidate before construction. Parser-created constructors support nested construction before `super()` and can return a separately constructed custom element; the returned instance is inserted while parsed attributes and insertion reactions target that instance. Parser and registry upgrade lookups exclude nodes outside the HTML namespace. The WPT runner now classifies `image/svg+xml` documents as XML, allowing the SVG XML parser case to reach the XML parser. A complete balanced `document.write()` fragment now mutates the DOM before the call returns; partial tokens, open elements, comments, and character references remain buffered across calls. Parser attribute processing now also preserves Window named properties, event-handler attributes, and custom-element registry association. Template-content nodes receive the inert template-contents owner document and are not upgraded using the active document's registry. HTML serialization emits the stored `is` candidate when the content attribute is absent. XML documents built through active iframe navigation now attach start tags during parsing and pause on completed script tokens; XML Blob iframes parse with their bound runtime, so parser-created customized built-ins and autonomous custom elements run constructors before their insertion step. Go regressions verify HTML parser reaction order and document.write chunking. The expanded pinned parser selection passes 12/12 files, including `parser-custom-element-in-foreign-content.html`, `parser-uses-constructed-element.html`, `parser-uses-create-an-element-for-a-token-svg.svg`, and XML reparenting; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-parser-complete-final/results.json`. The namespace lookup fix also passes `custom-elements/upgrading.html`; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-upgrading-namespace-fixed/results.json`. Earlier focused document-write and template reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-parser-write-verified/results.json`, `/tmp/goosie-task55-template-parser/results.json`. The preceding full pinned custom-elements checkpoint reported 118/187 passing, 52 failing, and 17 errors/timeouts; it predates these corrections and is recorded at `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-xml-fixed/results.json`. Focused DOM, JS, engine, WPT, and architecture tests and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**HTMLConstructor NewTarget behavior measured 2026-10-28:** The pinned `custom-elements/htmlconstructor/newtarget.html` and `newtarget-customized-builtins.html` both fail (0/2 files; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-htmlconstructor-baseline/results.json`). The failures cover fallback prototype selection across iframe realms, cross-realm `Reflect.construct()` errors, and native HTML constructors reading a proxy `NewTarget.prototype` before the expected extends validation. Existing Go coverage validates illegal constructors and interface mismatches but does not exercise these NewTarget realm/proxy cases. Full HTMLConstructor conformance remains open.

**Custom-element defined state, registries, and built-in coverage refreshed 2026-10-28:** `:defined` now reflects unresolved parser and DOM-created custom-element state and changes after upgrade. Iframe `Document.createElement()` resolves its registry at call time if its stable wrapper was first created before navigation installed the child document. Cross-document iframe navigation creates a new `CustomElementRegistry` for the active Document while preserving the old document association and WindowProxy identity; `per-document.html` passes. `ShadowRoot` no longer inherits the nonstandard `Node.createElement()` method, and `attachShadow()` reads only recognized option dictionary members; `historical.html` passes, with a Go regression for both behaviors. Shadow-root child moves now unlink the separate shadow child list, and detached elements retain their effective custom-element registry; DOM and JS regressions cover append/insert-before moves and null-registry adoption. Declarative null-registry roots are distinguished from imperative null roots when hosts cross documents. `CustomElementRegistry-initialize.html`, `Element-customElementRegistry.html`, and `element-mutation-null-registry-removal.html` pass. The broader `scoped-registry-effective-global-registry.html` matrix improved from 32 to 14 failing assertions; five registry files still fail overall. The runtime recognizes the standard HTML element-name set for customized built-in definitions and maps the missing caption, datalist, description-list, heading, legend, menu, picture, and track interfaces; `builtin-coverage.html` passes. The refreshed pinned 189-file custom-elements selection reports 132 passed, 49 failed, 8 errored or timed out (69.8% pass rate); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-registry-reassociation/results.json`. Parser remains 12/12, customized-built-in reactions 28/28, and state 5/5. Remaining failures include general reaction coverage, registry/adoption behavior, imports and clones, NewTarget realm semantics, and testdriver accessibility cases. TASK-055 remains open.

**Scoped-registry cross-document adoption refreshed 2026-10-28:** `Runtime.SetDOM()` now binds the main global custom-element registry to the parsed document when the runtime was created before parsing. Adoption owner-document lookup now includes shadow descendants, and declarative null-root adoption preserves inherited null associations while still updating independent per-node registries. The new `TestLateSetDOMBindsGlobalRegistryDocumentForAdoption` regression fails if the SetDOM binding is removed and passes when restored. The pinned registry selection (`scoped-registry-append.html`, `scoped-registry-effective-global-registry.html`, `CustomElementRegistry-initialize.html`, `Element-customElementRegistry.html`, and `element-mutation-null-registry-removal.html`) passes 5/5; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-registry-adoption-5files/results.json`. The refreshed full 189-file custom-elements selection reports 136 passed, 45 failed, and 8 errored (72.0%); registries are 30/32, parser 12/12, customized-built-in reactions 28/28, and state 5/5. Full report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-registry-adoption-final/results.json`. Focused DOM, JS, engine, WPT, and architecture suites pass, and `git diff --check` is clean. Broader custom-element failures and overall TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**ShadowRoot ownership and scoped-registry upgrade ordering checked 2026-10-28:** ShadowRoot now exposes `ownerDocument` from its host, preserving the actual document wrapper identity for iframe-owned roots. Scoped registries retain associated documents in association order; cross-document adoption associates the registry with each destination document before definition-time upgrades. The pinned `scoped-registry-define-upgrade-order.html` passes all 7 subtests; removing adoption-time document association makes its cross-document upgrade-order case fail with no upgraded candidates. The registry directory selection passes 31/32; the remaining failure is `Element-customElementRegistry-exceptions.html`, involving script and named-property behavior during iframe `document.write()`. The full 189-file custom-elements selection reports 137 passed, 44 failed, and 8 errored (72.5%); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-scoped-order-final/results.json`.

**Scripts in declarative shadow-root templates checked 2026-10-28:** The HTML parser now runs parser-inserted scripts in declarative shadow-root templates while keeping scripts in nested ordinary templates inert. DOM callback and engine end-to-end regressions cover the distinction. Suppressing declarative-template script callbacks makes both regressions fail; restoring the parser rule passes. This does not yet cover scripts streamed through `document.write()`, which is the remaining failure path in the registry exception WPT above. Focused DOM, JS, engine, WPT harness, and architecture suites pass; `go vet -all ./...` and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains incomplete.

**Iframe `document.write()` parser scripts and registry identity checked 2026-10-28:** Open document-write parsing now reports eligible parser-script tokens and runs them after committing the current tree. Template-content elements retain their inherited registry association until the declarative shadow root supplies one. Script-free writes to an uninitialized `about:blank` iframe retain the iframe's existing registry; markup containing parser scripts creates the child realm lazily, routes `open`/`write`/`close` through it, and preserves `CustomElementRegistry` wrapper identity across `contentWindow`. Go regressions cover declarative shadow scripts, child-window script execution, and failed custom-element registry reflection. Mutation checks confirmed that suppressing the parser-script callback breaks both script regressions and restoring the old template insertion registry assignment breaks the registry assertions. Four pinned WPTs pass 4/4 (`element-internals-shadowroot.html`, `CustomElementRegistry-upgrade.html`, `Element-customElementRegistry-exceptions.html`, and `scoped-registry-define-upgrade-order.html`); the previously failing parser owner-document test and registry exception test also pass together. The refreshed 189-file custom-elements selection reports 139 passed, 42 failed, and 8 errored or timed out (73.5%); registry tests are 32/32 and parser tests 12/12. Full report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-current4/results.json`. Focused JS, DOM, engine, and architecture checks, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. General reaction coverage, HTML constructor NewTarget behavior, import/clone behavior, testdriver-dependent cases, and overall TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**ChildNode and DOMTokenList custom-element reactions checked 2026-10-28:** `ChildNode.before()` and `replaceWith()` are now exposed on Element, CharacterData, and DocumentType. Both convert string arguments to text nodes, flatten DocumentFragments, perform one admitted mutation, and deliver disconnection, adoption, and connection steps. DOMTokenList now implements `replace()` and forwards assignment to `Element.classList` through the token list's value setter. Token-list operations enqueue observed attribute reactions even when a present attribute serializes to the same value; unchanged values still avoid visual invalidation. Go regressions cover ChildNode ordering, fragment consumption, observed class reactions, and no-op removal. Mutating `replaceWith()` into a no-op fails the ChildNode regression; mutating token-list callbacks to run only when serialized values change fails the reaction regression. The pinned `custom-elements/reactions/ChildNode.html` and `DOMTokenList.html` WPTs pass together (2/2). The refreshed 189-file custom-elements selection reports 141 passed, 40 failed, and 8 errored or timed out (74.6%); reaction tests are 11/29, parser 12/12, and registries 32/32. Full report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-latest5/results.json`; focused reaction report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-reaction-2files-final/results.json`. Focused DOM, JS, engine, and architecture packages, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Broader reaction algorithms, HTML constructor NewTarget behavior, import/clone behavior, testdriver-dependent cases, and overall TASK-055 acceptance remain open.

**DOMStringMap, Selection, and Range reactions checked 2026-10-28:** `dataset` writes and deletes now use shared mutation helpers that invoke observed-attribute callbacks for same-value writes while avoiding redundant visual invalidation. `Selection.deleteFromDocument()` now deletes the selected range and leaves its live range collapsed; Range subtree removal routes through node admission, radio-group invalidation, and custom-element disconnection handling. Range insertion, `surroundContents()`, and `cloneContents()` now reuse runtime insertion, removal, and custom-element clone/upgrade paths. Go regressions verify dataset add/same-value/delete reaction order and that selection deletion removes a connected custom element, runs `disconnectedCallback`, and leaves a collapsed selection. Suppressing dataset callbacks or the Range disconnection callback makes the corresponding Go regression fail; restored code passes. **Custom-element constructor microtask timing checked 2026-10-28:** Promise reaction callbacks now count as active JavaScript execution while they run, so custom-element construction or upgrade inside a reaction does not prematurely drain Promise and MutationObserver jobs before the callback returns. **Parser-created custom-element owner documents and dynamic-markup guard re-audited 2026-10-28:** Parser tokens produced through fragments/document.write can originate in a temporary parse document, so `UpgradeParserElementInDocument()` now adopts the token to its eventual owner document before invoking the constructor. **Parser constructor checkpoints respect active script execution checked 2026-10-28:** `UpgradeParserElementInDocument()` now performs its pre-construction microtask checkpoint only when the JavaScript stack is empty; synchronous `document.write()` parsing inside a running script leaves queued promise and mutation-observer work for the caller's checkpoint. **Parser-created customized built-in constructor mutations and exception prototypes checked 2026-10-28:** The synchronous `createElement()` constructor restrictions no longer reject children or attributes added directly by a parser-invoked constructor. **Synchronous custom-element constructor exception fallback checked 2026-10-28:** Prototype retention after a constructor throws is now limited to parser upgrades. Synchronous `document.createElement()` returns its `HTMLUnknownElement` fallback when the constructor throws, while reporting the thrown value; parser-created customized built-ins continue to retain the prototype installed by `super()`, as required by the separate parser fixture. `Document-createElement.html`, `customized-built-in-constructor-exceptions.html`, `microtasks-and-constructors.html`, and both HTML/XML parser-checkpoint files pass in the focused run (5 intended files; the filename include also selected the registries variant, for 6/6 total): `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-element-exceptions-refresh/results.json`. **Proxy-wrapped custom-element `instanceof` behavior checked 2026-10-28:** A Go regression now covers construction through a Proxy-wrapped custom-element class, one `prototype` access for valid construction, and `instanceof` checks against the proxy, its target class, and `HTMLElement`. Because goja's default `Function.prototype[Symbol.hasInstance]` path rejects a callable Proxy receiver, `CustomElementRegistry.define()` installs an OrdinaryHasInstance-equivalent handler on the target of a Proxy custom constructor that inherits the default handler. This workaround adds an own `Symbol.hasInstance` property to that target, an observable difference that still needs a semantics-preserving engine-level fix. The two pinned `HTMLElement-constructor*.html` files now have 3 failing assertions total, down from 6; the remaining cases require validating `NewTarget` before goja's native constructor allocates `this` and reads `NewTarget.prototype`. Focused report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-htmlconstructor-focused/results.json`. The refreshed custom-elements selection remains 166 passed, 15 failed, and 6 errored of 187 (88.8%): `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-after-proxy-instanceof/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The constructor ordering gap and overall TASK-055 remain open.

**Nested iframe document ownership in dynamic-markup counters checked 2026-10-28:** `runtimeForDocument()` now returns only a runtime that owns the requested document, rather than returning an unrelated nested child runtime when lookup misses. Document-write call sites explicitly fall back to their caller for detached or otherwise unowned documents. A unit regression covers both a known nested document and an unrelated document. The unrelated iframe lookup was triggered by the WPT's parallel `document.open(URL, "_self")` navigation; both HTML dynamic-markup counter files now pass (construct and reactions). Dynamic-markup XML behavior and TASK-055 remain open.

**HTML and XML dynamic-markup counter cases closed checked 2026-10-28:** Cross-realm document imports now retain their Document API surface through a stable proxy backed by the child realm's Document wrapper, and DOMExceptions assigned across the WindowProxy boundary are reconstructed in the receiving realm with their name, message, and legacy code. `window.open()` no longer requires an HTML `body`, allowing `document.open(URL, "_self")` from an XML browsing context to navigate. The earlier document-ownership correction remains in place. All four pinned `throw-on-dynamic-markup-insertion-counter-{construct,reactions}` HTML/XML WPT files pass (4/4), including cross-document operations and same-context navigation; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-dynamic-markup-all-final/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes these four counter fixtures only; TASK-055's full HTML Living Standard acceptance remains open.

**HTML constructor pre-allocation checks and `NewTarget.prototype` access order checked 2026-10-28:** HTML interface constructors now use a Proxy `[[Construct]]` boundary to validate constructor registration and interface compatibility before Goja performs `GetPrototypeFromConstructor`, preventing invalid `NewTarget` objects from triggering an observable `.prototype` read. Valid constructions then delegate to Goja's ordinary constructor allocation, preserving the one-read path. The Proxy `get` trap supplies OrdinaryHasInstance behavior for HTML interface constructors without adding own `Symbol.hasInstance` properties; a Go regression checks both inherited `instanceof` behavior and the absence of that own-property change. The pinned `HTMLElement-constructor.html` and `HTMLElement-constructor-customized-builtins.html` now pass 2/2, including the prior three prototype-read failures; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-htmlconstructor-focused/results.json`. The refreshed 189-file custom-elements run reports 174 passed, 11 failed, and 4 errored (92.1%); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-constructor-fix/results.json`. Remaining failures are in ElementInternals/testdriver support, cross-realm callback reporting, advanced NewTarget realm fallback, and focus delegation; these remain open. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes the constructor access-order cases only; TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-realm custom-element callback exception reporting checked 2026-10-28:** Accessing a blank iframe's `Function` constructor now lazily creates its initial `about:blank` runtime and replays pending WindowProxy handler assignments. Imported child functions preserve both call and construct behavior through a callable/constructible Proxy bridge; constructor failures are reported in the function's iframe global, and the outer custom-element construction path marks the element failed without reporting the same error again to the top window. A Go regression covers blank-iframe Function construction plus constructor and connected-callback exception targets. The pinned `custom-elements/cross-realm-callback-report-exception.html` passes all five constructor/lifecycle callback cases: `/tmp/goosie-task55-crossrealm-callback-fifth/results.json`. The refreshed custom-elements selection reports 173 passed, 10 failed, and 4 errored of 187 discovered candidates (92.5%); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-crossrealm-refresh/results.json`. The refreshed selection also shows `upgrading/upgrade-custom-element-error-event.html` still fails all four cross-realm custom-element constructor cases, now with constructor-result/exception-target issues; this bridge does not complete cross-realm HTML constructor integration. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes only the five callback exception-reporting cases; TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-realm constructor error ownership follow-up checked 2026-10-28:** Imported function proxies now retain the source iframe Runtime as their constructor realm, use ordinary `instanceof` behavior for inherited `Symbol.hasInstance`, and preserve Error-object prototype identity across realm imports. Custom-element result-validation failures for a foreign constructor are reported in its source realm while the destination element receives the required failed-construction fallback. The focused `cross-realm-callback-report-exception.html` remains passing. The pinned `upgrading/upgrade-custom-element-error-event.html` still fails its four subtests; its current errors are `Cannot read property 'target'/'currentTarget' of undefined` while forming the assertion failure message after awaiting an error event, so error-event ownership is not yet verified by this fixture. Focused `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Cross-realm constructor integration and TASK-055 remain open.

**Shadow DOM `delegatesFocus` and label activation checked 2026-10-28:** `ShadowRoot` now stores and exposes the readonly `delegatesFocus` flag from `attachShadow()` and declarative shadow roots; clonable roots preserve it. Programmatic focus on a delegating host chooses the first focusable shadow descendant, retargets `document.activeElement` to the host, exposes the inner active element from `ShadowRoot.activeElement`, and dispatches focus state/events on the delegate and host. Forwarded label activation now focuses its associated control after an uncanceled click, which also permits delegatesFocus to route focus to an internal input. The pinned `custom-elements/form-associated/disabled-delegatesFocus.html` and `label-delegatesFocus.html` pass 2/2 at `/tmp/goosie-task55-delegatesfocus-followup/results.json`. The refreshed 187-candidate custom-elements WPT selection reports 175 passed, 8 failed, and 4 errored (93.6%), improving from 173 passed, 10 failed, and 4 errored before this slice; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-delegatesfocus-refresh/results.json`. The remaining failures include advanced NewTarget realm fallback, ElementInternals/testdriver coverage, and cross-realm upgrade error reporting. `go test ./internal/dom ./test/dom ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes the measured delegatesFocus and label-focus cases only; TASK-055 remains open.

**HTML constructor NewTarget and initial iframe interface follow-up checked 2026-10-28:** Cross-window constructor proxies forward `new.target`, cache imported callables to preserve identity, and resolve fallback interface prototypes from the constructor definition's realm. Initial `about:blank` WindowProxy access lazily creates an iframe runtime for HTML interface constructors; `contentDocument` returns stable child-owned node proxies with the child interface prototype. `TestIframeInitialWindowExposesHTMLInterfaceConstructors` verifies that the child `HTMLDivElement` prototype and `instanceof` identity differ from the parent realm. Additional Go regressions cover NewTarget identity across an iframe constructor bridge, primitive-prototype fallback for both matching and nonmatching customized-built-in constructors, and object-prototype identity when a child value crosses the WindowProxy bridge. The bridge now carries generic object prototype chains when importing values. A follow-up caches the dynamic NewTarget `prototype` read after registration/interface checks, checks fallback at the HTML constructor boundary for primitive values, and inspects Proxy targets to resolve function realms; the cross-realm NewTarget WPT results did not improve. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. A fresh headless-binary five-file WPT run passes the two `HTMLElement-constructor` files and cross-realm callback exception file (3/5); `newtarget.html` and `newtarget-customized-builtins.html` still fail 8/10 subtests each, with primitive-prototype realm mismatches and `TypeError: Value is not an object: undefined` in GetFunctionRealm cases. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-newtarget-focused-results/results.json`. NewTarget realm semantics and TASK-055 remain open.

**Cross-realm legacy error-event state follow-up checked 2026-10-28:** When a child realm invokes a parent-realm listener for an `ErrorEvent`, the parent Window now retains the same imported event object in `window.event` after the listener returns, including across the promise continuation used by the WPT. `TestCustomElementCrossRealmErrorEventSurvivesPromiseContinuation` checks event identity and that its target is the constructor definition's Window after the microtask checkpoint. The pinned `upgrading/upgrade-custom-element-error-event.html` no longer errors while evaluating its `event.target`/`event.currentTarget` failure messages, and the definition-global target assertion is satisfied; it still fails 4/4 testharness cases because autonomous failures return an object that does not pass `instanceof w2.HTMLUnknownElement`, while customized-built-in failures have a prototype object that is not identical to `w2.HTMLDivElement.prototype`. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-error-event-followup/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Cross-realm fallback prototype identity and TASK-055 remain open.

**Fresh-runner cross-realm error closure and NewTarget gap re-audit checked 2026-10-28:** A current-source WPT runner passes `cross-realm-callback-report-exception.html` and `upgrading/upgrade-custom-element-error-event.html` (2/2) after retaining explicit imported function realms through proxy target traversal; the earlier registry-timeout reports came from a stale runner binary. The Go regression `TestCustomElementCrossRealmInvalidReturnReportsToConstructorRealm` covers the customized-built-in invalid-return error target and native fallback interface. A fresh five-file NewTarget run still passes only the two HTML constructor access-order files and the callback-exception file (3/5); `newtarget.html` and `newtarget-customized-builtins.html` each fail 8/10 subtests. The failures show that an empty iframe with definitions can expose the parent HTML interface constructor, causing the fallback prototype to have the wrong identity; the GetFunctionRealm variants also access an unavailable `w.Proxy`. Creating the child Runtime on `customElements` access was tested and reverted because it broke existing registry/adoption and iframe document-write/parser regressions. Treating `Proxy` as an eagerly imported intrinsic also caused a cross-runtime import stack overflow and was reverted. The current focused JS, engine, and architecture packages pass after reverting those experiments. Reports: `/tmp/goosie-task55-crossrealm-construct-fresh-runner/results.json` and `/tmp/goosie-task55-newtarget-postrealm-fix/results.json`. Correct per-Window intrinsic identity and stable registry ownership remain open; TASK-055 remains incomplete.

**Initial WindowProxy interface identity and NewTarget WPT slice checked 2026-10-28:** When an initial iframe has no child Runtime, its WindowProxy now supplies per-frame HTML interface constructor wrappers with distinct prototypes inheriting the canonical interface prototypes. A frame-specific `Proxy` constructor forwards construction to the native Proxy constructor so GetFunctionRealm follows the proxy target. Failed custom-element construction chooses the frame-specific native fallback interface prototype for nodes owned by that initial iframe document. `TestInitialIframeWindowUsesFrameSpecificHTMLConstructorPrototypes` verifies constructor identity, prototype inheritance, Proxy identity, and custom-element construction; existing initial-interface and cross-realm fallback regressions pass. TASK-055 remains incomplete.

**Fresh browser-binary custom-elements baseline checked 2026-10-28:** Rebuilt `cmd/goosie` and `cmd/wpt-runner` from the current worktree and reran all 189 selected custom-elements WPT files; 170 passed, 15 failed, and 4 errored (89.9%). **Iframe parser native-interface prototype alignment checked 2026-10-28:** HTML node wrappers now select the native interface prototype associated with their owner iframe document, including `HTMLElement` for valid autonomous custom-element names and `HTMLUnknownElement` for invalid names. When an iframe document’s cached native wrapper predates its per-frame interface mapping, `wrapNode()` refreshes that wrapper’s prototype while preserving already-upgraded and failed custom elements. `iframeInterfacePrototype()` imports the child Runtime’s own interface prototype when the iframe has loaded. `TestIframeInnerHTMLUsesIframeHTMLInterfacePrototype` covers both initial `about:blank` and a lazily-created iframe Runtime, including isolation from the parent custom-element registry. Focused JS, engine, and architecture packages, full `go vet`, and `git diff --check` pass. One initial full run deadlocked because the wrapper refresh tried to relock `Runtime.mu` from `wrapNode`; the stack trace identified the nested lock and the refresh now reads the construction map directly on the serialized JS path. Remaining iframe identity, parser, ElementInternals, and other HTML Living Standard gaps keep TASK-055 open.

**Synchronous custom-element adoption-back construction checked 2026-10-28:** `wrapNode()` now leaves the prototype untouched while a node is on the active HTML custom-element construction stack. The pinned `custom-elements/Document-createElement.html` passes, including all adoption-back document variants; a focused seven-file run covering it, the scoped-registry counterpart, iframe parser, NewTarget, and cross-realm constructor cases passes 7/7 at `/tmp/goosie-task55-adoption-frame-current/results.json`. Focused JS, engine, and architecture packages, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Other audited custom-element, ElementInternals, parser, and HTML Living Standard gaps keep TASK-055 open.

**Failed custom-element upgrade prototype and reaction ordering checked 2026-10-28:** When an existing undefined custom element is upgraded by `customElements.define()` and its constructor throws after `super()`, the failed element now retains the prototype installed by construction and does not receive `attributeChangedCallback` or `connectedCallback`. **Iframe exception brands and Node identity across realms checked 2026-10-28:** Same-origin iframe exceptions now retain their source `DOMException` prototype when observed through the parent WindowProxy. `importParentValue()` checks its established child-wrapper mapping before resolving the wrapper's numeric Node ID in the parent runtime; this prevents collisions from resolving a child element to an unrelated parent node. Go regressions cover source-realm `DOMException` identity and a forced child/parent Node ID collision. **WindowProxy constructor prototype invariant and callback exception reporting checked 2026-10-28:** The iframe `newTarget` bridge now returns its seeded `prototype` object for the fixed function property and synchronizes writable prototype assignments through the source constructor. This avoids a nested Proxy `[[Get]]` invariant error when a callback constructs an Error from another frame. `TestIframeImportedErrorConstructorPreservesPrototypeIdentity` covers `instanceof` and strict prototype identity. **ElementInternals accessibility roles and author names checked 2026-10-28:** Engine AX generation consults ElementInternals `role`, `ariaLabel`, and `ariaLabelledByElements`; it also applies supported ARIA role tokens and host `aria-label`/`aria-labelledby` to native and generic elements. The first supported role token is selected; referenced Elements contribute name text in list order and must share the owner's document and shadow tree scope. An invalid explicit host ID reference suppresses ElementInternals and fallback child text. The WPT harness exposes `get_computed_role` and `get_computed_label` through the engine AX tree. AX now includes supported named or role-bearing elements that lack layout objects, with DOM identity and zero bounds, and orders those nodes by DOM traversal. `TestAccessibilityElementInternalsRoleAndLabelledByElements`, `TestAccessibilityContentRoleTokensAndARIAName`, and `TestAccessibilityEmptyCustomElementWithoutLayout` cover precedence, invalid references, role token fallback, and empty custom elements. A fresh current-source WPT run passes `ElementInternals-role.html` and `element-internals-aria-element-reflection.html` (2/2, all assertions): `/tmp/goosie-task55-accessibility-axbridge3/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. **ElementInternals strong-reference and shadow-root stress coverage checked 2026-10-29:** `ElementInternals.shadowRoot` now reports an already-attached open root and retains the declarative-root behavior. Per-node `CustomStateSet` and non-form-associated validity state are allocated lazily. Shadow-root mutation methods share prototype functions, shadow-root lookup uses a stored host-node ID, and detached custom-element registry lookup avoids scanning all shadow hosts. This removes quadratic construction work and lowers the cost of the pinned 100,000-element strong-reference test.
