The full HTML Living Standard task remains open.

**Form validation reporting focus and visual mismatches checked 2026-10-29:** `ElementInternals.reportValidity()` now dispatches `invalid` and, unless canceled, focuses its validation anchor (or the host when no anchor exists). Form `reportValidity()` and interactive `requestSubmit()` use the reporting path; `checkValidity()` continues to dispatch invalid events without moving focus. `TestElementInternalsReportValidityFocusesValidationAnchor` checks check/report separation, canceled-event behavior, and anchor focus. The pinned `ElementInternals-reportValidity-bubble.html` and `ElementInternals-reportValidity-delegatesFocus.html` expected-mismatch reftests now pass (2/2, 99.8% image score each): `/tmp/goosie-task55-report-validity-focus/results.json`. The refreshed 187-file custom-elements aggregate reports 183 passed, 1 failed, and 3 errors: `/tmp/goosie-task55-custom-elements-report-validity-refresh/results.json`. The remaining failure is the tentative `ElementInternals` behaviors API (14/15 subtests fail because `HTMLSubmitButtonBehavior` is not implemented); three tentative behavior pages report no tests. Focused JS/engine/WPT/architecture suites, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This does not close full HTML Living Standard conformance; TASK-055 remains open.

**`Document.domain` getter and setter subset checked 2026-10-29:** The Document prototype now exposes a URL-derived `domain` getter and a setter that requires a browsing context, rejects sandboxed frames without `allow-same-origin`, IDNA-normalizes the supplied host, and permits only the current effective domain or a registrable suffix (including exact IP hosts). The effective domain is stored per Document, and subsequent assignments cannot widen it back to a narrower host. `TestDocumentDomainGetterAndRelaxation` and `TestDocumentDomainRejectsInvalidSuffixes` cover getter, valid relaxation, public-suffix rejection, unrelated hosts, and narrowing rejection. Focused JS, engine, WPT harness, and architecture suites pass; `go vet -all ./...` and `git diff --check` pass. The focused `show-picker-cross-origin-iframe.html` still times out with 3/4 harness tests failing, so iframe module execution/message delivery remains unresolved and this implementation does not claim full domain-relaxation conformance. TASK-055 remains open.

**Synchronous iframe access after `document.domain` checked 2026-10-29:** `iframe.contentDocument`'s origin gate now applies same-origin-domain comparison for live child runtimes: both documents must have set the same domain, and their schemes must match; relaxed comparisons ignore ports. If neither document relaxed, the existing tuple-origin check still requires scheme, host, and effective port to match. `TestIframeSameOriginDomainRequiresBothDocumentsToRelax` exercises the transition through the actual Document setters, rejects one-sided relaxation, accepts both documents setting `example.test` across ports, and rejects a subsequent scheme mismatch. Focused JS, engine, and architecture suites, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The picker API intentionally retains its separate origin check; its focused WPT remains unresolved at `/tmp/goosie-task55-cross-origin-picker-current/results.json`. This closes only the synchronous iframe access comparison path; TASK-055 remains open.

**Unrelaxed child before Runtime creation checked 2026-10-29:** The iframe access gate now also denies a non-blank child whose Runtime has not been created if the parent has already set `document.domain`; matching URL tuples alone cannot bypass the child's still-unrelaxed origin. The same iframe regression test covers this interim state. Focused JS/engine/architecture suites, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. A fresh run still reports the existing picker WPT timeout (3/4 failed or timed out): `/tmp/goosie-task55-cross-origin-picker-current/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-origin WindowProxy and Location access surface checked 2026-10-29:** Iframe WindowProxy reads, writes, `in` checks, property descriptors, own-key enumeration, deletion/definition, prototype operations, and symbol access now apply a cross-origin allowlist. It exposes only the HTML-defined window references, navigation/location, lifecycle methods, frame count/indexes, `postMessage`, `then`, and the supported well-known symbols; unknown properties raise `SecurityError`. The cross-origin Location proxy permits `href` assignment and `replace()`, denies href reads and other property access, and reports only its permitted own keys. `TestCrossOriginWindowProxyExposesOnlySafelistedProperties` now covers these checks and both proxies' reflection behavior. `TestIframeSameOriginDomainRequiresBothDocumentsToRelax` continues to pass. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The focused cross-origin picker WPT remains unresolved (3/4 failed or timed out in the last recorded run); this closes only the tested synchronous access/reflection subset, and TASK-055's full HTML Living Standard scope remains open.

**Cross-origin Location fallback reflection and iframe opener checked 2026-10-29:** Cross-origin `Location` now exposes the HTML-defined `then` and well-known-symbol fallback descriptors in addition to `href` and `replace`; unsupported symbol reads, checks, descriptors, deletion, and definitions raise `SecurityError`. Iframe browsing contexts also expose `window.opener` as `null` when they have no opener. The WindowProxy regression now checks these reflection paths. The focused iframe-origin/WindowProxy tests and `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. A fresh `show-picker-cross-origin-iframe.html` run still times out with 3/4 harness tests failing at `/tmp/goosie-task55-cross-origin-picker-current/results.json`; its iframe module/message integration remains an open, separately audited gap. These changes close only the tested reflection and opener cases; TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-origin indexed WindowProxy key shape and nested-frame transfer audit checked 2026-10-29:** The cross-origin WindowProxy own-key builder now emits iframe indices as property-key strings, and indexed child descriptors are marked enumerable, matching the WindowProxy own-key and indexed-property algorithms. While adding a nested-iframe regression, constructing/synchronizing a WindowProxy for a child document containing its own iframe raised `TypeError: Object has no member 'item'` from the cross-realm value importer (`internal/js/iframe.go` `importChildValue` property read). The end-to-end indexed child access test was not retained as passing; nested cross-realm WindowProxy transfer remains open and needs an importer fix before this behavior is covered. The tested cross-origin access/reflection regressions and `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. A fresh run of `show-picker-cross-origin-iframe.html` still reports 3/4 failing or timed out at `/tmp/goosie-task55-cross-origin-picker-current/results.json`. TASK-055 remains open.

**Nested cross-origin iframe WindowProxy import and accessor preservation checked 2026-10-29:** Cross-realm ordinary-object import now copies enumerable own-property descriptors without invoking source getters, and bridges imported accessors so they execute in their source Runtime when read. Arrays use the same descriptor-preserving path. This fixes iframe-global synchronization that previously raised `TypeError: Object has no member 'item'` when a child document contained another iframe. `TestCrossOriginWindowProxyIndexedChildAccess` now verifies nested indexed WindowProxy access, own-property descriptor enumerability, and `Object.keys`; `TestImportChildValuePreservesEnumerableAccessorsWithoutInvokingThem` checks that import does not run a getter and a later read calls it exactly once. Both pass, as do `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check`. The picker WPT remains unresolved at `/tmp/goosie-task55-cross-origin-picker-current/results.json` (3/4 failures or timeout), so the module/message path requires further investigation. This closes the importer failure and tested indexed access only; TASK-055 remains open.

**Dynamic iframe navigation pump and cross-origin picker WPT checked 2026-10-29:** `Session.PumpJS()` now flushes iframe navigations queued by script callbacks after document startup. `TestPumpJSProcessesIframeNavigationQueuedAfterStartup` checks that setting `iframe.src` waits for the event-loop pump, then installs the child document and fires one load event. The WPT server now uses `wpt.localhost` plus reachable `*.localhost` aliases for its original, sibling, and alternate hosts; URL origins remain distinct while requests reach the local server. This replaces the previous single-origin transport restriction that left `show-picker-cross-origin-iframe.html` unable to exercise its intended cases. The fresh pinned WPT run now passes all four testharness cases (1/1 file): `/tmp/goosie-task55-cross-origin-picker-current/results.json`. The WPT result collector also includes failure stacks and timeout fetch/iframe counters for future diagnosis. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. This closes the audited picker origin/message fixture and dynamic iframe pump behavior only; the full HTML Living Standard scope remains open.

**InputEvent `dataTransfer` construction subset checked 2026-10-29:** Synthetic `InputEvent` instances now expose `dataTransfer` with the nullable default (`null`), retain the identity of an actual `DataTransfer` supplied in `InputEventInit`, and throw `TypeError` for a non-DataTransfer object. Wrapped browser-generated input events expose `dataTransfer: null`, covering the current typing/editing path. `TestInputEventConstructorAndDispatchPayload` checks defaults, identity, and invalid input; `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The runner did not discover either `input-events/idlharness.window.js` (an IDL harness support file) or `input-events-cut-paste.html` as a runnable test in its current curated selection, so no WPT result is claimed for this change. The latter remains the relevant unmeasured behavior. In particular, rich contenteditable paste still needs to provide its clipboard `DataTransfer` on `beforeinput`. This closes only the synthetic-construction and null-default subset; TASK-055 remains open.

**InputEvent `dataTransfer` IDL attribute placement checked 2026-10-29:** Corrected the preceding constructor subset so `dataTransfer` is exposed as an enumerable, configurable getter without a setter on `InputEvent.prototype`, rather than as an own data property. Constructor instances store the nullable value internally; native event wrappers expose the same getter, with `null` for the current typing path. The JS regression checks the prototype descriptor and absence of an own property, while `TestContentEditableEngineTextEntryUsesSelectionAndInputEvents` checks the native event value. Both focused tests pass. Rich paste/cut transfer population and full InputEvent IDL conformance remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**Text-control keyboard edits use trusted Input Events checked 2026-10-29:** Printable insertion and textarea Enter now route through the existing `CommitText` path, dispatching cancelable trusted `beforeinput` before mutation and non-cancelable trusted `input` afterward. Backspace and forward Delete on text controls dispatch the matching `deleteContentBackward`/`deleteContentForward` InputEvents, honor cancellation, and keep the caret synchronized after mutation. The event dispatch runs outside the session lock so handlers can call back into the engine. When a JS Runtime is absent, the same actions still dispatch trusted DOM `InputEvent` payloads. Regressions cover event order/type/data/trust/cancelability, insertion cancellation, Backspace, and Delete; `go test ./test/engine -run 'TestTextControlKeyboardEditsUseInputEventsAndHonorCancellation|TestTextInputFiresInputEvent|TestBackspaceFiresInputEvent' -count=1` passes. This does not cover clipboard cut/paste, all text-control types and editing commands, or full Input Events conformance; TASK-055 remains open.

**Text-control Input Events also preserve selected-range replacement checked 2026-10-29:** The keyboard edit path now uses the selection bounds when replacing text and computes `maxlength` against the value after the selected range is removed. This preserves select-all/Shift+Arrow replacement semantics while routing ordinary typing through `CommitText`. JS-backed and runtime-free tests cover trusted `beforeinput`/`input` payloads, cancellation, and edit order; a first engine regression caught and fixed the lost-selection behavior. Focused engine checks pass. The event work remains limited to these text-control keyboard actions; clipboard, paste, and broader editing algorithms remain open.

**Text-control Input Events repository verification checked 2026-10-29:** `go test ./...` passes all packages except `test/qc`; that gate reports 59 automation cases awaiting QC sign-off and a stale protected `traceability.golden.json`, consistent with the repository verification recorded above. The QC golden was left unchanged. `go vet -all ./...` and `git diff --check` pass after the text-control editing changes. This does not alter TASK-055's open status.

**Plain-text paste Input Events and DataTransfer branding checked 2026-10-29:** The browser Cmd+V handler now calls `Session.PasteText`. Text-control paste dispatches trusted `beforeinput`/`input` with `inputType="insertFromPaste"`, clipboard text in `data`, and null `dataTransfer`. Contenteditable paste dispatches trusted `beforeinput` with null `data`, a read-only `DataTransfer` containing `text/plain`, then disassociates that transfer after dispatch; the accepted edit produces `input` with the paste input type. DataTransfer wrappers now inherit `DataTransfer.prototype`, so `instanceof`, `constructor`, and `Object.prototype.toString` identify them correctly. Regressions cover text-control and contenteditable paste payloads, read-only and post-dispatch behavior, insertion, and DataTransfer branding. The pinned `input-events-cut-paste.html` remains undiscovered by the current WPT runner, and this path provides plain text only: ClipboardEvent `paste` dispatch, copy/cut, and rich `text/html` clipboard data remain open. Focused tests pass; broader package and repository verification follows below. TASK-055 remains open.

**Trusted paste ClipboardEvent and cancellation checked 2026-10-29:** `Session.PasteText` now dispatches a trusted, bubbling, cancelable `paste` `ClipboardEvent` before `beforeinput`; its read-only `clipboardData` contains the plain-text payload and becomes disabled after dispatch. Preventing `paste` stops the subsequent `beforeinput` and mutation. `ClipboardEvent` is exposed with a nullable, readonly prototype `clipboardData` getter, DataTransfer brand checking, and the Event prototype chain. Text controls then receive null `InputEvent.dataTransfer` with text in `data`; contenteditable receives the read-only plain-text transfer on `beforeinput`. Engine and JS regressions cover event order, event/interface brands, payload lifetime, cancellation, and resulting text. This supersedes the prior note that paste ClipboardEvent dispatch was missing. Copy/cut, rich HTML clipboard payloads, and the pinned cut/paste WPT remain open; the current runner does not discover that WPT file. TASK-055 remains open.

**Selection copy/cut ClipboardEvents and host shortcuts checked 2026-10-29:** `Session.CopySelection` and `Session.CutSelection` now dispatch trusted `copy`/`cut` ClipboardEvents with writable `text/plain` DataTransfer payloads and return any text supplied by handlers. Cut follows an uncanceled event with trusted `beforeinput`/`input` using `deleteByCut`; canceling either `cut` or `beforeinput` preserves the selected content. Selection handling covers text controls, contenteditable Selection ranges, and document text selection for copy. The Cmd+C/Cmd+X host path writes the event payload to the platform clipboard, and Cmd+X reflows after deletion. Engine and app regressions cover text-control copy/cut, contenteditable copy/cut, handler payload edits, event order/trust, cancellation, and platform-shortcut integration. Rich `text/html` clipboard serialization, copy/cut behavior for every selection type, and the pinned cut/paste WPT remain open; the WPT runner currently does not discover that file. TASK-055 remains open.

**Clipboard copy/cut implementation verification checked 2026-10-29:** The affected internal DOM/JS/engine packages, engine and JS test packages, browser app, WPT harness, and architecture tests pass with `go test ./internal/dom ./internal/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./test/js ./cmd/goosie ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`. `go vet -all ./...` and `git diff --check` pass. The app Cmd+C regression now selects the text explicitly before asserting copy behavior. This verifies the current plain-text clipboard increment, not full clipboard API conformance or TASK-055 completion.

**Rich HTML clipboard copy, cut, and paste checked 2026-10-29:** Contenteditable copy/cut now return both plain text and serialized HTML; partial selections inside inline elements retain the ancestor markup needed to represent the selection. The macOS clipboard shim reads and writes `text/html` alongside `text/plain`. Cmd+V passes both formats into a trusted `paste` ClipboardEvent and `beforeinput`; contenteditable prefers HTML insertion through bounded fragment parsing and `execCommand("insertHTML")`, while text controls continue to insert plain text. The inserted fragment uses the existing runtime mutation paths and emits `input` after the accepted edit. `TestCopyAndCutSelectionDispatchClipboardAndInputEvents` checks partial inline serialization, and `TestContentEditableRichPasteUsesBoundedHTMLInsertion` checks insertion position, markup, event ordering, and both transfer formats. `go test ./internal/dom ./internal/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./test/js ./cmd/goosie ./test/wpt ./internal/archtest/ ./internal/platform/darwin -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The current WPT runner still does not discover `input-events-cut-paste.html`; no WPT result is claimed. Rich clipboard support remains a limited editing subset and TASK-055 remains open.

**Formatting command state queries checked 2026-10-29:** `Document.queryCommandState()` and `queryCommandIndeterm()` now report bold, italic, and underline state from the current editing selection. A collapsed caret uses its formatting ancestor or pending typing style; a non-collapsed range reports indeterminate when only part of the selected text has that style. `TestContentEditableFormattingCommandStateAndIndeterminateQueries` covers collapsed, uniformly formatted, mixed, and pending-style states. A mutation that forced pending typing styles to report false caused the new regression to fail, confirming the oracle detects the behavior change; the mutation was restored. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. No dedicated query-state WPT was present in the checked-in WPT corpus. Broader editing command algorithms remain incomplete, and TASK-055 remains open.

**Host Clipboard API wiring and activation checks checked 2026-10-29:** App document runtimes now receive the platform clipboard provider, so `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` and `readText()` share text with the browser's system clipboard. Trusted pointer clicks set transient user activation before dispatching click handlers. Host clipboard operations reject with `NotAllowedError` outside a secure context or without transient activation; standalone runtimes retain their per-runtime in-memory clipboard for isolated tests and non-platform use. `TestDocumentClipboardAPIUsesHostProvider` loads a page with a fake host clipboard, clicks a button, and checks the page's write/read round trip; `TestPlatformClipboardRequiresSecureContextAndTransientActivation` checks rejection and confirms the provider remains unchanged, then checks an activated secure-origin write. A mutation that bypassed the access guard caused the security regression to fail and was restored. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./cmd/goosie ./internal/platform/darwin ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Permissions API state/prompt integration, Clipboard API rich `read()`/`write()`, and iframe-provider propagation remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**ClipboardItem and rich Clipboard API text formats checked 2026-10-29:** `ClipboardItem` now exposes ordered MIME `types`, `getType()`, `presentationStyle`, and `supports()`. `navigator.clipboard.write()` and `read()` round-trip one clipboard item with `text/plain` and `text/html` Blob representations through the host's rich clipboard interface; the macOS clipboard shim already transports those two pasteboard flavors. Direct Blob, File-as-Blob, and Promise<Blob> representations resolve before the host clipboard is updated; a promised value that resolves to a non-Blob rejects with `TypeError`. Unsupported formats, missing representation types, invalid items, and unsupported multiple-item writes reject. `TestClipboardItemReadWriteTextAndHTML` checks constructor branding, format support, both Blob payloads, and round-trip data; `TestClipboardItemRejectsUnsupportedRepresentationAndType` checks rejection behavior; `TestClipboardItemPromiseRepresentationsResolveForReadAndWrite` checks promised data and invalid Promise results; `TestClipboardItemAcceptsFileAsBlobRepresentation` checks that File instances satisfy the Blob representation type. The app-level `TestDocumentClipboardAPIUsesHostProvider` exercises a trusted click, writes both formats from promised Blob representations, reads HTML back through `ClipboardItem.getType()`, and checks the host provider and page result. A mutation that skipped awaiting promised representations made the new Promise regression fail; restoring the implementation passes. The `js.Options` copy into iframe runtimes carries the provider, but the child has no verified path to receive transient user activation. Clipboard permission prompt/state integration, multiple native items, formats beyond text, and iframe activation propagation remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.

**Per-origin clipboard permission states and Permissions query checked 2026-10-29:** Site settings persist separate `clipboard-read` and `clipboard-write` allow/block decisions. The JS runtime exposes `navigator.permissions.query()` for those descriptors and returns a `PermissionStatus` with a live `state` getter (`prompt`, `granted`, or `denied`). Permission lookup uses the querying document URL, so copied iframe runtime options resolve the child document’s origin instead of retaining the parent’s decision; `TestClipboardPermissionStateUsesEachDocumentOrigin` checks distinct top and child URLs. Default platform access still requires secure context and transient activation; an explicit block rejects reads/writes with `NotAllowedError`, including for the in-memory fallback. The site inspector displays prompt-on-activation defaults and lets users allow, block, or reset each clipboard capability. `TestClipboardPermissionsQueryAndLiveState` checks default/changed states, unsupported descriptors, and block enforcement; `TestSitePermissions_ClipboardReadStatusAndDenialReachRuntime` checks permission query and read/write denial through an activated document; `TestSitePermissions_InspectPageActions` checks UI state and persistence actions. A mutation that bypassed the denied-state check made the activated access regression fail and was restored. Focused permission tests and `go test ./internal/perms ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./cmd/goosie ./internal/platform/darwin ./internal/archtest/ -count=1` pass. The broader Permissions API, permission status change events, browser-owned prompt UI, multiple native clipboard items, other formats, and iframe activation propagation remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.


**Iframe transient user activation propagation checked 2026-10-29:** The runtime now applies HTML activation notification across browsing contexts: activation reaches the initiating runtime and its ancestor runtimes, plus descendant runtimes whose effective tuple origin matches the initiating document. `about:blank`/`about:srcdoc` child runtimes inherit the parent origin for this comparison. Activation consumption clears transient state across the registered top-level runtime tree, including cross-origin descendants. `TestTransientActivationPropagatesAcrossIframeOrigins` checks parent-to-same-origin, parent-to-srcdoc, cross-origin isolation, child-to-ancestor propagation, and page-wide consumption; it also verifies that the activated same-origin child can write to its host clipboard while the unactivated cross-origin child cannot. Focused activation regressions pass. Host pointer hit-testing and event delivery into real iframe documents are not yet end-to-end verified, and the broader Permissions API, browser prompt UI, multiple native clipboard items, other formats, and full TASK-055 acceptance remain open.


**BroadcastChannel origin isolation and event origin checked 2026-10-29:** The shared hub now matches channels by tuple origin and channel name rather than channel name alone. Opaque-origin runtimes use private hub keys, so two unrelated `null` origins cannot exchange messages; initial `about:blank`/`srcdoc` runtimes inherit their creator origin. Message events now expose the sender origin serialization. Cross-runtime tests prove same-origin pages still exchange cloned plain-object data, distinct origins cannot receive messages in either direction, and `event.origin` omits the URL path. `go test ./test/js -run TestBroadcastChannel -count=1` passes. `postMessage()` now throws `InvalidStateError` after close, rejects missing arguments with `TypeError`, and reports unsupported values with `DataCloneError`. The JSON-encoded reference graph also reconstructs `Date`, `RegExp`, cyclic `Map`/`Set`, `BigInt`, `ArrayBuffer`, and typed arrays in the destination realm. Regressions verify cycles, repeated object identity, sparse holes, `undefined`, `NaN`, infinities, negative zero, an own `__proto__` data property, invalid dates, regular-expression flags, map/set self references, buffer alias identity, and typed-array values. `DataView`, `Blob`/`File`, storage-key partitioning, and other structured-clone types remain unsupported; storage-key partitioning, worker eligibility, and complete `MessageEvent` semantics remain open; TASK-055 remains open.


**BroadcastChannel built-in structured-clone types checked 2026-10-29:** The bounded clone graph now serializes and reconstructs `Date` (including invalid dates), `RegExp`, `Map`, `Set`, `BigInt`, `ArrayBuffer`, and numeric/BigInt typed arrays, in addition to the earlier primitive/array/plain-record subset. Object identity is registered before nested Map/Set entries so self references survive; repeated `ArrayBuffer` references resolve to one destination buffer. Cross-runtime coverage checks cyclic maps/sets, Date values, RegExp flags, large BigInts, buffer bytes and aliasing, and typed-array constructors and contents. `go test ./test/js -run TestBroadcastChannel -count=1` passes. `DataView`, Blob/File and other platform cloneables, storage-key partitioning, and complete MessageEvent behavior remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**App BroadcastChannel hub wiring and private-context separation checked 2026-10-29:** Network document runtimes now receive the frame's shared `BroadcastHub`, with a separate hub selected for the private HTTP client, matching the existing normal/private storage split. `TestFramePathBroadcastHubSeparatesPrivateBrowsing` checks hub selection, and `TestLoadedDocumentsKeepPrivateBroadcastMessagesSeparate` loads three real document sessions to prove that two normal pages exchange a same-origin channel message while a private page does not receive it. Full storage-key partitioning, including sharing rules across separate app windows/profiles, remains incomplete, as do DataView, Blob/File and other platform cloneables, worker eligibility, and complete `MessageEvent` behavior; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel DataView cloning checked 2026-10-29:** The clone encoder now detects Goja DataView objects before its generic object path, serializes the backing ArrayBuffer with byte offset and length, and reconstructs the view in the destination runtime. The cross-runtime built-ins regression checks the destination interface, offset, length, an endian-aware read, and backing bytes. This closes DataView cloning for the currently supported non-shared ArrayBuffer case; `SharedArrayBuffer`, transfer semantics, Blob/File and other platform cloneables, and complete structured-clone conformance remain open. `go test ./test/js -run TestBroadcastChannelStructuredCloneBuiltinsAcrossRuntimes -count=1` passes; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel Blob and File cloning checked 2026-10-29:** Objects backed by Goosie's Blob/File data now clone as platform objects, including bytes, MIME type, File name, and lastModified. The built-ins regression checks Blob/File branding and metadata, then awaits `.text()` in the receiving runtime to verify the cloned bytes. The existing 8 MiB BroadcastChannel message cap bounds encoded payloads. Other platform cloneables such as ImageData and CryptoKey, SharedArrayBuffer, transfer semantics, storage-key details, worker eligibility, and complete MessageEvent behavior remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel typed-array backing-store identity checked 2026-10-29:** Typed arrays now clone by backing ArrayBuffer reference, byte offset, and element length, rather than by copying indexed values into independent arrays. The cross-runtime built-ins regression checks the typed-array view and a partially overlapping Uint8Array share the cloned buffer, retain their offsets, and observe writes through the shared bytes. Typed-array view types are covered alongside standalone ArrayBuffer alias identity. Resizable/growable buffers, transfer semantics, and the wider structured-clone type set remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel MessageEvent dispatch state checked 2026-10-29:** Inbound channel messages now use a branded `MessageEvent` instance. Event constructors are installed for runtimes without a DOM tree as well as document runtimes, so BroadcastChannel-capable non-DOM contexts have the corresponding event interface. During delivery, the event exposes `target`/`currentTarget`, `AT_TARGET`, a positive timestamp, trusted status, and a one-entry composed path; after delivery `currentTarget` clears, the phase returns to `NONE`, and `composedPath()` is empty. The core cross-runtime regression checks interface identity and these dispatch lifecycle fields. Listener propagation/order and the full HTML MessageEvent/EventTarget algorithms remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel event-handler listener ordering checked 2026-10-29:** `onmessage` and `onmessageerror` now use the channel's listener list. Replacing an installed handler updates it in place, clearing it removes it, and assigning a handler again registers it at the new point in order. `TestBroadcastChannelOnMessageUsesListenerRegistrationOrder` verifies handler-before-listener delivery across replacement and listener-before-handler delivery after clear/reassignment. More complete EventTarget mutation-during-dispatch and propagation rules remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel live listener removal/addition during dispatch checked 2026-10-29:** The dispatcher now snapshots listener identities, then consults the live listener list before each callback. Removing a not-yet-called listener during dispatch suppresses it; a listener added during dispatch waits until the next message. Event-handler replacement resolves the current callback at invocation time while retaining its registration slot. `TestBroadcastChannelListenerMutationsDuringDispatch` verifies those rules across consecutive messages, and `TestBroadcastChannelStopImmediatePropagation` verifies later listeners are skipped after `stopImmediatePropagation()`. Broader EventTarget propagation phases and signal/abort integration for BroadcastChannel listeners remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel listener AbortSignal integration checked 2026-10-29:** `addEventListener()` now validates its optional `signal`, ignores already-aborted signals, and unregisters the listener when its AbortSignal abort algorithm runs. `TestBroadcastChannelListenerAbortSignal` covers already-aborted and active signals, abort before and after delivery, and rejection of a non-AbortSignal object. Full EventTarget option conversion and propagation semantics remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel top-level schemeful-site partitioning checked 2026-10-29:** Channel matching now includes the schemeful site of the top-level browsing context alongside the channel origin and name. The site key uses the Public Suffix List's effective registrable domain, falling back to the host for IPs and hosts without a registrable suffix. `TestBroadcastChannelUsesTopLevelSchemefulSitePartition` proves same-origin third-party frames under separate subdomains of the same top-level site exchange messages, while a frame under a different top-level site is isolated. Full storage-key state, including nonces and browser profile partitioning across separate windows, and exhaustive PSL/opaque-context behavior remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel IDN origin serialization checked 2026-10-29:** Tuple-origin and top-level-site host keys now use IDNA Lookup ASCII serialization before matching and populating `MessageEvent.origin`. `TestBroadcastChannelIDNOriginUsesASCIIHostSerialization` proves a Unicode hostname and its punycode spelling share the same origin/site key and exchange messages; the event origin serializes as ASCII. Invalid IDNA hosts become opaque-origin keys. Full URL/IDNA edge-case conformance and the remaining storage-key dimensions are still open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel.name readonly IDL attribute checked 2026-10-29:** `name` is now a branded getter on `BroadcastChannel.prototype` rather than a writable own data property. The constructor regression checks the getter value, `Reflect.set()` rejection, inherited property shape, absent setter, and `TypeError` for an incompatible receiver. This closes the tested attribute surface only; other BroadcastChannel members and full Web IDL descriptors still need conformance coverage; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel operation placement and iframe Error bridge checked 2026-10-29:** `close()` and `postMessage()` now live on `BroadcastChannel.prototype` as non-enumerable writable/configurable operations, and both reject incompatible receivers. While exercising the prototype `name` getter, the iframe import bridge was found reading `.name` on arbitrary child objects to detect Errors; that could invoke unrelated throwing accessors. Error conversion now checks the source object's Error class before reading inherited Error fields. BroadcastChannel constructor, exchange, and structured-clone tests pass, as do the affected JS, engine, app, Darwin platform, and architecture packages; `go vet -all ./...` and `git diff --check` pass. EventTarget methods and event handler attributes still need complete Web IDL prototype placement and descriptor coverage; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel EventTarget inheritance in detached runtimes checked 2026-10-29:** Runtimes without a DOM document now install the shared EventTarget interface before BroadcastChannel, so `BroadcastChannel.prototype` inherits `EventTarget.prototype` and instances pass `instanceof EventTarget`. The constructor regression checks the prototype link and brand. This resolves the missing base interface for detached/worker-like runtimes; aligning BroadcastChannel listener storage and all EventTarget dispatch algorithms remains open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel EventTarget dispatch and cancellation checked 2026-10-29:** Inbound `message`/`messageerror` events and explicit `channel.dispatchEvent(event)` now share the same at-target listener dispatcher. Explicit dispatch validates its receiver and event argument, sets and clears target/currentTarget/phase and composed-path state, returns `false` for canceled events, honors `stopImmediatePropagation`, live listener removal, registration order, and `once` removal before invocation. Listener option parsing now includes `passive`, and constructed MessageEvent.preventDefault() respects both cancelability and passive-listener state. A new regression verifies manual `MessageEvent` dispatch, `onmessage` ordering, the returned cancellation result, post-dispatch state reset, and passive cancellation suppression. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, `git diff --check`, and gofmt checks pass. Complete EventTarget brand conversion, propagation beyond a single target, and full Web IDL placement/descriptors for BroadcastChannel event methods remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel Event argument and constructed MessageEvent state checked 2026-10-29:** `dispatchEvent()` now checks the argument's Event prototype chain and Goosie Event marker before dispatch, rejecting a plain `{type: ...}` object with `TypeError`. Constructed `MessageEvent` objects now start with initialized dispatch flags, null targets, phase `NONE`, and `isTrusted=false`; their `stopPropagation()` and `stopImmediatePropagation()` methods update the flags used by dispatch, and `preventDefault()` honors cancelability and passive-listener state. The manual-dispatch regression checks the Event brand, constructor trust state, invalid-object rejection, listener/handler order, cancellation, and passive behavior. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, `git diff --check`, and gofmt checks pass. Cross-realm Event branding and complete Event interface conversion remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel EventTarget prototype operations checked 2026-10-29:** BroadcastChannel now inherits `addEventListener()`, `removeEventListener()`, and `dispatchEvent()` directly from `EventTarget.prototype`; those shared operations route channel instances into the same channel-specific listener records used by queued message delivery. `onmessage` and `onmessageerror` are now inherited accessors on `BroadcastChannel.prototype`, not per-instance own properties. EventTarget operations have non-enumerable writable/configurable descriptors. The constructor regression checks inherited function identity, attribute placement, and descriptors; all BroadcastChannel tests plus `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1` pass. `go vet -all ./...`, `git diff --check`, and gofmt checks pass. Cross-realm Event branding, generic EventTarget receiver branding, and propagation beyond a BroadcastChannel's at-target dispatch remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**Same-origin cross-realm BroadcastChannel dispatch checked 2026-10-29:** The iframe value bridge now exposes child BroadcastChannel instances through a source-backed proxy, so inherited EventTarget methods and BroadcastChannel accessors execute with the child channel's brand and listener state. Event dispatch accepts Goosie Event instances created in another same-origin runtime, even though their prototype chain belongs to the creating realm. `TestBroadcastChannelDispatchAcceptsParentRealmEventInSameOriginChild` proves a parent-created `MessageEvent` reaches a listener registered on the child channel through its WindowProxy. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./cmd/goosie ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, `git diff --check`, and gofmt checks pass. Cross-origin channel object access remains barred by WindowProxy, while cross-realm platform brand propagation for other EventTarget subclasses and full HTML conformance remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**Iframe `Document` wrapper identity and customized built-in import checked 2026-10-29:** `contentDocument`, same-origin `contentWindow.document`, and imported iframe nodes' `ownerDocument` now resolve to one stable parent-visible Document wrapper, including when the child Runtime is created lazily after the first `contentDocument` read. `createElement()` and `createElementNS()` on that wrapper delegate into the child Runtime once it exists, preserving the child document's custom-element registry and child-realm node wrappers. The regression checks stable identity across all three Document access paths plus target-registry upgrade, clone identity, and `is` preservation. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, `git diff --check`, and gofmt checks pass. This is local Go coverage, not a fresh WPT runner result; the previous aggregate WPT report remains stale or inconsistent with current source. Additional Document operations in this bridge and full HTML conformance remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**Forgiving `:is()`/`:where()` selector lists checked 2026-10-29:** CSS selector validation and precompilation now discard invalid alternatives in `:is()` and `:where()` while retaining valid members; the resulting list may be empty and then matches nothing, `:is()` specificity uses only retained selectors, and `:where()` remains zero-specificity. `TestIsAndWhereUseForgivingSelectorLists` covers matching, valid-member specificity, pseudo-element/unknown-pseudo filtering, and valid empty-list behavior. This follows the current [Selectors Level 4 forgiving-list algorithm](https://www.w3.org/TR/selectors/#forgiving-selector-list). `go test ./internal/css ./test/css ./internal/style ./test/style ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, `git diff --check`, and gofmt checks pass. This closes the forgiving-list defect slice only; broader Selectors 4 and TASK-055 conformance remain open.

**WPT full-scope directory override and profile checked 2026-10-29:** Explicit `Config.Directories` now replace the built-in curated catalog and may name uncurated roots such as `html` and `dom`; path traversal and absolute paths are rejected, and a missing explicitly configured root is reported as an error instead of being silently skipped. Added `testdata/wpt-task55-full-config.json` with no test exclusions, a 100% pass-rate floor, the full HTML/DOM/DOM Parsing roots, and dependent platform suites. `TestConfiguredWPTDirectoriesOverrideCuratedCatalog`, `TestConfiguredWPTDirectoriesRejectPathsOutsideCheckout`, `TestExplicitWPTDirectoryDiscoveryReportsMissingRoots`, and `TestTask55FullConfigSelectsHTMLDOMAndDependentSuites` cover selection and config integrity. This broad profile has not been run, so it supplies coverage configuration only and is not evidence of conformance. The default WPT config remains curated; TASK-055 remains open.

**Pinned WPT archive extraction and selector smoke run checked 2026-10-29:** Fixed the GitHub archive extractor to ignore PAX metadata when identifying the archive root, reject paths that escape the destination using `filepath.Rel`, and fail on archives with no regular files so an empty checkout cannot receive a valid commit marker. `test/wpt/wpt_checkout_test.go` covers PAX-prefixed extraction, empty archives, and traversal. Rebuilt `cmd/goosie` and ran `TestWPTSuite` with the full TASK-055 directory profile and `WPT_INCLUDE=is-where-basic.html`; the pinned checkout at `11ae8c1179be2dd38070846b01c2e9338ef81770` extracted successfully and the selected `css/selectors` test passed (1/1, 100%). This only validates checkout/discovery/execution for one selector test; the full profile has not been run and TASK-055 remains open.

**Pinned HTML parser spot-check and iframe Node descriptors checked 2026-10-29:** The `html/syntax/parsing` spot-check initially found `iframe.contentDocument.body.childNodes` missing. The iframe value bridge copied only enumerable own properties when importing prototype objects, dropping non-enumerable Node IDL accessors; it now preserves own property descriptors and known well-known symbols without evaluating getters during the copy. The iframe integration regression checks a live `childNodes` length and indexed element access. After the fix, `adoption-agency-reparenting-document-target.html`, `form-element-pointer-in-template.html`, and `inhead-noscript-head.html` pass 3/3 in the pinned WPT checkout, along with the iframe integration test. This focused slice does not establish parser conformance; broader cross-realm Web IDL behavior and TASK-055 remain open.

**Pinned `html/syntax/parsing` directory audit checked 2026-10-29:** The entire directory profile discovers 93 files: 60 pass, 1 fails, 32 support/manual files are skipped, and 0 error (98.4% of runnable tests). Cross-realm node imports now reuse one parent-visible wrapper for native nodes in an iframe document, keeping `contentWindow` and `contentDocument` node identity stable; custom-element wrappers and nodes with script-replaced prototypes remain source-backed, and prototype changes made through WindowProxy are reflected on shared wrappers. The bridge preserves non-enumerable IDL descriptors, well-known iterator symbols, and the non-enumerable `length` property hidden by collection proxy own-key traps. This closes the reparenting identity cases in `adoption-agency-reparenting.html`, host-identity cases in `custom-element-reparenting.html` and `foster-parenting-into-template-element.html`, and `frameset.children` length in `end-tag-frameset.html`. The one remaining failure is tentative DOMContentLoaded/timer task ordering; the standard leaves ordering between those task sources unspecified, and an existing Goosie lifecycle test exercises the opposite ordering. This directory result does not establish parser conformance; TASK-055 remains open.

**Pinned BroadcastChannel opaque-origin script and worker slice checked 2026-10-29:** The HTML script-data tokenizer now tracks escaped and double-escaped states, keeping an embedded `</script>` inside legacy `<!-- ... -->` script content until the actual end tag. Parser-inserted classic scripts strip line-leading HTML-like comment tokens for Goja while leaving string contents untouched. Blob workers inherit their creator's opaque BroadcastChannel storage identity, and bounded `data:` worker URLs are decoded before script admission so dedicated workers run with opaque origins. Regressions cover script tokenization, classic-script comments, opaque Blob-worker channel exchange, and data-URL worker startup. The focused WPT `webmessaging/broadcastchannel/opaque-origin.html` now executes without script errors and reaches three result callbacks, but still times out before harness completion; its data-URL SharedWorker case remains unimplemented. The earlier full BroadcastChannel profile had 7 pass, 1 fail, and 3 errors; it has not been rerun after these changes. This slice is partial and TASK-055 remains open.

**SharedWorker and executable ServiceWorker BroadcastChannel paths checked 2026-10-29:** `SharedWorker` now reuses workers by top-level site, origin (including private opaque-origin identity), script URL, and name across same-origin Runtime instances on a shared BroadcastHub. It dispatches `connect` as a `MessageEvent` with a transferable `MessagePort`, supports both `onconnect` and `addEventListener("connect", ...)`, and keeps the worker alive while another Runtime still references it. Dedicated Worker objects now accept `addEventListener()`/`removeEventListener()` and dispatch their message/error callbacks. Where a bounded worker script fetcher is configured, ServiceWorker registration executes the fetched script in a worker Runtime and drains its BroadcastChannel work; `unregister()` returns a Promise, as required by WPT cleanup. The pinned opaque-origin, workers, and service-worker WPT files pass individually, and the full `webmessaging/broadcastchannel` directory now reports 10 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, and 1 timed out (90.9%): `/tmp/goosie-task55-broadcastchannel-service-worker-full/results.json`. The remaining cross-partition test's first two iframe-dispatch cases reject with “Unable to trigger … BroadcastChannel message creation”; the full file does not complete, and later worker/service-worker partition cases remain unverified. WPT result diagnostics now retain completed subtest names, statuses, and messages on timeout. The WPT fetch adapter can transport requests only to configured same-port loopback aliases; the engine still applies its origin and CORS checks. ServiceWorker lifecycle, persistence, client control, and full SharedWorker Web IDL/event-loop behavior remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.

**Dynamic classic-script insertion checked 2026-10-29:** Added reflected `HTMLScriptElement.src`, `type`, `async`, `defer`, and `noModule` properties, plus a bounded host fetch hook for connected external classic scripts. Runtime fetches and runs one queued script per later event-loop turn and dispatches `load` after successful execution or `error` after failure; child browsing contexts inherit the hook, and the engine shares its per-document external-script cap and CSP check. `TestDynamicClassicScriptRunsThenFiresLoad`, focused JS/engine/WPT package tests, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The refreshed pinned `webmessaging/broadcastchannel` profile reports 9 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped, and 1 timed out: `/tmp/goosie-task55-broadcastchannel-dynamic-script-child/results.json`. The cross-partition test now makes no harness subtest callbacks before timing out; dispatcher diagnostics show 1 POST, no queued messages, and 1,040 GET polls. Dynamic script insertion is implemented and its basic lifecycle is covered, but this did not unblock the executor popup's returned iframe handshake; the popup/dispatcher path, dynamic load failure diagnostics, and remaining partition cases still need work. This change advances the HTML script-loading slice only; TASK-055 remains open.

**BroadcastChannel cross-partition profile closed 2026-10-29:** The remaining wait was caused by two independent gaps. The WPT harness now gives `.https.` files an HTTPS document origin while fetching bytes through the bounded same-port HTTP alias transport. Opened browsing contexts now form their own BroadcastChannel top-level-site and task-queue roots, while nested frames continue to inherit that root; a focused Runtime test checks cross-site isolation and same-site delivery. Worker Runtime instances inherit their creator's bounded HTTP fetch backend, and dynamic script admission is capped per Runtime instead of being incorrectly shared across unrelated iframe documents in one Session. Focused tests for popup partitioning, worker fetch, secure WPT URL selection, and per-Runtime dynamic script limits pass; `go vet -all ./...`, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, and `git diff --check` pass. The full pinned `webmessaging/broadcastchannel` directory now passes 10/10, including `cross-partition.https.tentative.html`: `/tmp/goosie-task55-broadcastchannel-after-popup-partition/results.json`. This closes that directory's current profile only; TASK-055 and broader HTML Living Standard coverage remain open.

**Radio group checkedness and required form validation checked 2026-10-29:** The browser-pipeline discrepancy came from parser attribute callbacks reading live checkedness while the parser was still processing a start tag: an early `type=radio` callback initialized checkedness false before the later `checked` attribute was appended. The tree builder now synchronizes clean input checkedness from the complete start-tag attributes before insertion. A DOM parser regression reproduces the early callback read; JS regressions cover parser-created and script-created required radio groups, and `reportValidity()` uses the shared tree-scope/name/form-owner group algorithm. The pinned `radio-multiple-selected.html` now passes. The refreshed pinned input-element profile reports 162 passed, 0 failed, 5 skipped (all runnable tests passed): `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-element-final/results.json`. Focused DOM/JS/architecture tests pass; TASK-055 remains open.

**Testdriver-driven clipboard shortcuts and Input Events audit checked 2026-10-29:** The WPT action adapter now focuses a focusable target on primary pointer press and forwards modifier state with key defaults. The engine's testdriver path handles Ctrl/Meta+A/C/X/V using focused control/contenteditable selection and an in-memory clipboard routed through the existing trusted copy, cut, and paste editing algorithms. The unmodified pinned `input-events/input-events-cut-paste.html` now passes 1/1: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-cut-paste/results.json`. The broader 27-file `input-events` directory profile reports 5 passed, 21 failed, and 1 timed out: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile/results.json`. Remaining measured gaps include StaticRange/target ranges, keyboard editing algorithms, spin-button activation, and broader `execCommand` support. The full TASK-055 WPT config now includes the `input-events` root and retains its 100% pass floor; that full profile has not been run. Focused JS/engine/WPT/architecture tests, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass; TASK-055 remains open.

**Selection.setBaseAndExtent direction and boundary validation checked 2026-10-29:** `Selection.setBaseAndExtent()` now validates both nodes, roots, and offsets; normalizes the associated Range into document order while preserving forward/backward anchor and focus direction. `Selection.extend()` now uses the same boundary ordering and retains anchor direction instead of constructing an inverted Range for backward extensions. JS regressions cover forward/backward endpoints, normalized Range bounds, out-of-range offsets, and different roots. With a freshly built browser binary supplied for smoke cases, the refreshed pinned 27-file Input Events profile reports 5 passed, 21 failed, and 1 timed out; `contenteditable-insertfrompaste-type-inputevent-data.html`, previously failing because the Selection method was absent, now passes. Remaining failures still include StaticRange/target-range support, deletion/keyboard algorithms, spin-button activation, and broader `execCommand`. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./internal/engine ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass; TASK-055 remains open.

**StaticRange and InputEvent target ranges checked 2026-10-29:** Added the read-only `StaticRange` boundary properties and constructor, synthetic `InputEventInit.targetRanges` validation, and `getTargetRanges()` snapshots for constructed events. Trusted `beforeinput` on contenteditable now captures the current selection before listener dispatch; input controls and post-edit `input` events expose an empty range list. Target range snapshots use live boundary adjustment for character-data edits and node removals, and `Node.normalize()` now merges adjacent text nodes while adjusting live ranges. New JS and engine regressions cover constructor branding, invalid doctypes/missing dictionary members, fresh returned arrays, and trusted contenteditable ranges. The pinned `input-events-range-exceptions.tentative.html` passes 1/1: `/tmp/goosie-task55-static-ranges-wpt3/results.json`. The refreshed 27-file Input Events profile is 6 passed, 20 failed, and 1 timed out: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-static-range-keydefaults/results.json`. Remaining failures are still concentrated in keyboard editing/testdriver coverage, spin-button behavior, broader `execCommand`, and shadow-DOM event routing. Focused JS/engine tests, `go test ./internal/archtest/`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. The combined package command was interrupted during the WPT package run after the JS and engine test packages passed. TASK-055 remains open.

**WPT editing-key defaults and deletion target ranges checked 2026-10-29:** The WPT action shim now invokes the existing host editing defaults for Backspace, Delete, and Enter after dispatching the corresponding key event. Trusted contenteditable `beforeinput` snapshots now expand a collapsed text selection by one UTF-16 code point for `deleteContentBackward` and `deleteContentForward`, including surrogate pairs. The focused target-range run passes `input-events-get-target-ranges-during-and-after-dispatch.tentative.html` (3/3 subtests); the large backspace matrix still fails 127/173 subtests, mainly for block boundaries and cross-node deletion. **Text-control selection, clipboard, and paste Input Events checked 2026-10-29:** `setSelectionRange(start)` now collapses at `start` when the end argument is omitted. Script-visible UTF-16 selections are synchronized into host edits and clipboard selection handling without letting stale runtime selection overwrite host-driven selection changes; `Selection.toString()` returns the selected text-control substring while that control is active. Regressions cover `getSelection().toString()`, script-selected copy/paste, and paste replacement. The pinned cut/paste WPT and all three textarea paste-data WPTs pass (4/4): `/tmp/goosie-task55-textarea-selection-wpt/results.json`. **Number-input spin Input Events checked 2026-10-29:** The WPT testdriver adapter now maps WebDriver ArrowUp/ArrowDown keys, keeps `send_keys` constructable for legacy tests using `new`, emits cancellable `beforeinput` before number stepping, and emits `input` then `change` only when the edit proceeds. Pointer actions in the number control's spin-button area use the same step sequence. A connected-document check suppresses follow-up events when a listener removes the input's iframe. All three Arrow-key WPTs pass; five of six focused Arrow-key and spin-button WPTs pass, with the iframe spin-button pointer case still failing because the adapter does not route the action into the iframe context. **Contenteditable editing keys and nested-context spin actions checked 2026-10-29:** The testdriver adapter forwards `Actions.setContext()` into the selected document, handles the zero-size child-frame control geometry used by the pinned spin-button fixture, emits `keypress` for printable `send_keys` input, and routes editable keyboard defaults through the engine. Contenteditable focus now initializes a caret at the first text node when no in-host selection exists; Enter edits carry `insertParagraph`/`insertLineBreak`, and Ctrl/Alt+Backspace/Delete select word-deletion input types for collapsed selections. Successful contenteditable operations rely on the `execCommand` input event path, with a fallback input event for no-op deletion attempts required by the WPT. Added a Go regression for the focused caret position and insertion. The three number spin-button WPTs pass 3/3, and `input-events-delete-selection.html` passes 6/6; `input-events-typing.html` now passes 9/13 testharness cases, with history undo/redo, selected-range keyboard event ordering, and combined typing data/order still failing. The refreshed pinned 27-file Input Events profile reports 16 passed, 10 failed, and 1 timed out: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile-current/results.json`.
