Focused JS/engine packages, architecture checks, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Contenteditable Backspace ranges and block joining checked 2026-10-29:** Expanded collapsed-selection deletion target ranges across adjacent inline nodes and block boundaries, including UTF-16 character boundaries, atomic inline elements, and collapsed whitespace at block joins. Backspace at a block start now joins adjacent editable blocks, removes a trailing placeholder `<br>`, trims collapsed boundary whitespace, preserves preformatted whitespace, and emits `input` after a real edit. Removed the no-op deletion input fallback after the pinned target-range suite showed that a no-op Backspace must not emit `input`; this supersedes the previous ledger claim that the fallback was required. Added an engine regression that checks the joined markup, `beforeinput` target range, and post-edit empty `input` ranges. The pinned `input-events-get-target-ranges-backspace.tentative.html` now passes 84/173 subtests (89 remain failing): `/tmp/goosie-task55-backspace-ranges-final/results.json`. The full pinned Input Events profile is 16 passed, 10 failed, 1 timed out; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-profile-latest/results.json`. `input-events-typing.html` currently passes 5/13 cases because its no-op deletion expectation conflicts with the no-op behavior asserted by the target-range WPT. Remaining failures include cross-block whitespace variants, forward deletion and list joins, shadow-DOM target ranges, `execCommand`, and typing history/order. JS/engine package tests, architecture checks, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Element-boundary selection target ranges checked 2026-10-29:** Trusted `beforeinput` target ranges now shrink a selection that covers exactly one Text child through its parent element offsets to that Text node's full UTF-16 range. Public Selection and Range boundaries are unchanged, and selections of atomic/non-text children keep their element boundary range. Added an engine regression for selecting `<p>` offsets 0–1 around `abc`. The pinned non-collapsed selection WPT, run with its declared `?Backspace`, `?Delete`, and `?TypingA` variants, now passes three additional subtests per variant (from 42 to 39 failing subtests for Backspace and Delete, and 39 to 36 for TypingA); paragraph joining, cross-node whitespace, and several edit-result cases still fail. The focused report is `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-noncollapsed-final/results.json`. The task-wide WPT profile had also been invoking four parameterized list/definition-list files without their required query variants, producing false “Unhandled variant” failures; `testdata/wpt-task55-full-config.json` now supplies those variants so future task-wide reports exercise the actual tests. `go test ./internal/js ./test/engine -run 'TestContentEditable(BeforeInput|Backspace)' -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, and `git diff --check` pass. This increment closes only the selected-text target-range case; TASK-055 remains open.

**Empty-block deletion placeholder checked 2026-10-29:** Contenteditable `execCommand("delete")` now inserts a `<br>` into an emptied editing block after deleting its contents, keeping the block caret-positionable. Extended the element-boundary selection regression to assert `<p><br></p>` after deleting all paragraph text. The corrected, variant-driven non-collapsed-selection WPT now has 36/70 failures for each of Backspace, Delete, and TypingA; compared with the preceding run, Backspace and Delete each gain three passing delete-result subtests. Cross-block joining, whitespace normalization, and several other selection outcomes remain failing. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-noncollapsed-placeholder/results.json`. Focused engine/JS tests, WPT config selection, architecture checks, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-block selection editing checked 2026-10-29:** Contenteditable deletion now merges the remaining content of adjacent same-tag sibling blocks after deleting a non-collapsed selection. It removes boundary-collapsed whitespace for normal white-space handling, preserves whitespace for preformatted blocks/styles, carries the remaining end-block children into the start block, removes the emptied block, and retains mutation, radio-group, and custom-element connection notifications. Text insertion across a selection uses the same join. Trusted target ranges now include boundary spaces that collapse at block joins and resolve a selected atomic inline element between text nodes to those adjacent text boundaries; `<br>` selections retain their element offsets. Added a regression for deleting across paragraph text while preserving the prefix and suffix. The pinned non-collapsed selection WPT now passes 53/70 subtests for each `?Backspace`, `?Delete`, and `?TypingA` variant (down from 36 failures to 17 per variant); report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-noncollapsed-target-normalize3/results.json`. Remaining failures cover nested block-to-inline joins, lists, tables, and other structural editing cases. Focused engine tests pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Definition-list join and corrected list-item WPT measurements checked 2026-10-29:** Same-tag definition-list items now merge across adjacent `<dl>` containers when a non-collapsed edit selection crosses the two items; a Go engine regression checks the resulting markup. An earlier focused `joining-dl-elements` run passed 336/440 assertions in each Backspace and Delete variant (104 failures each); the latest measurement is recorded above. The previous focused run passed 238/440 per variant. The same earlier focused run measures `deleting-in-list-items` at 141/316 Backspace and 135/316 Delete subtests per `ul`/`ol` variant, and `joining-dl-element-and-another-list` at 52/144 Backspace and 52/152 Delete subtests. These are now actual variant-driven engine measurements rather than the earlier “Unhandled variant” results. Cross-type `dt`/`dd` joins, definition-list joins with an adjacent `ul`/`ol`, list-item selection deletion, and many target-range edge cases remain open. Focused paragraph and definition-list engine regressions pass; TASK-055 remains open.

**Nested list-item paragraph Backspace checked 2026-10-29:** The contenteditable Backspace default now searches ancestor levels for the previous editable block, so a caret at the start of a paragraph nested inside an `<li>` can join into the preceding list item. After moving the text, it removes the emptied paragraph and list-item wrappers, then positions the caret in the surviving text and dispatches one `input` event. Added a Go engine regression for the two-item unordered list case. The variant-driven `deleting-in-list-items` WPT now passes 144/316 subtests for Backspace and 135/316 for Delete in each `ul` and `ol` variant; Backspace improved by two subtests per list type over the preceding source run. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-ancestor-results/results.json`. Numerous list target-range, empty-item, forward-delete, and selection cases remain failing. TASK-055 remains open.

**Sole empty list-item deletion checked 2026-10-29:** When the caret is in the placeholder `<br>` of a sole empty list item, trusted deletion target ranges now select the containing list at its parent boundary. The editing default removes that list; when it is a nested list, the emptied parent `<li>` receives a `<br>` placeholder. Added an engine regression checking the outer-list target range, removal, and one `input` event. The refreshed variant-driven list-item WPT passes 165/316 Backspace subtests and 139/316 Delete subtests per `ul`/`ol` variant, improving by 21 and 4 cases respectively over the preceding run. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-empty-list-item-results/results.json`. Other list-boundary target ranges, deletion across editable/noneditable items, and forward-delete cases remain open; TASK-055 remains open.

**Selected list-item ranges and list deletion checked 2026-10-29:** Trusted `beforeinput` ranges resolve a selected last text-only `<li>` to its text node, trim selected lists at noneditable first/last items, and resolve a selected nested list containing one editable text node to that text. For editing selections crossing from a list with nested lists into a separate list, target ranges now end at the first editable text node in the right item. Forward Delete at the end of a text-only list item merges the next editable text-only item; forward Delete in a `<p>` list item and Backspace into a preceding `<p>` list item now merge the text into that paragraph; forward Delete from a plain item into a following item containing one text-only `<p>` flattens and joins the paragraph text. Backspace/Delete joins across a `<br>` split the first line into the adjacent item and retain the following line separately. Successful joins emit one `input` event. Deleting selected trailing list items preserves the item with a `<br>`; deleting a trailing nested list in a list inserts a placeholder `<li>`. Deleting a selected list containing noneditable items preserves the list and inserts an editable `<li><br></li>` placeholder; deleting a sole empty list now leaves a `<br>` in its empty editable owner. Engine regressions cover these joins, the split-line behavior, and cross-list target ranges. The focused variant-driven `deleting-in-list-items` WPT now passes 274/316 Backspace and 274/316 Delete subtests per `ul`/`ol` variant; the preceding run passed 250 for both input types. Forward Delete target ranges now span from the current text end to the next list item text start when one or more trailing `<br>` elements separate them. The empty-list-item target-range check now reads text data from the DOM text-data field, so nonempty list items are not misclassified as empty placeholders. During range deletion, an emptied nested list is removed when the selected text starts outside it and ends inside it; nested lists at the selection start remain in place. A fully selected empty trailing `<li>` is removed when it follows nested-list content, preserving the nested list item placeholder. When a selected item directly precedes a nested-list sibling, the empty item is removed only if the selection ends at the beginning of the nested item. Selection joins between adjacent list roots now merge the right item into the left list, including when the left item is the last item in a nested-list path; when the right item has a line break and following content, only its first line moves and the remaining line stays in the right list. Removing the sole empty item in a nested list directly under an outer `<ul>` or `<ol>` now inserts an outer `<li><br></li>` at the removed list position, including when later outer items remain. Selecting a nested list element with one editable text node now deletes that text and retains the nested list with its `<li><br></li>` placeholder; deleting an empty final sublist item still takes the outdent path. Focused engine regressions and the WPT cover these behaviors. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-boundary-results/results.json`. Selections spanning multiple editable list items, mixed editable/noneditable content, nested lists with multiple text nodes, and forward joining of complex list-item content remain open. Focused JS/engine tests, architecture checks, the task-55 WPT config test, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Same-root nested-list selection joins checked 2026-10-29:** When a selected list item is at the end of a nested list that is itself the immediately preceding sibling of the target item, or when the containing list item is the immediately preceding sibling, deletion now joins the target item's remaining content into the nested item. The candidate anchor must share the target list parent, have no meaningful siblings between it and the target, and be the last meaningful content along the path from the starting item. The pinned `deleting-in-list-items` WPT improves from 274 to 280 passing subtests per variant (Backspace/UL, Backspace/OL, Delete/UL, Delete/OL), reducing failures from 42 to 36 in each variant; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-boundary-results/results.json`. Existing cross-list join and retained-following-line engine regressions pass. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Other list selection, mixed editability, nested content, and full HTML Living Standard gaps remain; TASK-055 remains open.

**Nested list retained across selected outer items checked 2026-10-29:** When a selection deletes the text of one outer `<li>` and reaches the text of a nested list in the following `<li>`, the nested list is moved under the surviving outer item and retains an editable `<li><br></li>` placeholder. Empty nested list items transferred by this join are preserved during selection cleanup. `TestContentEditableSelectionJoinPreservesNestedListPlaceholder` checks the final DOM and one `input` event. The four pinned `deleting-in-list-items` variants now pass 282/316 subtests each (34 fail each), up from 280; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-nested-preserve.log` and `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-boundary-results/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, the TASK-055 WPT config test, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. Other editing-list failures and TASK-055 acceptance criteria remain open.

**Nested right-list joins and empty leading roots checked 2026-10-29:** Cross-root selection joins now recognize a right item reached through its first nested-list path, move its remaining content into the left item, and prune only empty list/item wrappers after the move. When the entire leading list item is deleted and the target is inside a nested right list, the right list root and its content remain in place. Engine regressions cover both flattened joins and preservation of the nested right root. The four pinned `deleting-in-list-items` variants now pass 314/316 subtests each, up from 282; report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-right-root2.log` and `/tmp/goosie-task55-input-events-list-boundary-results/results.json`. Two subtests per variant still fail in the case selecting across two items in the first list before a following list; the WPT expects `ist-item3` plus a new `ist-item4`, though the input contains only `list-item3`. A direct Playwright selection and keypress reproduction in Chrome 156.0.8078.48 produces the same final markup as Goosie for all eight Backspace/Delete and UL/OL root combinations; evidence: `/tmp/goosie-task55-chrome156-list-selection-reference.txt`. This is tracked as a WPT expected-result/reference mismatch and remains a visible WPT failure pending upstream clarification; Goosie is not changed to produce the extra item. Scoped JS/engine/architecture tests, the TASK-055 WPT config test, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**TreeWalker and NodeIterator core traversal checked 2026-10-29:** TreeWalker now implements `FILTER_ACCEPT`, `FILTER_SKIP`, and `FILTER_REJECT` distinctly: skipped nodes expose descendants, rejected subtrees are pruned, and whatToShow exclusions skip the node without invoking the callback. Filter objects call `acceptNode` with the proper receiver; invalid callbacks and thrown JS values propagate, and recursive navigation raises `InvalidStateError`. TreeWalker properties are read-only except for a validated `currentNode`; traversal exhaustion leaves its position unchanged. NodeIterator now exposes read-only `root`, `whatToShow`, `filter`, `referenceNode`, and `pointerBeforeReferenceNode`, returns the root on its first accepted `nextNode()`, preserves pointer state when filtering yields no result, handles previous traversal within the iterator root, and rejects recursive filter calls. The `NodeFilter.SHOW_PROCESSING_INSTRUCTION` node-type mapping was added. Focused Go traversal tests pass. Five pinned WPT files pass 5/5 (`NodeFilter-constants.html`, `NodeIterator.html` with 766 assertions, `TreeWalker-acceptNode-filter.html`, `TreeWalker-traversal-reject.html`, and `TreeWalker-traversal-skip.html`): `/tmp/goosie-task55-traversal-wpt/results.json`. The selected core refresh passes 4/5 files; `TreeWalker.html` passes 757/761 assertions, with one reentrant `nextSibling()` expectation and three cases where the upstream helper compares boolean callback results directly instead of applying NodeFilter's unsigned-short conversion: `/tmp/goosie-task55-treewalker-html-final/results.json`. The complete pinned `dom/traversal` directory run reports 9 passed, 8 failed, 2 helper skips, and 0 errors across 19 candidates: `/tmp/goosie-task55-traversal-all/results.json`. Remaining measured gaps include NodeIterator reference retargeting after mutations/removal, iframe/cross-realm filters and returned Node identity, and broader TreeWalker edge cases. This advances the traversal subset only; TASK-055 remains open.

**NodeIterator pre-remove retargeting checked 2026-10-29:** Node removal now runs the iterator pre-remove retargeting step before `Node.removeChild()`, `Element.remove()`, and `ShadowRoot.removeChild()` detach referenced subtrees. During filter evaluation, callbacks still observe the last accepted `referenceNode` and pointer state, while a separate in-flight position lets removal retarget the iterator; if the filtered candidate itself is detached, traversal returns that candidate for the current call but continues from the retargeted reference. The pinned `NodeIterator.html`, `NodeIterator-removal.html`, and `NodeIterator-removal-during-filtering.html` pass 3/3: `/tmp/goosie-task55-nodeiterator-removal/results.json`. The refreshed complete pinned `dom/traversal` run improves from 9/19 to 13 passed, 4 failed, 2 helper skips, and 0 errors (76.5% of runnable files): `/tmp/goosie-task55-traversal-after-retarget/results.json`. The remaining four failures are cross-realm filter and returned-Node identity support plus four assertions in `TreeWalker.html` (one recursive `nextSibling()` case and three legacy helper expectations for boolean filter results). Removal paths internal to other DOM algorithms still need wider coverage. TASK-055 remains open.

**Central DOM pre-remove observer for NodeIterator checked 2026-10-29:** The pre-remove notification now originates in `dom.Node.RemoveChild()` and `dom.ShadowRoot.RemoveChild()`, covering mutations that detach children through APIs and internal DOM algorithms, including `textContent`, `innerHTML`, and replacement operations. Each Runtime registers once per Document used by one of its iterators, including secondary XML/foreign documents, and unregisters its callbacks when closed. `TestNodeIteratorRetargetsForTextContentAndInnerHTML` confirms references retarget before both setters replace a subtree. The three pinned NodeIterator creation/traversal/removal WPTs pass 3/3: `/tmp/goosie-task55-nodeiterator-central-remove-final/results.json`. The refreshed complete pinned `dom/traversal` result remains 13 passed, 4 failed, 2 helper skips, and 0 errors (76.5% of runnable files): `/tmp/goosie-task55-traversal-central-final/results.json`; all four remaining failures are TreeWalker cross-realm support or the four known `TreeWalker.html` assertions. Focused DOM/JS/architecture tests, the Task 55 WPT config test, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**TreeWalker iframe realm and detached browsing context checks checked 2026-10-29:** Lazy iframe realm initialization now aligns an escaped `DocumentElement` wrapper from its initial per-frame prototype to the child realm, so TreeWalker results satisfy `instanceof iframe.contentWindow.Node`. `Window` named iframe properties are installed for parser-provided `name` attributes and when the engine binds a document to a runtime. A NodeIterator whose filter belongs to a removed iframe now stops before calling that filter, and `contentWindow` reports `null` after an accessed iframe browsing context is removed. Bridged TreeWalker filters now execute in their owning iframe Runtime, preserving cross-realm `TypeError` identity and revoked Proxy exceptions. Added Go regressions for TreeWalker Node identity and bound-document iframe names. The three selected pinned traversal WPTs (`TreeWalker-realm.html`, `TreeWalker-acceptNode-filter-cross-realm-null-browsing-context.html`, and `TreeWalker-acceptNode-filter-cross-realm.html`) pass 3/3: `/tmp/goosie-task55-traversal-cross-realm-fix/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Traversal performance and remaining TreeWalker assertions checked 2026-10-29:** Cross-realm filter detection now avoids scanning child runtimes for ordinary same-realm callback functions. This removes the timeout previously seen halfway through `NodeIterator.html`; the pinned file passes 1/1 with all 766 assertions: `/tmp/goosie-task55-nodeiterator-perf/results.json`. The other 16 runnable `dom/traversal` files pass 16/16 (two support files are correctly skipped): `/tmp/goosie-task55-traversal-no-legacy-html-fast/results.json`. `TreeWalker.html` remains 4/761 assertions short: three cases use a boolean-returning helper but compare the boolean directly to numeric `FILTER_ACCEPT`; the fourth calls `nextSibling()` while `currentNode` is the last child and there is no sibling to filter. The DOM Standard checks the active flag when filtering a candidate, so no candidate means no `InvalidStateError` (see [Traversal](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#traversal)); a new Go regression verifies all seven navigation methods reject recursive filter calls when they reach a candidate. The focused `TreeWalker.html` report is `/tmp/goosie-task55-treewalker-html-current/results.json`. `go test ./internal/js ./test/js ./test/engine -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest/ -count=1`, `go vet -all ./...`, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**Rendered text properties and SVG structure checked 2026-10-29:** `innerText`/`outerText` now handle inherited and locale-aware text transforms, first-line/first-letter transforms, CSS white-space, hidden/non-rendered content, `<p>` paragraph spacing, table rows/cells/captions and `inline-table`, empty select rows, closed `<details>`, root `<br>`, positioned inline boxes, and SVG elements that do not render text. The HTML parser now keeps SVG foreign-content elements such as `<stop>` open until an explicit self-closing token. Added Go regressions for the new behaviors. The pinned `html/dom/elements/the-innertext-and-outertext-properties/getter.html` passes 276/276 assertions: `/tmp/goosie-task55-innertext-pseudo-final2/results.json`. Focused `go test ./internal/js ./internal/engine ./test/js ./test/engine -count=1`, `go test ./internal/archtest -count=1`, scoped `go vet -all`, and `git diff --check` pass. The wider `go test ./...` run exposed two unrelated suite-wide blockers: `test/qc` reports a stale `traceability.golden.json` and pending QC sign-off entries, and `test/wpt` timed out in `TestWPTSuite` after 10 minutes while rendering its broad corpus. TASK-055 remains open.

**Cross-realm XML innerHTML exception propagation checked 2026-10-29:** Imported iframe accessor calls now rethrow translated child-realm exceptions instead of reporting them to the child window and returning silently. A regression covers malformed XML `innerHTML` assignment through a parent-realm wrapper. The pinned `domparsing/innerhtml-05.xhtml` passes 1/1; the regression, existing XHTML `innerHTML` test, architecture checks, and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.

**DOM parsing WPT refresh checked 2026-10-29:** A fresh build against the current tree passes all 42 runnable non-tentative files in the pinned `domparsing` directory (1 support file skipped); the former normative XML `innerHTML-05.xhtml` failure is now green. The full directory includes tentative streaming/positional HTML APIs with 25 failing files and 9 harness errors, so the aggregate is not a conformance pass. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-domparsing-current-results/results.json`. Crash-smoke fixtures were excluded from this comparison because the configured headless binary does not accept the browser runner's `-backend` flag. TASK-055 remains open.

**Trusted MessagePort MessageEvents checked 2026-10-29:** Queued MessagePort deliveries now create branded `MessageEvent` objects with trusted dispatch state, receiver target, origin/source/ports fields, and target-phase lifecycle state instead of plain objects. The dispatcher now honors `stopImmediatePropagation()` across registered listeners and `onmessage`. Added a Go regression for event branding, trust, payload fields, target/currentTarget, and post-dispatch state. The focused `MessageEvent.any.js`, `MessageEvent_properties.htm`, and `MessageEvent-trusted.any.js` WPTs pass 3/3: `/tmp/goosie-task55-messageevent-current/results.json`; focused MessagePort tests pass. Remaining MessagePort structured-clone, transfer, and EventTarget semantics need broader coverage; TASK-055 remains open.

**ParentNode.replaceChildren checked 2026-10-29:** `replaceChildren()` is implemented for Element, Document, and DocumentFragment with argument conversion, replace-all behavior, and source-parent removal records when moving nodes. Added Go regressions for replacement and fragment behavior. The pinned `dom/nodes/ParentNode-replaceChildren.html` passes 31/31 cases; focused JS tests and `git diff --check` pass. TASK-055 remains open.
**OffscreenCanvas and ImageBitmap transfer-list support checked 2026-10-29:** Added a bounded `OffscreenCanvas` constructor with width/height reflection, a 2D context using the existing canvas operation recorder, OffscreenCanvas input to `createImageBitmap()`, and structured cloning to a fresh object with dimensions/context pixel and operation metadata copied. Same-window and MessagePort transfer-list handling now validates OffscreenCanvas/ImageBitmap entries and detaches the source only after payload cloning succeeds. Go regressions cover ordinary clone identity/dimensions/context and window transfer source detachment. The focused `html/infrastructure/safe-passing-of-structured-data/messagechannel.any.js` WPT reaches 137/152 passing subtests (one optional skip), up from 135; both OffscreenCanvas and ImageBitmap transfer cases now pass. Report: `/tmp/goosie-task55-offscreen-focused/results.json`. Focused JS and architecture tests pass. Broader OffscreenCanvas rendering, transfer between independent runtimes, and full transfer atomicity across every transferable type remain incomplete; TASK-055 remains open.
